# TaroBird 1.6.0  A feature release: two more engines, conversation fork and rewind, remote screen viewing from the phone, quota and context indicators, and a workspace rail.  ## Downloads  | Platform | Download | | --- | --- | | macOS - Apple silicon | [TaroBird 1.6.0 DMG](https://github.com/cloveric/tarobird/releases/download/v1.6.0/TaroBird_1.6.0_aarch64.dmg) | | Windows - x64 | [TaroBird 1.6.0 installer](https://github.com/cloveric/tarobird/releases/download/v1.6.0/TaroBird_1.6.0_x64-setup.exe) | | Android - GitHub distribution | [TaroBird 1.6.0 APK](https://github.com/cloveric/tarobird/releases/download/v1.6.0/TaroBird_1.6.0_android.apk) | | iOS App Store (stable) | [TaroBird on the App Store](https://apps.apple.com/app/tarobird/id6828071641) | | iOS TestFlight public beta | [TestFlight invitation](https://testflight.apple.com/join/ByFWEfFW) | | Standalone Host / CLI | [tarobird-1.6.0.tgz](https://github.com/cloveric/tarobird/releases/download/v1.6.0/tarobird-1.6.0.tgz) |  Store availability follows its own review and rollout. The iOS 1.6.0 build has been uploaded to App Store Connect and submitted for App Store review and for TestFlight beta review; both were waiting for review at publication. The matching Google Play bundle has been uploaded to the closed test track and sent for review. This GitHub release does not claim that 1.6.0 is already available in either store. The App Store version verified while preparing this release is 1.5.1. Google Play is in closed testing.  ## Changes  - New engines: Pi, and Antigravity through its ACP runtime. - Conversations can be forked and rewound natively. - Remote screen viewing on phones and on Windows, sharing one relay line. - Model quota and context indicators. - A workspace rail beside the conversation; a turn's changed files are listed with file names and added/removed line counts. - Bot-to-Bot messages are delivered and steered between computers. - A task panel for Kimi. - Share inbox: one unreadable shared item no longer hides the others. - Claude quota: a usage answer that arrives after the App stopped waiting is kept, so the indicator no longer stays empty when the CLI answers slowly. - Widgets stay self-contained with no network access, as in 1.5.1.  ## Compatibility  App and standalone Host / CLI use 1.6.0 from the same source revision. Android APK and Play AAB use version code 2026110416; the iOS build number is 1. Platform updater compatibility entries remain available for installed clients.  The Host database schema moves from 25 to 26. A Host managed by the desktop App migrates when the new App starts it; `tarobot start` does the same for a CLI Host, and a Host installed as a background service is upgraded with `tarobot update stage` and `tarobot update apply`. Returning to 1.5.1 after the migration was not tested.  Electron stays at 45.3.0; the upgrade to the current 45.x line follows as its own release.  This version was published directly as stable at the owner's request, without a release-candidate stage. Validation was local and GitHub Actions did not run. The two changes made after the version freeze (widgets, Claude quota) were checked with their own tests only; one browser test of widget layout failed in that run and its cause was not established. Windows native install and upgrade acceptance, physical-phone acceptance of these packages and live FCM/APNs delivery were not established.  Known issue, older than this release: clearing a long history on a phone can stop part-way with "清理未完成"; "继续清理" resumes it and no record is lost.  On Android, keep Android System WebView updated.  Source: `29e277d6bd594ad75dfac842ca240b80200cb907`. 
```

### 2026-11-24T08:53:48Z [**vivaui/viva**](https://github.com/vivaui/viva)

```
v0.6.8 是 v0.6.7 之后的版本：`ui.Run` 改为接收 `PageConfig`，与 `OpenWindow` 一致；加入触摸输入、双向文本光标与 CodeEditor 装饰、只读选择文本 `textview`、关系图、响应式布局与延时投递，并有大量 Linux、Windows 与 macOS 的输入法、剪贴板和性能修复。macOS 是参考平台。  v0.6.8 comes after v0.6.7: `ui.Run` takes a `PageConfig` as `OpenWindow` does, and the release adds touch input, bidirectional text carets and CodeEditor decorations, a read-only selectable `textview`, a link graph, responsive layout and delayed posting, with many input-method, clipboard and performance fixes on Linux, Windows and macOS. macOS is the reference platform.  ## 拿到它 · Getting it  `go get github.com/vivaui/viva@v0.6.8`，需要 Go 1.26 或更新；系统依赖与 v0.6.7 相同。每个 Release 附带 `viva` 命令的预编译压缩包、`SHA256SUMS`，以及 Homebrew 与 Scoop 清单（`brew install vivaui/tap/viva`，或 `scoop bucket add vivaui https://github.com/vivaui/homebrew-tap` 后 `scoop install viva`）。  `go get github.com/vivaui/viva@v0.6.8` needs Go 1.26 or newer; the system requirements are those of v0.6.7. Every Release carries prebuilt archives of the `viva` command, `SHA256SUMS`, and Homebrew and Scoop manifests (`brew install vivaui/tap/viva`, or `scoop bucket add vivaui https://github.com/vivaui/homebrew-tap` then `scoop install viva`).  ## 有什么 · What is in it  - `ui.Run(config, ui.PageConfig{View: m.page})`：一个窗口的程序可以在同一个 `PageConfig` 里设置主题和样式；`ui` 重新导出了自定义控件与绘制要用的类型（`Identity`、事件与画布类型、字重、阴影层级、文本方向与对齐、`ButtonVariant`、`TableColumn`、各控件的外观）。 - `Box.Variant` 可以在运行时选择按钮变体；`ui.Table` 现在自己完整：页面把移动选中行的按键送给它，点击选中一行，`TableState.Changed` 报告选择变化，只给 `Headers` 时各列均分宽度。 - `ui.AlertDialog` 与 `Modal` 一样开在 `*bool` 上，并报告确认动作；`ui.MenuAt` 相对应用自绘表面的矩形打开菜单；没有指定初始焦点的 `Dialog` 把键盘交给正文的第一个控件；`FormField` 的标题像 HTML label 一样标注它的第一个控件。 - `Box.From` 与 `Container`、`Breakpoint` 让布局随窗口或容器宽度变化，`Box.Hide`、`Box.Show` 隐藏和重新显示元素，`Box.Maxs`、`Box.Italic`、`Box.Strike`、`Box.OverflowWrap` 补全文本与尺寸属性，长单词在没有空间时可以折断。 - `ui/textview` 是只读、可选择、可复制的文本；`ui.TextFieldID` 也能编辑模型里的字符串；输入、分割条、提示和树控件补全了几处缺口。 - `components` 新增力导向的链接图（`PaintGraph`、`GraphState`、`GraphSim`），CodeEditor 增加行号栏、字节范围装饰与下划线，保持双向文本的成形光标位置，只重排被编辑触及的行；下拉菜单可以设置行高与最大宽度；Select 与 Combobox 的列表按弹出规则定位。 - `app.Invalidator.Post` 让 goroutine 把结果交回窗口，`Application.PostAfter` 在事件循环上延时执行；`Config.Maximized` 以最大化打开窗口，`Config.OnDeactivate` 报告窗口失去激活，`Config.CaptureMenuKeys` 让应用先于菜单取键。 - `app.WindowNotification` 是可点击的通知弹窗；`app.SetClipboardImage` 把图片放上剪贴板；`WebViewConfig` 增加 `Partition`、`UserAgentSuffix` 和 `OnProcessFailed`，`WebView.IsCrashed` 与 `ProcessFailure` 报告页面进程的死亡。 - 触摸：手指读作点击、拖动、甩动与长按，滚动可以越过末端并弹回，小于 44 px 的控件也能被手指点中，`Config.OnPinch` 报告双指缩放，轻点放置光标、按住选词、按住显示提示；Windows 按触摸指针读手指，Linux X11 按 X Input 触摸事件读手指。 - `Canvas.TitlebarInsets` 与 `ui.C.TitlebarInsets` 给出本窗口的标题栏边距：自定义标题栏时是平台的窗口按钮，全屏或系统标题栏下为零。 - `Theme.WithTextScale` 按偏好缩放字号与行高，`Theme.TooltipDelay` 设置提示延迟，`gfx.TextHit` 返回点击所在的簇边缘，完全透明的文本不再绘制。 - 辅助技术：Linux 上新增的无障碍控件会发出焦点通知；X11 上剪贴板变化会通知、按 TARGETS 读取并刷新过期的所有权，Fcitx 4 取消预编辑时保留选中文本，GTK 在窗口失焦时丢弃未选中的预编辑，IBus 先从 `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` 找守护进程地址。 - Windows 上菜单捕获按键时保持输入法组合，最小化的窗口不再报告为窗口位置。 - X11 窗口不用等输入就能绘制，重新曝光后会重绘；所有窗口共用一个 Vulkan 设备，标签图像从块里子分配，上传经保留的缓冲区，不再让 GPU 空等。 - 编辑器的文本以 UTF-8 叶子保存，点击与选词不再扫描整行，Textarea 只排版视口内的行且只重排被编辑的段落，CodeEditor 只排版被编辑触及的行；`textarea` 跟随程序修改。 - 通知窗口会保持在屏幕内并可以移动，`Select` 在对话框、抽屉或弹出层里不再替外层结束出现动画，面板的滚轮事件不再传给下面的页面，`Markdown` 解码行内字符引用，PAC 求值在取消时被唤醒而不是轮询。 - 打包 `.dmg` 时等待磁盘映像服务放手、在卷已卸载或仍挂载时都能分离，映像构建过程中不会暴露可安装的应用。  - `ui.Run(config, ui.PageConfig{View: m.page})`: a one-window program sets its theme and styles in the same `PageConfig`; `ui` re-exports the types a custom control and a draw take (`Identity`, the event and canvas types, font weights, shadow levels, text direction and alignment, `ButtonVariant`, `TableColumn`, and the control looks). - `Box.Variant` chooses a button's variant at run time; `ui.Table` is whole as declared: the page routes it the keys that move its selection, a click selects a row, `TableState.Changed` reports the change, and `Headers` without `Columns` share the width. - `ui.AlertDialog` opens on a `*bool` as `Modal` does and reports the confirming action; `ui.MenuAt` opens a menu against a rectangle of the application's own surface; a `Dialog` that names no initial focus gives the keyboard to the first control of its body; a `FormField` title labels its first control as an HTML label does. - `Box.From` with `Container` and `Breakpoint` lays out by the window's or a container's width, `Box.Hide` and `Box.Show` hide and show an element, `Box.Maxs`, `Box.Italic`, `Box.Strike` and `Box.OverflowWrap` complete the text and size attributes, and a long word breaks when the box has no room. - `ui/textview` is read-only text that selects and copies; `ui.TextFieldID` also edits a model string; fields, the splitter, tooltips and trees close several gaps. - `components` adds a force-directed link graph (`PaintGraph`, `GraphState`, `GraphSim`), and CodeEditor gains a gutter, byte-range decorations and underlines, keeps shaped bidirectional caret placement, and lays out only the lines an edit touched; dropdown menus take a row height and a maximum width; Select and Combobox lists are placed by the popup rule. - `app.Invalidator.Post` hands a goroutine's result to a window and `Application.PostAfter` runs delayed work on the event loop; `Config.Maximized` opens a window maximized, `Config.OnDeactivate` reports a window losing the activation, and `Config.CaptureMenuKeys` lets an application take keys before the menu. - `app.WindowNotification` is a clickable notification popup; `app.SetClipboardImage` puts an image on the clipboard; `WebViewConfig` adds `Partition`, `UserAgentSuffix` and `OnProcessFailed`, and `WebView.IsCrashed` and `ProcessFailure` report the death of a page's process. - Touch: fingers read as taps, pans, flings and long presses, scrolling carries past its end and springs back, a fingertip reaches controls smaller than 44 px, `Config.OnPinch` reports two-finger pinches, a tap places the caret, a held finger selects a word or opens a tooltip; Windows reads a finger as a touch pointer and Linux X11 reads X Input touch events. - `Canvas.TitlebarInsets` and `ui.C.TitlebarInsets` give the titlebar insets of the window being painted: the platform's buttons for a custom titlebar, none in full screen or under a system titlebar. - `Theme.WithTextScale` scales text size and line height by a preference, `Theme.TooltipDelay` sets the tooltip delay, `gfx.TextHit` returns the cluster edge a click hits, and fully transparent text draws nothing. - Platform input: newly added accessibility controls announce focus on Linux; on X11 clipboard changes notify and the clipboard is read by TARGETS with stale ownership refreshed, Fcitx 4 keeps selected text when its preedit is cancelled, GTK drops an unselected preedit on focus loss, and IBus finds the daemon address from `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` first. - On Windows IME composition survives a menu that captures keys, and a minimized frame is no longer reported as the window position. - X11 windows paint without waiting for input and after a re-expose; every window shares one Vulkan device, label image memory is sub-allocated from blocks, and uploads go through kept buffers without idling the GPU. - The editor keeps its text as UTF-8 leaves, a click or word selection no longer scans the line, Textarea lays out only the rows in view and rewraps only the paragraphs an edit changed, CodeEditor lays out only the lines an edit touched, and a textarea follows program edits. - The notification window stays on screen and can move, a `Select` inside a dialog, drawer or popover no longer closes the enclosing presence in its place, panel wheel events stay off the page underneath, `Markdown` decodes inline character references, and PAC evaluation wakes on cancel instead of polling. - Packaging a `.dmg` waits until the disk image service lets go, detaches whether the volume is already unmounted or still attached, and keeps an image build from exposing an installable app.  ## 注意 · Caveats  - `ui.Run(config, m.page)` 现在是 `ui.Run(config, ui.PageConfig{View: m.page})`。按 `main` 上 v0.6.8 之前的指南写出的程序在 v0.6.7 上无法编译，原因就是这一处；升到 v0.6.8 后指南与代码一致。 - `app.TitlebarInsets()` 已移除，在页面里用 `c.TitlebarInsets()`，在自绘代码里用 `Canvas.TitlebarInsets`。 - `ui.AlertDialog(c, spec)` 现在是 `ui.AlertDialog(c, &open, spec)` 并返回确认结果；`PopoverWith` 的 `*bool` 移到了 spec 之前；`base` 包同步变化。`ui.TextFieldID` 变为泛型，按值取用该函数的代码要写类型实参。 - `Box.Variant` 选的按钮变体互不叠加：指南里的 `Ghost().Destructive()` 以前是实心红色按钮，现在是带危险色的幽灵按钮。 - dev 构建会拒绝没有 `Columns` 也没有 `Headers` 的 `Table`，它以前什么也不画。 - Android 与 iOS 后端不在 Viva 主分支上，它们留在 `mobile` 分支；桌面上的触摸、手指滚动、捏合与触摸提示都在本版本里。 - 细则见 [升级指南 / Upgrading](../upgrading.md#v068---uirun-takes-a-pageconfig-and-four-more-signatures)。  - `ui.Run(config, m.page)` is now `ui.Run(config, ui.PageConfig{View: m.page})`. A program written from the guides on `main` before v0.6.8 does not compile against v0.6.7 for this reason alone; with v0.6.8 the guides and the code agree. - `app.TitlebarInsets()` is removed: use `c.TitlebarInsets()` in a page and `Canvas.TitlebarInsets` in custom painting. - `ui.AlertDialog(c, spec)` is now `ui.AlertDialog(c, &open, spec)` and returns the confirmation; `PopoverWith` takes its `*bool` before the spec; the `base` package changes alike. `ui.TextFieldID` is now generic, so code taking the function as a value must name a type argument. - Button variants chosen with `Box.Variant` do not combine: the guide's `Ghost().Destructive()` used to be a solid red button and is now a ghost with the danger color. - A dev build stops a `Table` with neither `Columns` nor `Headers`, which painted nothing before. - The Android and iOS backends are not on Viva's main branch; they continue on the `mobile` branch. Desktop touch, finger scrolling, pinch and touch tooltips are in this release. - Details are in the [upgrading guide](../upgrading.md#v068---uirun-takes-a-pageconfig-and-four-more-signatures). 
```

[macOS Intel · DMG](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-darwin-amd64.dmg) - [Windows x64 · 安装 EXE](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-windows-amd64-setup.exe) - [Windows x64 · 便携 ZIP](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-windows-amd64.zip) - [Linux x64 · DEB](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.deb) - [Linux x64 · 便携包](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.tar.gz) - [SHA-256 校验值](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/SHA256SUMS) - [全部下载文件](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/tag/v0.4.8)  ### 重点更新  每个窗口拥有自己的活动仓库、编辑页签、提交草稿和终端，已添加的仓库列表和偏好设置在窗口间共享。  #### ✨ 新功能  - 从左上角项目菜单选择「在新窗口中打开…」，在保留当前窗口的同时打开另一个本地仓库。  #### ⚡ 改进  - 退出应用或确认重启安装更新时，Viff 会检查所有窗口是否有未保存的编辑。  ### 升级说明  - macOS：按机型选择 Apple Silicon 或 Intel 的 DMG，退出旧版后打开 DMG，将 Viff.app 拖入“应用程序”并替换旧版。 - Windows：退出旧版后运行 x64 安装器。 - Windows 便携包：解压后进入新目录执行 `viff.exe`，不写入安装信息。 - Debian/Ubuntu：执行 `sudo apt install ./viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.deb`。 - Linux 便携包：解压后进入新目录执行 `./viff`，并自行安装所需系统库。 - Git 需单独安装并加入 `PATH`，Linux 系统库要求见[发布指南](https://github.com/1lck/viff/blob/v0.4.8/docs/releases.md)。  ### 兼容性与已知问题  - macOS 要求 13 或更高版本。Linux 需要可用的图形会话、渲染后端和发布指南中列出的系统库。 - macOS 应用使用 ad-hoc 签名，未取得 Developer ID 签名和公证。Windows 安装器未取得 Authenticode 签名。系统可能显示安全提示。 - 应用重启时恢复一个窗口。  仅安装来自项目发布页且已核对校验值的文件。  [查看完整变更](https://github.com/1lck/viff/compare/v0.4.7...v0.4.8)  ---  ## English  Viff 0.4.8 lets you open repositories in separate windows so you can view and work on several projects side by side.  ### Downloads  - [Project homepage](https://github.com/1lck/viff) - [macOS Apple Silicon · DMG](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-darwin-arm64.dmg) - [macOS Intel · DMG](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-darwin-amd64.dmg) - [Windows x64 · Setup EXE](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-windows-amd64-setup.exe) - [Windows x64 · Portable ZIP](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-windows-amd64.zip) - [Linux x64 · DEB](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.deb) - [Linux x64 · Portable archive](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.tar.gz) - [SHA-256 checksums](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/download/v0.4.8/SHA256SUMS) - [All downloads](https://github.com/1lck/viff/releases/tag/v0.4.8)  ### Highlights  Each window has its own active repository, editor tabs, commit drafts, and terminal, while the repository list and preferences are shared across windows.  #### ✨ New features  - Choose Open in New Window… from the project menu in the upper-left corner to open another local repository while keeping the current window open.  #### ⚡ Improvements  - Viff checks every window for unsaved edits when you quit the app or confirm a restart to install an update.  ### Upgrade instructions  - macOS: choose the Apple Silicon or Intel DMG, quit the old version, open the DMG, and replace Viff.app in Applications. - Windows: quit the old version and run the x64 installer. - Windows portable: extract the archive, enter its directory, and run `viff.exe` without installing. - Debian/Ubuntu: run `sudo apt install ./viff-v0.4.8-linux-amd64.deb`. - Linux portable: extract the archive, enter its directory, and run `./viff` after installing the required system libraries. - Install Git separately on `PATH`, and check the [release guide](https://github.com/1lck/viff/blob/v0.4.8/docs/releases.md) for Linux dependencies.  ### Compatibility and known issues  - macOS requires version 13 or later. Linux needs a working graphical session, rendering backend, and the libraries listed in the release guide. - The macOS app uses ad-hoc signing and has no Developer ID signature or notarization. The Windows installer has no Authenticode signature. The operating system may show a security prompt. - Viff restores one window when it restarts.  Install packages from the project release page after checking their checksums.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/1lck/viff/compare/v0.4.7...v0.4.8)  ### 🙌 感谢贡献者  感谢 [@genhao0823](https://github.com/genhao0823) 提出需求，以及 [@1lck](https://github.com/1lck) 实现此功能。  ### 🙌 Contributors  Thanks to [@genhao0823](https://github.com/genhao0823) for requesting the feature and [@1lck](https://github.com/1lck) for implementing it.

## 📝 更新日志 / Changelog (v4.7.0)  ### 中文  ### 新增功能  - **DeepSeek ASR**：新增 DeepSeek 在线语音识别，支持非流式录音识别 - **按语言推荐识别服务商**：可按常用语言查看推荐的在线和本地识别服务商，推荐项以星标显示并在组内优先排列 - **自定义 GitHub 下载镜像**：「其他设置」可添加、编辑和删除镜像。下载本地模型或开源版更新时，可在下载源弹窗中选择镜像并查看延迟 - **[Pro] ElevenLabs 热词和上下文**：录音文件和流式识别均支持热词，流式识别还可使用配置的上下文  ### 改进优化  - **ElevenLabs 模型升级**：录音文件识别升级至 Scribe v3，流式识别升级至 Scribe v3 Live - **粤语识别选项**：百炼、火山引擎、ElevenLabs 和 FunASR Nano 多语种模型新增粤语选项 - **本地模型说明**：模型版本选项显示模型体积和简短的内存建议 - **增强保活**：支持 Porter、Shizuku 和 Root，并优化保活实现方式 - **剪贴板附件同步**：优化选定目录的文件变动的坚挺方式，减少 CPU 消耗 - **UI 优化**：优化部分组件 UI 细节和文案细节  ### 问题修复  - **[Pro] 自动备份设置迁移**：避免覆盖已设置的开关和备份间隔  ---  ### English  ### New Features  - **DeepSeek ASR:** Added DeepSeek online speech recognition for non-streaming recordings. - **ASR Provider Recommendations:** View recommended online and local providers by language. Recommended providers are marked with a star and prioritized within their groups. - **Custom GitHub Download Mirrors:** Add, edit, or remove mirrors in Other Settings, then select one when downloading local models or open-source app updates. - **[Pro] ElevenLabs Hotwords and Context:** Added hotword support for file and streaming recognition, plus context support for streaming recognition.  ### Improvements  - **ElevenLabs Models:** Upgraded file recognition to Scribe v3 and streaming recognition to Scribe v3 Live. - **Cantonese Recognition:** Added Cantonese options for Alibaba Qianwen, Volcengine, ElevenLabs, and multilingual FunASR Nano models. - **Local Model Details:** Added download sizes and brief memory recommendations to model variants. - **Enhanced Keep-Alive:** Added Porter, Shizuku, and Root support, with improvements to keep-alive behavior. - **Clipboard Attachment Sync:** Improved file-change monitoring for the selected folder to reduce CPU usage. - **Settings UI:** Refined several interface details and localized text.  ### Bug Fixes  - **[Pro] Auto-Backup Settings Migration:** Prevented migration from overwriting the configured switch and backup interval.

After dragging Airhand into Applications, run this once in Terminal: `xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine /Applications/Airhand.app` - **Android TV / Google TV**: the same APK; sideload it (e.g. `adb install`) and it shows up in the TV's launcher. ## 下载与升级  **图形客户端**是桌面应用，自带终端客户端 `ccdt`；只用终端时（例如没有图形界面的机器）单独下载 `ccdt`。`ccdt` 在自己的终端里使用原版 Claude Code，与图形客户端共用会话。都要与服务端同一版本。  | | Windows | macOS（实验性） | Linux（实验性） | | --- | --- | --- | --- | | **图形客户端** | [x64 安装包](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/CC-Desk-Tunnel-Setup-0.3.19-x64.exe) | [Apple 芯片](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/CC-Desk-Tunnel-0.3.19-mac-arm64.dmg) · [Intel](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/CC-Desk-Tunnel-0.3.19-mac-x64.dmg) | [x64 .deb](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/CC-Desk-Tunnel-0.3.19-linux-amd64.deb) | | **终端客户端 ccdt** | [x64](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/ccdt-0.3.19-windows-x64.zip) | [Apple 芯片](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/ccdt-0.3.19-mac-arm64.tar.gz) · [Intel](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/ccdt-0.3.19-mac-x64.tar.gz) | [x64](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/ccdt-0.3.19-linux-x64.tar.gz) · [arm64](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/ccdt-0.3.19-linux-arm64.tar.gz) |  **一键安装 `ccdt`**：装的是最新的正式版；要装这一版时先指定 `CCDT_VERSION=0.3.19`，见[终端客户端](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/cli.md#安装)。  powershell # Windows，在 PowerShell 里运行 irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/main/deploy/install-ccdt.ps1 | iex   sh # macOS、Linux curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/main/deploy/install-ccdt.sh | sh   - **已在使用**：在图形客户端的“设置 → 关于与更新”里先升级服务端，再按提示升级客户端；图形客户端自带的 `ccdt` 随它升级；单独安装的 `ccdt` 运行 `ccdt upgrade`，服务端与它自己一起升级。 - **新部署服务端**：在服务器上用一条命令安装，见[快速开始](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel#快速开始)；镜像为 `ghcr.io/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel:0.3.19`，[服务端程序包](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/cc-desk-tunnel-server-0.3.19.tar.gz)是安装和升级时下载的。 - **校验**：[SHA256SUMS](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/SHA256SUMS) · [release.json](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/releases/download/v0.3.19/release.json)。Windows 安装包没有代码签名，SmartScreen 与杀毒软件的提示见[说明](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/troubleshooting.md#杀毒软件与-smartscreen)。  ---  > 在一台服务器上集中运行 Claude Code，让它在你的多台电脑上执行开发和操作，无公网的机器也能连入；会话在桌面、手机和终端之间共用。  0.3.18 的跟进版：帮助改为独立的帮助中心，界面上看不懂的地方可以直达讲解，新功能也有了上手指引；ACP 客户端里可以直接选模型。0.3.18 的更新一并列在下面，从更早版本升级的可以一次看全。  ## 新增功能  - 帮助中心：左侧栏的 ? 打开。   - 按主题分组的目录和搜索；首页讲清它怎么工作、三步上手。   - 文章配有界面示意图，以及点一下就能试的提示词。   - 界面上不好一眼看懂的地方旁边有 ?，直达讲它的那一段：项目、其他设备、审批模式、定时任务、文件面板、额度、各设置页和难懂的设置项。   - 空会话里给出开场示例；“这一版新增”列出新功能和用法，更新说明里也能直接跳过去。 - 设置的讲解按想达到的效果组织，讲清几项设置怎么配合：   - 缓存与压缩；   - 让任务无人值守地跑完；   - 思考、快速模式与备用模型；   - 记忆与说明；   - 在你电脑上执行；   - 权限开关管到哪里。    另外标出哪几项 CLI 设置只在 `ccdt` 里起作用。 - 会话按名字连到你其他在线的电脑：Claude 用 `ssh device-<名字>` 在那台电脑上执行命令，用 `scp` 在两台电脑之间传文件，`devices` 列出在线的电脑。它只做你要求的事，改动前先确认；审批照常由 Claude Code 自己做，审批卡片上能看到命令发往哪台电脑。 - 在另一台在线电脑的项目里新建会话，或把会话移过去：“其他设备”下列出各台电脑的项目，悬停项目点加号即在那台电脑上新建；“移到项目…”也按电脑列出其他在线电脑的项目。运行中的会话不能移到另一台电脑。 - `ccdt online`：不开终端，只让这台电脑保持在线，断线自动重连，适合没有图形界面的服务器、NAS 等。同一台电脑上的 `ccdt` 窗口会借用它的执行通道。后台运行的写法见 `docs/cli.md`。 - 其他 agent、脚本和编辑器可经 `ccdt` 调用 Claude：   - `ccdt -p "任务"` 派发，写法同 `claude -p`；`status`、`wait`、`tell`、`approve`、`deny`、`answer`、`logs`、`stop` 跟进。   - `--output-format stream-json` 逐行输出过程；开了“显示思考过程”时含思考。   - Claude 请求批准或提问时以退出码 2 停下，由调用方处理，图形客户端里同时也能处理。   - `ccdt acp` 作为 ACP agent 供编辑器接入，编辑器里可以直接选模型、推理强度和审批模式。   - `ccdt help agents` 是给模型看的完整用法。 - 图形客户端自带 `ccdt`，随它一起升级，“用 ccdt 打开”用的也是它：   - Windows 安装时加入当前用户的 PATH，卸载时去掉；   - macOS、Linux 上图形客户端启动时成为 `~/.local/bin/ccdt`，macOS 要先把应用拖进“应用程序”再打开（见 [macOS 客户端](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/macos.md#终端客户端-ccdt)）；   - 已经单独安装过 `ccdt` 时，以图形客户端自带的为准。 - 项目由服务端保存：图形客户端、浏览器和 `ccdt` 看到同一份项目，离线电脑的项目也在；可以改名、移除，名称各端一致。移除只删这一项登记，文件夹、会话和 Claude 的项目记忆都不动，其中的会话变为普通会话；再添加同一个文件夹，原来的会话和记忆都回来。 - 文件面板里，服务端以编号命名的工作目录旁标出所属的项目或会话。  ## 优化改进  - 设置页的说明压成一行，详细内容放进帮助。几处与 Claude Code 实际行为不符的说明已更正：   - 新模型上关掉“思考”不起作用；   - 订阅账号的快速模式从额外用量扣费；   - 备用模型最多 3 个；   - 关掉“触发安全拦截时换模型”后，图形客户端里这一轮以出错结束；   - “禁止在工作目录外写文件”只管服务器上的文件。 - CLI 设置里的开关项写明默认是什么：默认（开启）、默认（关闭）或默认（自动）。（#242，itisvincent） - 手机浏览器里长按对话文字，改用系统的选择与复制；桌面客户端里对话以外选中的文字，右键也能复制。各处菜单文字缩短。（#238，Pandaver） - 会话统计的“Token 用量”卡片补上等价费用，以及 1 小时与 5 分钟缓存写入的拆分；账号页只放账号的额度与统计。 - `ccdt` 打开会话时显示项目名称，普通会话显示所在文件夹。 - 文件面板的名称过长时省略，不再把大小和修改时间挤出去。  ## 问题修复  - 订阅额度会过旧或中途消失：`ccdt` 会话一轮结束后额度不见；没有读数时，显示与否还和“运行中每分钟读取一次额度”的开关有关。现在额度作为账号的状态由服务端推送给每个窗口，窗口到了重置时间也随之更新。 - Windows：本机 OpenSSH 的默认 shell（注册表 `HKLM\SOFTWARE\OpenSSH\DefaultShell`）设成 PowerShell、pwsh 或 Git Bash 时无法连接。现在照常连接；连接失败时显示本机 shell 的原话，不再笼统归因于安全软件。（#236，Amigosazul） - macOS、Linux：本机 SSH 服务在登录前不再处理会被用来卡住它的请求（ssh2 的 CVE-2025-70034），服务端文件面板同样处理；另修几处命令名额与进程清理问题。 - 服务端文件面板偶尔一直打不开，要重启服务端才恢复。 - 停止文件面板里的复制时立即结束。 - `ccdt` 连接被放弃时只显示“连接已关闭。”，现在说出原因。 - Windows：Git Bash 里找不到 `ccdt`，只能写全名 `ccdt.cmd`。现在直接运行 `ccdt` 即可，参数原样传入（`"/review"` 这类任务不会被当成路径），其他 agent 在 Git Bash 里也能用 `ccdt -p`。  ## 升级说明  - **从 0.3.18 升级**：协议版本不变，服务端没有改动，先升级哪一端都能连上。照常在图形客户端里升级即可；单独安装的 `ccdt` 运行 `ccdt upgrade`，才有 ACP 里的模型选择。 - **从 0.3.17 及更早版本升级**：   - 协议版本升到 29，图形客户端、`ccdt` 与服务端需要一起升级。照常在图形客户端里先升级服务端；图形客户端自带的 `ccdt` 随它升级，单独安装的运行 `ccdt upgrade`。服务端镜像没有变化，不需要在服务器上另行升级。   - 服务端第一次启动时按已有会话登记项目；各台电脑上 0.3.17 客户端里的项目列表和名称，在每个连接第一次连上新服务端时交给它。   - “显示思考过程”未设置时改按官方默认，不再显示思考摘要，图形客户端与 `ccdt` 一致；需要的在“设置 → CLI 设置”里开启。   - 已经单独安装过 `ccdt` 的，装上新的图形客户端后，终端里的 `ccdt` 换成图形客户端自带的那份（macOS 要先把应用放进“应用程序”并打开一次）；单独安装的文件留在原处，不再使用，可以删掉。   - 会话的提示词多了一句关于其他电脑的说明，升级后各会话的第一轮会重建一次提示缓存。  ## 平台支持  Windows 上的图形客户端和 `ccdt` 是主要支持的平台。macOS 与 Linux 上的图形客户端和 `ccdt` 都是实验性支持：没有代码签名，实际使用的人还少；图形客户端不带应用内升级，升级时重新下载安装，`ccdt` 照常用 `ccdt upgrade`。安装与首次打开见 [macOS 说明](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/macos.md)、[Linux 说明](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/linux.md)与[终端客户端](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/docs/cli.md)。服务端在 x86_64 上是主要支持，arm64 是实验性支持。  文档：[部署手册](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/deploy/README.md) · [变更记录](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) · [安全须知](https://github.com/sun168567/cc-desk-tunnel#安全须知) 
```

### 2026-11-24T05:30:36Z [**condrdev/condr**](https://github.com/condrdev/condr)

```
Condr 0.2.6 brings Git to Workspaces that hold several repositories, an Agents section you can group by state, and shell integration that works like Ghostty's.  ## Highlights  **Several repositories in one Workspace**  When a Workspace's Root Directory holds several repositories, Condr now shows each one's Changes and History. Changes lists every repository as a node with its branch, History picks one from a menu, and Files dims what each repository ignores. A Root that is itself a repository also picks up the repositories right under it, and Changes shows its own as a node named after the Workspace.  **Agents grouped by state**  A button on the Agents section's heading groups Agents by state or keeps the sidebar's order. **Blocked** and **Error** always come first, and an Agent that rang the bell now comes right after them, here, in the Command Palette's Go to and in Tasks.  **Shell integration like Ghostty's**  zsh and bash now put their prompt marks into the prompt itself, so they come back whenever the shell redraws it. zsh, bash and fish report their directory as you move around, and set the cursor as you work: a blinking bar at the prompt, a block in zsh's vi command mode, and the program's own shape while a command runs.  ## Fixed  - A terminal's cursor blinks by default, as Ghostty's does; a program can still ask for a steady one. - While an input method composes, its text covers the cells it sits on, as wide as the terminal draws them, and the cursor and candidate window follow its insertion point.  ## Update  Update the app and every Device's Server to 0.2.6.  - Desktop: download the installer below. - Headless Server: `curl -fsSL https://condr.dev/install.sh | sh`, or on Windows `irm https://condr.dev/install.ps1 | iex`. 
```

OpenIDEA 0.1.51 brings a large Python update (Python Packages and Python Console tool windows, Pipenv and Hatch environments, Smart Type completion, create-from-usage, f-string and type-annotation intentions, `# noinspection` suppression, Rearrange Code, `.pyi` stub navigation and import updates on move), test trees for Mocha, Playwright and node:test, Compare Directories, Biome and Stylelint linting, Tailwind CSS support, Helm and Go templates, embedded languages in template literals and Spring annotations, viewing pandas and numpy values as tables in the debugger, remote sessions that survive closing the window, faster Git Log, Find in Files and Agents, and Plugin API 1.15.  ### Download  | Architecture | Windows | macOS | Linux | |---|---|---|---| | x86-64 (64-bit) | [EXE](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/OpenIDEA_0.1.51_x64-setup.exe) &nbsp; [DEB](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/OpenIDEA_0.1.51_arm64.deb) &nbsp; [RPM](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/OpenIDEA-0.1.51-1.aarch64.rpm) |  **Remote development:** `openidea-server` for [Linux x86-64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/openidea-server-linux-x86_64.gz) · [Linux ARM64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/openidea-server-linux-aarch64.gz) · [macOS x86-64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/openidea-server-macos-x86_64.gz) · [macOS ARM64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.51/openidea-server-macos-aarch64.gz), each with a `.sha256` file.  **Already installed?** OpenIDEA updates itself: use **Check for Updates**.  ## What's new  ### Python - **Python Packages** tool window with installed and latest versions; **Python Console** tool window; view and edit an interpreter's `sys.path`. - Create and detect **Pipenv** and **Hatch** environments; requirements the interpreter doesn't satisfy are flagged with an install fix. - **Smart Type completion**; create a function, class or method from an unresolved reference; convert `%`-formatting and `str.format()` to f-strings; add type annotations from inferred types. - Docstring stubs in the selected format, **Rearrange Code** for classes, navigation between implementations and `.pyi` stubs, imports updated when a module or package moves. - `# noinspection` comments suppress the IDE's Python inspections; Optimize Imports uses Ruff without a Ruff configuration; Code Style › Python applies to Ruff and Black.  ### Editor - **Helm** and **Go templates**; CSS, GraphQL, SQL and HTML highlighted in template literals; JPQL, SQL and SpEL in Spring annotation strings. - **Biome** lint and format, **Stylelint** for CSS, SCSS and Less, the Tailwind CSS language server in Tailwind projects. - Language server colours in the gutter; documentation stubs with tags for Java, TypeScript and Python; a `;` typed before `)` moves to the end of the statement. - Markdown: stacked editor and preview layout, linked footnotes, math rendered as SVG. - Color Scheme: a JavaScript / TypeScript page, Diff & Merge colours with a live preview, more Kotlin entries; a custom accent colour from the colour picker.  ### Run, debug and test - **Mocha**, **Playwright** and **node:test** tests run with a test tree and Rerun Failed Tests. - Debugger: view **pandas and numpy values as a table**; toggle an exception breakpoint from a `throw` or `catch`. - Several Maven configurations started together build in one Maven run.  ### Version control - **Compare Directories** by content, text, size or timestamp, with editable folders and per-row synchronization. - Commit… scoped to the selected project directory; branches and stashes grouped by repository across linked worktrees. - Git Log pages, history filtering and status refreshes stay fast on large histories.  ### Workbench and projects - Attached projects have their own excluded folders; rarely used tool windows start off the stripe and the More Tool Windows button can be moved. - The UI language follows the system on first launch; Remote Development is a header button on the Welcome screen.  ### Remote development and services - Remote backend, terminals, runs, agents and workbench state persist when the window closes; WSL projects forward their ports. - Docker: Compose service resource usage and named volumes, volume disk usage, and a jump from Compose containers to their Compose file. - Database: pick a JDBC driver from a preset; tree visibility follows row counts.  ### Agents - GitHub skill browser with preview, static scan and installer; skill metadata badges; copy an agent reference for files and selected code. - Tool result images are kept and shown; the context meter follows Claude Code compaction; long conversations stream without re-rendering the whole chat.  ### Performance - Faster Find in Files on large result sets and large or non-UTF-8 files; typing stays fast in large TypeScript files; Git Log graph and history pages scale with what is loaded. - Hidden agent sessions stop rendering; IPC events are serialised once for all windows; run and build output queues are bounded.  ### Plugin API 1.15 - Declarative `[[contributes.skills]]` in `plugin.toml` and caret-moved events.  ### Security - The workbench Content Security Policy restricts `base` and `form` targets; `url` and `cookie` updated for RUSTSEC-2026-0592 and RUSTSEC-2026-0594. https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/compare/v0.1.50...v0.1.51  ## 中文  OpenIDEA 0.1.51 大幅增强 Python 支持：新增 Python Packages 和 Python Console 工具窗口，支持 Pipenv 和 Hatch 环境、智能类型补全、从未解析引用创建函数/类/方法、转换为 f-string、按推断类型添加注解、`# noinspection` 抑制检查、Rearrange Code、实现与 `.pyi` 存根间跳转以及移动模块时更新导入；Mocha、Playwright 和 node:test 测试可在测试树中运行并重跑失败用例；新增目录比较；支持 Biome、Stylelint 和 Tailwind CSS；支持 Helm 和 Go 模板，模板字符串和 Spring 注解中的嵌入语言可高亮；调试器可将 pandas 和 numpy 值以表格查看；关闭窗口后远程会话状态得以保留；Git 日志、在文件中查找和 Agent 更快；并带来 Plugin API 1.15。

<!-- msime-download-guide --> ## 下载  大多数人下载第一行的**水杉输入法**（`MetasequoiaIME-Full_Setup_v0.6.1.exe`）就行：它是完整版，包含全部输入方案。其他版本只带其中一部分方案，可以和完整版装在同一台电脑上。每个安装程序都同时支持 x64 和 ARM64 的 Windows。  | 版本 | 安装程序 | | --- | --- | | **水杉输入法**（完整版，推荐） | [MetasequoiaIME-Full_Setup_v0.6.1.exe](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/windows-v0.6.1/MetasequoiaIME-Full_Setup_v0.6.1.exe) |  下载后运行安装程序；输入法还不在键盘列表里时，它的设置窗口顶部会提示「去添加」。`SHA256SUMS` 是这些安装程序的 SHA-256 校验值；每个安装程序旁边的 `.exe.sha256` 是包管理器清单和设置页的校验提示要用的，一般不用下载。 <!-- /msime-download-guide -->  ## v0.6.1 更新内容  ### 🐛 问题修复  **兼容性与安装**  - 修复在 QQ 等自带旧版运行库的应用里使用输入法时应用崩溃的问题。0.6.0 本应让输入法在这类应用中改用独立的静态运行库，但完整版里这项隔离没有生效，输入法仍会和应用共用同名运行库；本版本修正后，输入法在应用进程里只加载自带静态运行库的宿主。(#11678，修复 #11675，相关 #11593) - 修复 0.6.0 在部分电脑上输入法无法激活的问题：系统里能看到水杉输入法，切换过去却仍由 Windows 默认输入法处理输入。(#11678，修复 #11675) - 升级或重装后，安装器会确认输入法已经在当前用户的键盘列表里启用，系统注册稍有延迟时自动重试，不再出现「看得到但用不了」的情况。(#11669，修复 #11657)  **语音**  - 豆包语音识别使用新版 API Key 时返回的未压缩结果现在可以正常识别，不再全部丢失。(#11644，修复 #11641)  ### 🔧 开发与构建  - 为跨设备学习同步加入底层的端到端加密同步轮次、密文队列和密钥存储；同步的设置入口和自动同步还没有开放。(#11527，相关 #10043) - 修复非完整版收集输入法宿主符号时的文件名错误。(#11652) - 本版本的安装程序、包内程序和运行库均已签名。  <!-- msime-build {"run":44564619443,"source":"5719141a3d56ecf9fbca371c1104d07768fad9a5","channel":"stable"} --> 
```

### 2026-11-25T06:20:26Z [**t8y2/motty**](https://github.com/t8y2/motty)

```
## 新功能  - SFTP 文件列表里可以直接行内重命名文件，侧栏、双栏窗口和标签页通用。 - SFTP 文件可以拖出应用到桌面。 - 工具栏按钮可以把输入广播到所选标签页。 - 没有 Docker 时可以管理 Podman 容器。 - Gitee 代码片段备份支持超过 1 MB 的内容。 - 传输中心在涉及多台服务器时，按服务器分段显示。  ## 改进  - SFTP 文件操作图标重新设计，上传、新建、返回上级一目了然。 - 工作区命令按钮移到主机名称旁，地址不再被截断。 - 多显示器的大尺寸 VNC 桌面不再被拒绝连接。  ## 修复  - 在笔记文件夹内新建子文件夹不再崩溃。 - 右键菜单重命名标签后，输入框立即获得焦点。 - 工作区命令说明不再重叠显示。 - 重命名遇到已存在的文件时可以替换。 - 延迟突增后，后台端口转发会继续重试。 - VNC 支持光标伪编码并显示本地指针。  ## 安装  | 平台 | 包 | | --- | --- | | macOS（arm64、amd64） | `.dmg`、`.zip` | | Windows（amd64、arm64） | 安装程序、便携版 zip（数据在程序旁的 `motty-data`） | | Linux（amd64、arm64） | `.deb`、`.tar.gz` |   Built against vivaui/viva `cfae17036c97fb111cc911fc0e4748f8f96c128f`. 
```

### 2026-11-25T07:58:57Z [**Binaryify/MyGit**](https://github.com/Binaryify/MyGit)

```
 - 右键直接把文件移到废纸篓（[#52](https://github.com/Binaryify/MyGit/issues/52)，感谢 [@nuo010](https://github.com/nuo010) 的建议）：更改列表里文件的右键菜单多了「移到废纸篓…」，AI 生成的不想要的文件不用再去访达里删。确认后文件进废纸篓（Windows 上是回收站，提示文字也会跟着系统变），还能找回；已跟踪的文件删掉后会显示为「已删除」，后悔了丢弃这条更改就回来了。未跟踪的文件原来的「丢弃更改…」就是移到废纸篓，现在直接叫这个名字。SSH 打开的远程仓库没有废纸篓，显示为「删除…」，确认后彻底删除 - 更改标记可以显示为文字（[#53](https://github.com/Binaryify/MyGit/issues/53)，感谢 [@nuo010](https://github.com/nuo010) 的建议）：设置里勾选「更改标记显示为文字」，文件后面的 M、A、D、U 会显示成完整的「已修改、新增、已删除、未跟踪」，重命名、复制、冲突也一样显示全称；英文界面显示 Modified、Added 等。默认仍是 git 的字母，鼠标停在字母上照旧显示完整说明 - 修复文件名很长时，更改列表和提交的文件列表里右边的 M、U 等标记和操作按钮被挤出去看不到，现在文件名会以「…」截断

## [6.10.1](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.10.0...rea-agents-6.10.1) (2026-11-20)  This is the first 6.10 release published to npm. It includes the changes, migration notes, and contributor credits from 6.10.0 below, plus the patch fix.  ### Bug Fixes  * **kuna:** use one constant for the serial job count ([#12735](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/12735)) ([3e3faf2](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/3e3faf2fc32dbadc96a45f51f4361eb64ce6ed32))  ---  ## [6.10.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.9.0...rea-agents-6.10.0) (2026-11-20)  ### Highlights  - Inspect and extract UEFI firmware volumes, files, and AMI NVRAM with three offline CLI and MCP tools. [#12342](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12342). Thanks @bartman081523. - Inspect Android boot images and OTA payloads, NES 2.0 containers, and Maven, Gradle, and NuGet package metadata. [a88c0a5](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/a88c0a55544e16a710ddaad2feb5a2c6e7011f4e) [#9487](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/9487) [#11378](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11378) [#10674](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10674) [05be423](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/05be423acbdf3a56673bdbd3004e4b500c0f5217) [#10353](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10353) [43b9fa8](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/43b9fa87fbbaa74224731a1fe3a8e67a2d336ae3) [#10351](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10351) [#8144](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/8144). Thanks @Davmunrey, @gensokyo-dev, @jayykernel, @mvanhorn. - Evidence revision 2 gives file, process, debugger, and browser observations explicit subject identities. See the migration details below. - The verified Ghidra 12.2 lane opens recognized DOS COM files and supports more RISC-V, MIPS32, and PowerPC64 ELF targets. [#4811](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4811) [#4487](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4487) [#2362](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/2362) [#2338](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/2338) [#12517](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12517) [#12085](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/12085). Thanks @abhay-2108, @AurelioB, @Davmunrey, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @Rinkia.  ### Changes  - JavaScript semantic indexing uses fixed object shapes for nested evaluation contexts to reduce allocation overhead. [#9007](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/9007). Thanks @mshzy. - Inspect ELF dynamic dependencies and required symbol versions, resolve separate debug-file linkage, inspect DWARF compilation units offline, and decode FDO packaging and dlopen notes. [e9c72db](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/e9c72dbd1fa6b318cb3e455c92df064eed62333a) [#7868](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/7868) [54e3918](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/54e3918d1c09991970aadf8f0eb1ab6bcf6f83fb) [#9560](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/9560) [637e4cb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/637e4cb60c99b1b17026855297cb03e299c3ba89) [#8523](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/8523) [#7237](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/7237) [#7235](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7235). Thanks @AniketR10, @gensokyo-dev, @mvanhorn. - Read cargo-auditable dependencies from WebAssembly and export Go and Rust dependency Evidence as CycloneDX. [#5069](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5069) [237b1ea](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/237b1ea5b39ea9fc5373aa08a9f54d25715c4873) [#8445](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/8445). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @mvanhorn, @yu0809. - Decode CPython 3.14 and 3.15 bytecode and use Kuna 1.1000. [1d08253](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/1d08253ff2cb68838b93cf9275000c39bf1f507a) [#10518](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10518) [#12107](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12107). Compact MCP input schemas use a portable advertised dialect. [#10709](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10709) [#9354](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/9354) [#11019](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11019) [#10632](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10632). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji. - Reader errors name failed integrity checks and enforced limits. Bridge refusals identify whether the request was sent, and recovery names the relevant control or the next operation for an oversized MCP result. APT inspection honors disabled deb822 stanzas, uses ASCII separators, preserves tokens and effective identities for one-line sources, and verifies single-stanza identities. Desktop application lookup honors hidden overrides and rejects malformed overrides or desktop group padding before masking IDs. Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @Plastic-time, @ShuaixinHuang. - Windows shortcut inspection handles pre-epoch timestamps and validates LinkInfo offsets and fixed-size ExtraData blocks. Windows executable discovery uses PATH/PATHEXT, continues past malformed entries, and refuses unresolved objdump or Rizin commands. Headless IDA stdio bounds executable discovery, handles Windows launchers, and retains failed-launch cleanup for retry. [#10305](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10305) [#10303](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/10303) [#12403](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12403) [#10361](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10361) [422cff0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/422cff00ab989e5c5eecbcf50ebb65ca271c8b87) [#10891](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10891) [998faa4](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/998faa41189c6924bdb42d18dc584ec5a8775d71) [#10739](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10739). Cleanup reports live resources independently of failed closes, retains composite provider clients for retry, and combines provider cleanup failures while retaining their causes. They preserve GNU version slots and dynamic-string diagnostics and distinguish an exhausted string table from a capped scan. ELF admission validates dynamic table framing, section and zlib declarations, interpreter sizes, and dependency table extents, and preserves unwind budget facts. [#10215](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10215) [#10189](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10189) [#12674](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12674) [#12685](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12685) [#12678](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12678) [#12693](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12693) [#12445](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12445) [#12193](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12193) [#12113](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12113) [#12344](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12344) [#12308](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12308). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @wunianze666-netizen, @XiaoBinGan. - PE readers reject out-of-file resource directories before applying the entry budget, validate certificate framing before capacity checks, and bound TLS and export reads to declared section content. GGUF readers reserve framing for fields following metadata values. UEFI extraction accepts the same `max_volumes` and `max_files` scan controls as inspection. [#10375](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10375) [#12336](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12336) [#12432](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12432) [#12338](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12338) [#12538](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12538). - Gradle recognizes metadata via the root version marker, reports unfamiliar format versions, and preserves variant identity. Maven preserves qualified property keys and non-ASCII whitespace in POM version values, and matches declarations in the project namespace. NuGet handles OPC part-name equivalence and rejects invalid manifest relationship target modes. [#12488](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12488) [#12648](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12648) [#12677](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12677) [#11860](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11860) [#12672](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12672) [#12691](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12691) [#12684](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12684) [#12714](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12714). Thanks @ShuaixinHuang, @xu91102. - Android mapping inspection reports residual signatures without a mapping member. Boot and OTA readers validate boot headers, vendor ramdisk tables, and OTA manifest and signature extents before capacity checks. MIPS inspection identifies unsupported Android relocation declarations. Harmony inspection matches manifests at exact archive-root paths. Deno lockfile inspection retains valid specifiers alongside undecodable rows. [#12494](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12494) [#12396](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12396) [#12395](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12395) [#12431](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12431) [#12397](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12397) [#12700](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12700) [#12667](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12667).  - Mach-O parsing validates symbol extents before the hardening scan limit and maps high-backed segments in their declared virtual range. Hopper verifies the Mach-O profile signature before loader selection. ELF and Android OTA share exact-frame Zstandard decoding. [#12465](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12465) [#12479](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12479) [#12485](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12485) [#12416](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12416) [#12398](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/12398). - GPU inspection reports malformed containers as `invalid_format` and validates standalone PTX encoding during discovery. Core-note reads respect the recorded source range, and PyInstaller and Swift readers check entry framing and vtable bounds before applying capacity ceilings. [#10644](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10644) [#12707](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12707) [#12634](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12634) [#12622](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12622) [#12627](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12627). - Go SBOM export retains local modules without Package URL namespaces. Managed inspection retains module identity when its name is unreadable. The reference-source importer preserves cancellation during policy-file cleanup, and process capture handles Darwin-helper exit without crashing the host. [#12293](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12293) [#12441](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12441) [#12323](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12323) [#11714](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11714). Thanks @IvenKooLab. - MCP doctor honors output choices on argument errors. Capture image readers validate PNG headers, and trace and keyed-archive errors expose the enforced parser limit. Sigrok catalog scans retain selected decoder rows only. The documentation site resolves authored links to their published routes. [#2693](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/2693) [#12310](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12310) [#12335](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12335) [#12324](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12324) [#12284](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12284) [#12453](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12453). Thanks @jasonchen505.  ### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES  - Evidence records and hand-built bundles require `evidence_format: 2`. Recreate older observations, bundles, and snapshots with 6.10. Re-exporting old observations does not update their identities. Read `subject.identity` in place of `subject.digest`, `subject.format`, and `subject.architecture` (file identities use `sha256`). Every record has a subject, using an explicit unknown identity when appropriate. Identity, including an artifact's selection, participates in Evidence IDs. Recreate 6.7-era artifact summaries before using `compare_artifacts`. [#11416](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11416) [#10861](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10861). - Snapshots use `target: {kind, identity}` and a non-null `execution.subject: {identity, name, local_path}`. Binding errors use the same target shape. Universal Mach-O Evidence IDs include the selected slice. Live page tools and their views use a `{kind: "url", url, origin}` identity with credentials removed, null origin for opaque URLs, and null subject name and local path. Withheld or unreadable URLs retain an unknown identity. Recreate affected Evidence and snapshots. [#11551](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11551) [PR 11559](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11559/files). - Process captures require `manifest.root_process`. Their subject identity is `{kind: "process", pid, start_time, executable_sha256}` when observed, `{kind: "unknown", reason: "not-observed"}` when the start identity could not be read, and `{kind: "unknown", reason: "withheld-by-caller"}` when PID normalization is enabled. Accept `cleanup_outcome: "cleaned"` on quiesced settlements. Recreate saved process Evidence. Existing omission semantics still apply: `raw_data` is omitted when it equals `data`, and `residual_unknowns` is omitted when there are none. [#11568](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11568) [#10481](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10481) [#8032](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/8032) [#10956](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10956). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @N0zoM1z0, @Rinkia, @wunianze666-netizen. - Rizin command Evidence and GDB startup and console Evidence use `{kind: "session", session_kind, id, target}`. The session target identifies the initial launch file when REA can observe it. GDB accepts an optional executable. Without one, or when the file identity could not be observed, the target is unknown with reason `not-observed`. The binding describes the launch file only. The current inferior's identity requires a separate observation. Recreate debugger Evidence because its IDs change. [PR 11570](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11570/files) [PR 11572](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11572/files) [230db70](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/230db70fb473dd15928ae6c1a885f7e8a5507dae) [#11710](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11710) [#11581](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/11581). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @N0zoM1z0. - Ghidra and Hopper profiles replace hand-bumped markers with `analysis_profile.parameters.producer` containing `rea_version` and `sources_sha256`. Ghidra drops `bridge_sources_sha256` and version suffixes on selection values. Hopper drops `function_dossier_coverage`. Profiles and Evidence IDs change. Open the target and save a fresh snapshot. [#11320](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11320). - Ghidra requires a supported 12.x build starting at 12.2 and a JDK within that build's declared range (25 or newer for the verified 12.2 build). For a DOS COM file accepted by ComLoader, read the reported `load_address` (now linear `0x100`). The BinaryLoader fallback still uses `1000:0100`. [#12081](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12081) [#12086](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12086). - IDA attached registrations must explicitly set `"protocol": "native"` and use the native plugin endpoint for an open GUI database. Migrate legacy 1.4 proxy registrations to that mode or to an ida-pro-mcp 2.x supervisor in headless mode. `procedure_callers` is removed from IDA profiles, and IDA assembly/instruction tools drop the `may_show_ui` annotation. [#10846](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10846) [#11546](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11546) [#10516](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/10516). - Use `rea mcp` or `rea --mcp` in place of `rea <command> --mcp`. Regenerate shell hooks with `rea completions <shell>` and update Markdown argument-diagnostic snapshots for the new rendering. Flag-shaped unknown MCP subcommands honor the selected output format. [7d502dd](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/7d502ddb0d8d80d0e16a80b0edc813f704e3ac31) [#11724](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11724) [62014b5](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/62014b566d79c563933e32182c4e7515fd9a54e2) [#11742](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11742) [#10583](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/10583) [#11654](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/11654). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @N0zoM1z0. - MCP rejects `full` input and output schema profiles. Remove those settings or select `compact`. An unset `REA_MCP_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE` now means `off`; set it to `compact` to receive `tools/list[].outputSchema`. MCP limit errors omit `details.limit.cli_flag`; the CLI still supplies its own flag when applicable. [#10693](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10693) [#11413](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11413). - Use absolute local paths in MCP calls to `inspect_pcap_capture`, `start_rizin_debug_session`, `start_gdb_session` (including `inferior_tty`), `load_frida_script`, and `instrument_with_frida`. CLI `--script-file` still accepts a relative path and records the resolved absolute `source_path`. [#11508](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11508) [#11079](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/11079) [#11580](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11580) [#11506](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/11506). - Native `rea analyze` rejects JavaScript-only options such as `--integrity-policy` with `invalid_request` naming the flag. Pass those options only for JavaScript targets. For `inspect-managed-members --detail summary`, rename `--evidence-bundle <path>` to `--evidence_output <path>` and read `.summary` and `.evidence_bundle` in stdout. Publication failures use the shared error shape. Composite CLI failures such as those from `compare-managed-members` can carry completed inputs as `details.argument_observations[]` entries with `{argument, observation}`. [PR 10808](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10808/files) [PR 10845](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10845/files) [PR 10961](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10961/files). - For a missing, unreadable, or non-regular target-selected plist, decode `target_unavailable` with `details.path` and `reason` in place of `invalid_request` with input issues. Open a plist outside the current app bundle or resource root as a separate target. [#9754](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/9754) [#10798](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10798). Thanks @Anandumv. - In Cutter JSON command output, integers beyond JavaScript's safe range are decimal strings. Parse them with a lossless integer type. Cutter's `output_truncated` and `document_generation` may be null. Treat truncation as unknown and list sessions again before choosing `expected_generation`. GDB MI decodes `\e` to ESC instead of rejecting the record. [#12457](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/12457) [#10726](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10726). - ADB package output preserves whitespace in `version_name`. Decode `numeric_issues` and `coverage`; malformed `versionCode` and `user_id` declarations make coverage partial. For a malformed version token such as `45abc`, expect a `version_code` issue with reason `invalid_format`, an increased issue total, and a null version code unless a later valid declaration supplies it. [1adc3fa](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/1adc3fa5532c9ac96fe44620c051e943af45504c) [#10519](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10519) [#7123](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7123) [#7271](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7271) [#7281](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7281) [#7388](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7388) [#7409](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7409) [#7534](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7534) [#7692](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/7692) [#8067](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/8067) [#8141](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/8141) [#8302](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/8302) [#9195](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/9195) [#10172](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/10172) [#10389](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/10389). Thanks @1052326311, @2SinceJuly, @carcraze, @coygeek, @deepakrakshit, @Eldergenix, @gensokyo-dev, @HeadmasterEggy, @Hiba-Malik, @kittimzhe, @mawahnnie, @N0zoM1z0, @Rinkia, @saariuslystoned, @ShuaixinHuang, @sx4im, @theo-73, @wunianze666-netizen. - Update coverage decoders for native initializers and kernel build configs to accept `partial` for observed but incomplete coverage and `unknown` when completeness could not be established. ELF unwind ranges use `partial` in place of `incomplete`, and capped model metadata arrays use `partial` in place of `preview`. [#11483](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11483) [#11486](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11486) [#11463](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11463) [#11469](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11469). - Managed member comparison uses tagged objects in `limitations[]`; read `code` and `side` in place of `limitation_reasons` or message prefixes. Recreate managed observations. Supply explicit nullable `assembly`, boolean `nesting_read`, and `signature_resolution` as `"type-names-v2"` or null. Null is only valid without examined members, references, call edges, or field accesses. The historical bare-name comparison mode is removed. Call edges and field accesses require `target_row_resolved`. [#11177](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11177) [#10952](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10952) [#11334](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11334) [PR 10992](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10992/files). - Recreate JavaScript and Electron captures missing runtime `scripts.parse_failed_total` or active Electron `parameters.resolved_paths`. Application analysis requires `statistics.skipped_javascript_files` and `summary.protocol_registrations`. Original-source and module-edge links require `source_index` and `declaration_index`. [#10958](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10958) [#10990](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10990). - Recreate browser captures using the current producers. Source-map items require `effective_url`, invalid maps require `response_identity`, and mapping rows require `source_index` and `declaration_index`. WebMCP declarations require `schema_property_names_coverage`. Storage results require `content_fingerprints`, `fingerprint_algorithm`, and `fingerprints_complete`; IndexedDB schema fingerprints require `projection_version: 2`. Link rows require `context_url` and `context_scope`. [PR 10991](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10991/files) [PR 11002](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11002/files). - Browser page inspections require accessibility states, script `frame_id`, and worker `opener_target_id` and `parent_frame_id`. Web-bundle captures require `capture.target_origin`. Scenario network rows with a null redirect predecessor require `redirected_from_transaction_state`. In page network requests, include `redirects: []` when there are no redirects. DOM snapshots require `node_value_length_unit`, and redirect timeline rows require `redirect_response`. Recreate captures before comparing them, including inline inspections. Re-import reference sources so every `exclusions[]` row carries `entry_kind`, using the explicit unknown kind where applicable. [#11344](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11344) [PR 11014](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11014/files) [PR 10897](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10897/files). - Recreate PCAP Evidence with `dns_queries[].query_labels_hex` and explicit tshark/capinfos identity fields. Recreate desktop state Evidence with `effective_view_policy.sqlite_view_bytes`, SQLite Evidence with `worker_timeout_ms`, and Dart AOT Evidence with `string_pool.policy`. [PR 10879](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10879/files) [PR 10886](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10886/files). - Recreate Android packages, component views, and reference observations with component declaration facets, `target_definition`, and `direct_invokes_coverage`. Declaration coverage and counts drop `unknown`; manifest views declare `facet: "manifest"`. JNI failures with kind `integrity` require `integrity_check`. [#11624](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11624) [PR 10904](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10904/files). - Recreate native Evidence for Mach-O encryption, malformed slice causes, import facts and symbol `runtime_name`; ELF import/export encoding and export visibility; value-flow `limits`; load-image permissions and relocation symbols; Swift `type_reference_kind`; and GPU `amd_target_metadata`. Update strict readers for removed legacy values such as unknown ELF export visibility and `modinfo_field_exceeds_limit`. [PR 10946](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/10946/files). - Recreate inventory Evidence missing ESP `format_metadata.flash_dump`, PE signer observations missing `unsigned_attribute_oids`, and firmware inspection or extraction results missing `analysis_timeout_ms`. [#11595](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/11595).  Full contribution record: [1589 commits and 1383 merged PRs](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/rea-agents-6.10.0/docs/releases/6.10.0.contributions.json).

### 2026-11-25T07:03:26Z [**yetone/magpie**](https://github.com/yetone/magpie)

```
## Features - Cindy's Pi now includes the same MCP servers as the Library.  ## Improvements - Added the memory stats endpoint to the CLI route parity list.  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`  <!-- lang:zh -->  ## 功能 - Cindy's Pi 现在包含与 Library 相同的 MCP 服务器。  ## 改进 - 将内存统计端点添加到了 CLI 路由一致性列表中。  ### 安装  从 [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai) 下载，或运行 `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`。已安装的 magpie 会自动更新。    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.4781 
```

## NyaTerm v2.0.8  本次更新加入 FTP/FTPS 文件连接、终端本地模式与关键词高亮管理，并增强快捷命令、外观主题、Android 设备操作及凭据填充体验。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **FTP/FTPS 连接与文件管理**：新增 FTP 连接类型，支持普通 FTP、显式及隐式 FTPS、被动连接模式、字符集与 ASCII 文本传输；可在双栏文件管理器中浏览目录、上传和下载文件，并使用现有文件操作能力（#1208，PR #1334）。 - **终端本地模式**：可在不向远端发送按键的情况下，使用键盘、鼠标浏览并选择终端输出，支持编辑器风格的光标操作和快捷键（PR #1342）。 - **终端关键词高亮管理**：可从终端右键菜单打开高亮规则管理器，分别维护当前会话和全局规则（#1317，PR #1342）。 - **快捷命令分段发送**：支持按行或按字符发送，分别设置发送间隔，并可中途停止；完善换行、取消以及多目标发送的处理（PR #1328）。 - **Android 屏幕手势**：支持配合 Ctrl、Shift 修饰键和鼠标模拟双指捏合，以及纵向、横向双指滑动。 - **OTP 智能建议**：可为动态验证码条目设置终端提示匹配规则，在匹配的验证提示中选择 OTP 并填入当前验证码（PR #1341）。 - **外观跟随系统**：新增随系统亮色／深色外观自动切换主题的选项，并允许终端配色跟随界面或独立设置（PR #1301）。  ### 🐛 问题修复  - 修复凭据自动填充在密码提示下的匹配边界、空凭据库处理，以及 OTP 过期后仍使用旧验证码的问题（PR #1310）。 - 修复终端本地模式、选区、光标定位及关键词高亮状态在部分交互中的异常，完善备用屏幕滚轮设置变更后的即时生效逻辑。 - 改善 FTP 文件传输的取消、错误处理与会话清理。 - 修复 Windows 贴边或特殊窗口状态下子窗口的位置计算，以及浮动面板层级和鼠标事件隔离问题（PR #1340）。 - 修复 Android 退出时部分由应用启动的 ADB 服务进程未能正确清理的问题（PR #1327）。 - 修复 AI 历史聊天显示和部分消息复制操作，以及快捷键搜索框对普通按键的处理。  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - 优化快捷命令和命令发送的分段调度、背压处理与取消响应，减少重复计算。 - 加强 Android ADB 服务启动及关闭的生命周期管理。 - 完善终端输入、会话状态与传输路径的边界处理，提升异常情况下的稳定性。  ### ♻️ 重构与优化  - 连接批量编辑按协议整理字段，并补齐部分 SSH、FTP、ADB 设置，便于批量维护不同类型的连接。 - 拆分桌面执行任务及主机工具相关内部模块，改善代码结构和测试覆盖。  ### 🎨 界面与交互优化  - AI 聊天消息加入复制操作和时间戳显示，调整操作按钮布局（PR #1330）。 - 新增终端命令块侧边标记栏的显示开关。 - 新增“新建连接保存后立即打开”与“操作链接中的命令插入终端而不直接执行”两个可选设置，默认保持原有行为（PR #1343）。 - 连接失败页面支持按 Enter 重新连接；连接描述最多显示三行；Docker 容器列表的名称和镜像增加悬浮提示（#1339）。 - 外观主题选择改为分段切换控件，并精简部分重复的界面说明。  ### 📚 文档与国际化  - 更新终端、主题和连接相关的使用说明，并同步完善六种界面语言的翻译。  ### 👥 贡献者  - @fonaix：新增 FTP/FTPS、终端本地模式和关键词高亮管理，改进连接批量编辑与 Windows 窗口交互（PR #1334、PR #1342、PR #1340）。 - @cdog-shen：实现快捷命令分段发送（PR #1328）。 - @LunaSpica：加入跟随系统外观切换主题的能力（PR #1301）。 - @zhonghes：新增 OTP 提示匹配，并改进终端凭据自动填充（PR #1341、PR #1310）。 - @skykiller15：新增连接保存后自动打开及操作链接命令插入选项（PR #1343）。 - @WerVa：新增备用屏幕滚轮发送方向键行为的控制选项（PR #1344）。 - @yaoxinghuo：改进 AI 聊天消息复制与时间戳显示（PR #1330）。 - @moonmicah：修复应用退出时的 ADB 服务清理（PR #1327）。  ### ⚠️ 升级说明  - **快捷命令兼容性**：旧版 NyaTerm 不识别本版新增的分段发送间隔等参数；若将新版本保存的快捷命令同步或导入旧版，实际发送方式可能不同，旧版重新保存也可能丢失这些新参数。 
```

## 修复 - **Gemini 3/4 思考档位不再报 400。**   - Gemini 3、Gemini 4、`gemini-*-latest` 和 `robotics-er-2` 改为发送 `thinkingLevel`，不再发送 `thinkingBudget`，修复 Gemini 4 返回的 `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT: Thinking budget is not supported for this model`。   - 档位按官方规范使用大写枚举（`MINIMAL` / `LOW` / `MEDIUM` / `HIGH` / `XHIGH`）。   - 档位按型号限定：     - `gemini-4-neon` / `gemini-4-hydrogen` 支持 `XHIGH`；     - Gemini 4 和 `gemini-3.8-flash` 不发送 `MINIMAL`；     - `gemini-3.1-pro-preview` 保留 `MEDIUM`。   - Gemini 2.x 和无法识别的中转别名仍使用 `thinkingBudget`。   - 使用完整地址时，模型名从 URL 中解析。（#249，via #250，感谢 @Is14w） - **兼容 Vertex AI 地址。**   - 基础地址可以填 `https://aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/publishers/google`（包括区域端点），也可以填以 `/models` 结尾的中转地址，不会再被重复拼上 `/v1beta/models`。   - Vertex AI Express Mode 不提供模型列表接口，拉取模型时会提示手动添加模型 ID。   - 带 OAuth 头时，从 Model Garden 拉取模型列表，并支持翻页。（#249，via #250） - **兼容原生 JSON 数组流。** Gemini 和 Vertex AI 的流式响应如果是 `[{...}, {...}]` 数组格式，也能正常解析，单块大小上限和断流重试的处理不变。完整地址里的 `:streamGenerateContent` 会自动补上 `alt=sse`。（#249，via #250）  ## 兼容性 - 只影响 Gemini 服务商。  ## 其他 - 新增 Gemini 思考档位、Vertex 路径、模型列表翻页和 JSON 数组流的测试。（#250）

### 2026-11-25T07:49:36Z [**openvetta/open-vetta**](https://github.com/openvetta/open-vetta)

```
# Vetta 0.6.23 <!-- release-status: unreleased -->  ## English  ### New  - You can now edit and resend any earlier message, not just the last one. The agent answers again using only the conversation before it; the original and what followed stay as another version you can switch back to on the bubble. - With input prediction on, the first suggestion appears as faded text in the empty input box; press Tab to fill it in, or type or press Esc to dismiss it. The toggle moved from Settings → Agent to Personalization, and stays as you set it.  ### Improvements  - On Windows, button groups drop the glass effect, and the buttons at the bottom of the sidebar and on the right of the title bar have no background until you hover or select them. macOS and Linux are unchanged.  ### Fixes  - SSH hosts can now be connected on Windows. Each action opens its own connection, so running commands takes one extra handshake and hosts with two-step login may ask you to verify more than once. - Remote hosts whose login shell is csh or tcsh now connect and work normally, including running commands, reading and writing files, and background tasks. - On Windows, an SSH host that can't be reached is now reported as a connection failure instead of a failed remote command.  ## 中文  ### 新增  - 较早的提问也能编辑重发，不再只限最后一条。改完发送后，模型只沿用这条之前的对话重新回答；原提问和后面的对话保留为另一个版本，可在气泡上切换回去。 - 开启输入预测后，第一条预测以淡色显示在空输入框里，按 Tab 填入，打字或按 Esc 即放弃；开关从「设置 → Agent」移到「个性化」，原有设置不变。  ### 改进  - Windows 上按钮组去掉玻璃效果，侧边栏底部和标题栏右侧的按钮平时没有底色，只在悬停或选中时显示；macOS 和 Linux 不变。  ### 修复  - Windows 上可以连接 SSH 主机了。每次操作单独建立连接，执行命令会多一次握手，开了两步验证的主机可能会多次要求验证。 - 登录 shell 是 csh 或 tcsh 的远端主机现在能正常连接，执行命令、读写文件和后台任务都可用。 - Windows 上连不上 SSH 主机时，会提示无法连接，而不是报远端命令执行失败。  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/openvetta/open-vetta/compare/v0.6.22...v0.6.23 
```

# Pebrel 2.6.1  ## English  macOS, Linux, and Android downloads are Preview builds.  ### Fixed  - Fixed enabling launch at login on Windows when the user’s Startup folder is missing. Addresses [#1028](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1028). - Fixed pasting TIFF screenshots into terminal panes. Addresses [#1016](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1016). - Fixed Windows upgrades and uninstalls removing user-edited WSL context menu registry entries. - Fixed opening a directory in an existing elevated Windows window ignoring the configured default shell profile. Addresses [#1042](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1042). - Fixed SSH agent hooks dropping a complete answer that fits the message limit when unrelated tool output makes the event too large. - Fixed local OMP conversations losing their session on restart. On Windows with PowerShell 7, reopening Pebrel restores the same conversation after a normal close or forced exit. Addresses [#1027](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1027). - Fixed Windows terminal tabs losing their previous output when waking from sleep. Addresses [#992](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/992). - Fixed Windows ignoring the `esc` alias when configured as the shortcut to minimize the window. - Fixed Windows upgrades leaving the WSL context menu pointed at the previous installation directory. - Fixed SFTP browsing after a cached channel closes. SSH image paste now retries a temporary channel refusal once before starting the upload. - Fixed reopening Pebrel to an idle shell when automatic Codex recovery is enabled and a saved tab has no thread ID. The tab now opens the Codex conversation picker. - Fixed a close confirmation acting on the wrong tab or split when its position changed while the dialog was open.  ## 中文  macOS、Linux 和 Android 下载为预览版。  ### 修复  - 修复 Windows 用户的启动文件夹缺失时无法启用登录自启动的问题。对应 [#1028](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1028)。 - 修复 TIFF 截图无法粘贴到终端窗格的问题。对应 [#1016](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1016)。 - 修复 Windows 升级或卸载时误删用户改过的 WSL 右键菜单注册表项的问题。 - 修复在已打开的 Windows 管理员窗口中打开目录时忽略默认 Shell 配置的问题。对应 [#1042](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1042)。 - 修复 SSH 上的 Agent 因工具输出过长而丢失完整答复的问题。答复本身未超出消息上限时会完整送达。 - 修复本地 OMP 对话在重启后丢失会话的问题。在 Windows 的 PowerShell 7 中，正常关窗或强制退出后重新打开 Pebrel 均可恢复原对话。对应 [#1027](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/1027)。 - 修复 Windows 终端标签休眠唤醒后丢失原有滚动历史的问题。对应 [#992](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/992)。 - 修复 Windows 在配置文件中使用 `esc` 绑定最小化窗口时，按 Esc 键无效的问题。 - 修复 Windows 更改安装目录升级后，WSL 右键菜单仍指向旧目录的问题。 - 修复缓存通道关闭后 SFTP 目录无法浏览的问题。向 SSH 标签粘贴图片时，若服务器临时拒绝打开通道，会在开始上传前重试一次。 - 修复已开启 Codex 自动恢复、但标签缺少对话 ID 时，重开 Pebrel 只看到空闲 Shell 的问题。现在会在原标签打开 Codex 对话选择器。 - 修复关闭确认框打开期间标签或分屏位置变化后，确认操作会关错目标的问题。  ## Contributors  <p>   <a href="https://github.com/Kuddev"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/292709043?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Kuddev" title="Kuddev" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/WilliamWang1721"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/140129782?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="WilliamWang1721" title="WilliamWang1721" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/yjjheizhu"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/86939240?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="yjjheizhu" title="yjjheizhu" /></a> </p>  - [@Kuddev](https://github.com/Kuddev) - [@WilliamWang1721](https://github.com/WilliamWang1721) - [@yjjheizhu](https://github.com/yjjheizhu)  ## SHA256  text 35e690d01788fc26eb9175c7c3027bfc7834d783bd3ebb9f6fd99dc86dea2628  Pebrel-v2.6.1-android-universal-preview.apk 210ad32e62a4f74f7d9c1ca8dcd47062fa7cd3b4d6df0de1a37f29ab627ec670  Pebrel-v2.6.1-linux-x64-preview.AppImage ea011973273a954468c74fa793010223d86d1b7243ce874ba25515aa445d2a09  Pebrel-v2.6.1-linux-x64-preview.deb 1190f22c007ded15594476bc62730e7945c6ac05b45386d8de49906e098e5678  Pebrel-v2.6.1-linux-x64-preview.tar.gz 0683b79912c422337128c254ad2a7449cc9bc25fdeb06ee23d7f9b54fa2512c2  Pebrel-v2.6.1-macos-arm64-preview.dmg eab33a95700630c0660159b0875ab0a7f3fc057c6a967860cf8c94b80400764d  Pebrel-v2.6.1-macos-x64-preview.dmg 9656ae6d6a6ace94aa00032e008610cb3355666916af7ae95b63cbacada66c72  Pebrel-v2.6.1-relay-manual.tar.gz d1137609b60f9739de791487eef1dd6d09c653d03ca9e52c23a0b5bd5056d75b  Pebrel-v2.6.1-windows-arm64-setup.exe 54be1d08514f2d3fecee8705f1bd12625a2b2c1df20437fb98356bbbcdddfdbf  Pebrel-v2.6.1-windows-arm64.zip f3812c572f912d419b205eee7bab943b587dcdd94c1eee4f72a3c93b2d3650ef  Pebrel-v2.6.1-windows-x64-setup.exe 55191de815fad98fa72c7db222f20416292d8e499f33e6e2b4def020a5997417  Pebrel-v2.6.1-windows-x64.zip
