### 2026-11-23T05:49:03Z [**tailscale/tailcat**](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat)

```
## Release notes  ### Breaking Go API change  - `Server.AllowedClients` and `Server.AddAllowedClient` are replaced by the `Server.AllowClient` hook, a `func(key.NodePublic) bool` asked about each client as it connects, so a program embedding tailcat can decide at runtime, for example from a database, instead of listing every key up front. A nil hook allows all clients. The hook runs without any server lock held, so it may block and may call other `Server` methods. For a list of keys, the new `KeySet` type's `Contains` method is a ready-made hook: `s.AllowClient = allow.Contains`. The new `Server.DisconnectClient` drops a connected client; it does not reset the client's connections, which stall instead. The `--allow` flag is unchanged. ([#134](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/134); see also [#119](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/119) and [#120](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/120) by [@jormenjanssen](https://github.com/jormenjanssen), and [#124](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/124) and [#125](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/125) by [@ybaelli](https://github.com/ybaelli))  ### New features  - `tailcat serve` takes port mappings like `5555:10.2.200.213:5555` to proxy a served port to a host on the server's network, or `8080:80` to a different port on localhost, instead of always the same port on localhost. This exposes one LAN service without the whole network that `exit-node` would. Targets may be hostnames, such as `5555:android.lan:5555`, resolved with the system resolver. ([#128](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/128); hostnames in [#132](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/132) by [@breken-ai](https://github.com/breken-ai)) - `tailcat serve` can proxy to a local Unix-domain socket. A serve argument starting with `/` or `unix:` names the socket; given alone, as in `tailcat serve /run/git-annex/socket`, it takes connections to every port not otherwise served, so clients need no port number, and as a mapping target, as in `8022:/run/app.sock`, it takes just that port. The server refuses to start if a socket doesn't exist. ([#93](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/93), requested by [@matrss](https://github.com/matrss); [#104](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/104) by [@atirna](https://github.com/atirna)) - `tailcat serve --log` writes an access log to stderr: a line when a client is allowed in or refused by `--allow`, and lines when each of its TCP connections or UDP flows opens and closes, with the client's public key, the port and service or exit-node destination reached, and whether the path is direct or via DERP. It is separate from `--verbose`. ([#39](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/39); [#99](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/99) by [@sksingh2005](https://github.com/sksingh2005), with UDP in [#196](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/196); thanks also to [@avionicharshit-byte](https://github.com/avionicharshit-byte) and [@SriVishnu230707](https://github.com/SriVishnu230707) for [#127](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/127) and [#137](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/137)) - `tailcat serve --qr` also prints the server's address as a terminal QR code, for copying it off a screen you can't paste from, such as a remote console. This adds about 160 KB to release binaries. ([#81](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/81); [#95](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/95) by [@harrisonhjones](https://github.com/harrisonhjones)) - The `ssh` and `no-auth-ssh` services support SSH local port forwarding (`ssh -L` and `ssh -D`) to loopback IP addresses on the server, which VS Code Remote-SSH needs. Forwarding to hostnames (including `localhost`) and remote forwarding (`ssh -R`) stay off, as does forwarding for the `files` service and forced commands. ([#141](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/141) by [@HappyAny](https://github.com/HappyAny)) - `tailcat ssh` and `tailcat cp` take `-i <identity-file>` to log in to a `tailcat serve ssh` server with a specific private key, without an ssh config entry. ([#177](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/177) by [@semmi88](https://github.com/semmi88)) - New `tailcat perf` command and `perf` service run an iperf-like throughput and latency test between a client and a server, over TCP or UDP, in either or both directions, with one or more parallel streams. UDP tests report loss, reordering, and jitter; all tests report round-trip latency under load. By default the test refuses to run until the path is direct, since a throughput test through a shared DERP relay mostly measures its rate limit; `--via-derp` allows relayed tests through your own relay, never through Tailscale's shared ones. ([#131](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/131); [#144](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/144) and [#145](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/145) by [@sntxrr](https://github.com/sntxrr) fixed relayed paths) - New experimental `tailcat serve --hybrid` flag (and `Server.Hybrid` in the Go API) gives post-quantum protection that doesn't depend on the tailcat address staying secret.    Through v0.5.0, tailcat had no post-quantum protection, and a DERP relay's operator, who sees the public keys of everyone using the relay, could connect to any server on it that didn't restrict clients with `--allow`. v0.6.0 added a pre-shared key to tailcat addresses, which DERP operators never see. It does two jobs: it keeps DERP operators out, and it adds post-quantum protection. But both only hold while the address stays secret; once it's published, as in a DNS TXT record, neither does.    With `--hybrid`, the server and its clients use Tailscale's hybrid key exchange, which adds ML-KEM-768 to the handshake, so connections are post-quantum safe even when the address is public. The pre-shared key stays, but its only job now is keeping out compromised or malicious DERP operators, so hybrid addresses carry 128 bits of it instead of 256, making them shorter. Clients from v0.7.0 and earlier can't use hybrid addresses and reject ones with a pre-shared key at parse time. ([#133](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/133); [#160](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/160)) - tailcat now works in restricted sandboxes such as ChatGPT Work's ([tailscale/tailscale#22412](https://github.com/tailscale/tailscale/issues/22412); [#176](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/176)):   - If a DERP relay's HTTP upgrade fails, tailcat retries by speaking DERP inside WebSockets, as browsers do. This gets through proxies that intercept TLS and allow WebSocket upgrades but block others.   - If tailcat can't monitor the OS network state (such as without netlink access), it continues without reacting to network changes instead of failing to start.   - Connections to DERP relays now go through the HTTP proxy named by `HTTPS_PROXY` (or the OS proxy settings, on Windows), or a SOCKS5 `ALL_PROXY`. Release binaries had compiled proxy support out; keeping it adds about 250 KB.  ### Go library and language bindings  - New `Server.PeerKey` returns the node key of the peer behind an accepted connection or UDP flow, the key the tunnel authenticated, so a program can tell which allowed client it is serving. This also fixed `TAILCAT_PEER_KEY` being left out of the environment for UDP flows. ([#129](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/129) by [@Mo3he](https://github.com/Mo3he)) - New `Client.DERPRegion` reports which DERP region a client is using. - New C shared library, `cmd/libtailcat`, exposes servers, clients, listeners, and connections through opaque handles, with per-call error messages and tokens to cancel any operation from another thread. It builds as a Windows DLL too, usable from MSVC through an import library. ([#130](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/130) by [@vvuk](https://github.com/vvuk); fixes in [#169](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/169) by [@iyuzhang](https://github.com/iyuzhang) for [#163](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/163) reported by [@rundel](https://github.com/rundel), and in [#180](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/180) and [#191](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/191) by [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate)) - New `TailcatKit` Swift package wraps the C library with Swift async servers, clients, listeners, and connections for macOS 14+ and iOS 17+. ([#64](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/64) by [@josuediazflores](https://github.com/josuediazflores))  ### Bug fixes  - A client allowed to modify files on a `files` service (`:rw` or a drop box) could crash the server with a malformed SFTP SETSTAT request. ([#181](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/181) by [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate)) - A client resetting a connection right after connecting could crash an `exec` service server, along with every other connection on it. ([#188](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/188), from [#167](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/167) by [@bra1nDump](https://github.com/bra1nDump)) - An `exec` service connection whose client stayed connected without sending anything kept running after the command exited, leaking a zombie process. ([#189](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/189); thanks to [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate) for [#182](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/182)) - A DERP map with a `null` region or node, from `--derpmap-url` or the Go API, crashed tailcat instead of being ignored. ([#183](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/183) by [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate)) - `Addr.Resolve` kept the first two nodes of each region even if they were STUN-only nodes, which can't relay and aren't encoded, leaving the resolved address with fewer than two relays or none. ([#173](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/173) by [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate)) - `Server.Close` and `Client.Close` could hang forever, leaking the WireGuard engine and DERP connection, after a few hundred recent short connections. Closed TCP connections now linger in TIME-WAIT for 5 seconds instead of 60, which also makes far fewer new connections stall when they reuse a recent port. ([#151](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/151), reported by [@FelineStateMachine](https://github.com/FelineStateMachine); [#161](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/161) by [@dajiaohuang](https://github.com/dajiaohuang) and [#186](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/186)) - On a host with no network interface other than loopback, such as a build sandbox, a server could fail to pick a home DERP relay for its first 20 to 26 seconds, so clients timed out. ([#138](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/138) by [@sophronesis](https://github.com/sophronesis)) - DNS now works on Android 9 and earlier, where v0.7.0 failed with errors like `androiddns: bogus answer length`: those versions lack the DNS resolver command that v0.7.0's Android support used. ([#126](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/126), reported by [@risharde](https://github.com/risharde); [#135](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/135)) - `tailcat serve` rejects port 0 in ports and ranges, and invalid target ports like `5555:0` or `5555:99999` in mappings, instead of accepting ports no client could reach. ([#179](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/179) by [@CJstate](https://github.com/CJstate)) - `tailcat socks --listen` accepts a bare IPv6 address such as `::1`, as documented, instead of failing with "too many colons in address". ([#155](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/155) by [@RaphaelFakhri](https://github.com/RaphaelFakhri)) - Choosing a DERP region no longer leaves two UDP sockets and their goroutines open for the life of the process (or of the caller's context, for Go library callers). ([#193](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/193) by [@joshuagay](https://github.com/joshuagay)) - The web (WebAssembly) client kept a copy of every chunk it wrote until the page closed. ([#150](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/150) by [@gjija](https://github.com/gjija))  ### Packaging and dependencies  - Releases now include an `android_arm64` binary for Termux. The v0.7.0 notes said the linux binaries work under Termux, but on Android 10 and newer Termux runs programs through Android's dynamic linker, which rejects them with `has unexpected e_type: 2`. They still work from `adb shell` or a rooted shell. ([#184](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/issues/184), reported by [@jy1655](https://github.com/jy1655); [#185](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/185)) - Updated the tailscale.com dependency from its 2026-09-16 main branch to its 2026-11-18 one, along with gVisor and wireguard-go. Building from source now needs Go 1.27.4 or later. The WebAssembly build is about 230 KB smaller, since it now omits UDP and NAT traversal code a browser can't use. ([#139](https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat/pull/139))   ## Changelog  * b9491c13c63925af4119aca70bd21b1691ff3d5b CHANGELOG.md: update for v0.8.0 release * ec53cc46858b52be0e8e5dc551e148b84dd39c5c cmd/tailcat: only wrap OnTCP for serve --log when it's given * 8f99ae2c32875ad3fceab10f310c2569f9710aad cmd/tailcat: log UDP flows with serve --log * 038415dbb89fcb252a5b296a009cda3dcbbfac72 swift: commit the TailcatKit scheme xcodebuild builds with * ecdbd1fcd3e6b27930ee6381b7997be6b6941d6c cmd/tailcat: serve Unix-domain sockets * f216f3eceaefceab632c1ffeffd27f245849287c tool/updateflakes: always hash the vendor tree * a3f969dd36938353d007c5f5b409ccdafbe1c1ce cmd/tailcat: add serve --qr to print the address as a QR code * returns * c14b171db436062b86e95aa09e5792d1d1fd90f1 internal/capi: keep connection addresses after half close * 1dd28b9e3143e71da51fdc9d39d467db0b9654ba cmd/tailcat: pick tcpForwardTo's dialer once, # 中文  本次 Next 更新将上游升级至 DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.5，macOS 正式改为按芯片架构分别提供 Apple Silicon（`arm64`）与 Intel（`x64`）独立安装包，将「桌面设置」整理为四个标签页并支持切换回「经典」应用图标，优化了长会话打开与流式输出时的界面样式开销，完善 Windows 安装版与便携版的更新体验，并修复树莓派等 ARM64 Linux 断线重连及桌面端 `LiveShare` 创建分享等问题。  DSH NEXT 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.5](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.5)。  ## 新增与改进  - **macOS 按芯片架构分包:** macOS 不再提供 `universal` 通用包，改为分别提供 Apple Silicon（`arm64`）与 Intel（`x64`）独立 DMG 安装包以减小体积。 - **桌面设置分页与经典图标选项:** 「桌面设置」按功能重组为「常规」、「Profile 与数据」、「浏览器访问」、「诊断」四个标签页，切换标签保留输入状态；桌面设置与首次引导支持一键切回「经典」旧鲸鱼图标，Windows 与 Linux 随深色主题自动切换暗色图标。 - **上游 `0.2.2-rc.4` / `rc.5` 与性能体验优化:** DeepSeek 模型默认使用 DeepSeek 服务端搜索且不按次收费（其他模型默认使用 DeepSeek Search API）；修复 `LiveShare` 在桌面端无法创建分享的问题，优化模型返回异常／拒答响应时的处理；优化了长会话打开与流式输出时的样式重算开销；为 Linux 补齐「编辑」、「视图」、「窗口」等系统菜单本地化翻译；内置 `dshmarket` 更新至 `1.73.21`。 - **故障定位与日志优化:** 故障报告与日志可直接从上游诊断中列出失败或未加载的插件名称，端口冲突时可在快速恢复页切回自动分配；合并诊断日志写入并折叠重复插件输出，减少主线程占用。  ## 修复  - **Windows 安装与便携版更新:** 修复 Windows 应用内更新因安装目录工作路径占用或数据目录低完整性权限标记导致失败的问题，将更新安装包改为下载到系统临时目录并安全回滚；Windows 便携版（Portable）改为提示并打开下载页手动替换，避免重复安装常规版。 - **ARM64 Linux 连接与流式模型兼容:** 修复部分采用 39 位虚拟地址空间的 ARM64 Linux 内核（如树莓派 4）上界面持续重连的问题；同步上游模型底层补丁，修复工具长参数流式解析高 CPU 占用及 Gemini 思考签名回放异常。 - **macOS 原生组件与退出状态:** 修复 macOS 打包遗漏部分原生能力模块的问题，并修复 Host 启动失败后被覆盖为正常退出码的问题。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS · Apple Silicon (ARM64) | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.dmg` | | macOS · Intel (x64) | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64.dmg` | | Windows x64 · 安装版 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Windows x64 · 便携版 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64-Portable.zip` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - **macOS 分包说明:** 本版起仅提供 `arm64` 与 `x64` 独立安装包，手动下载请按本机芯片选择对应包（2.0.22 / 2.0.23 应用内更新可自动匹配本机架构，更早版本请手动下载安装）；Windows 版若 2.0.23 因数据目录低完整性权限更新失败，请手动下载本版安装一次。 - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`（或在“设置 → 桌面设置”中使用“备份”），安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - 从 2.0.17-next 或更早版本升级时，建议手动下载安装；旧版应用内更新可能无法完成下载。 - Office 文档技能首次使用时将随包运行时安装到 `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime`，需要额外磁盘空间。 - 默认与旧稳定版共用 `~/.dsh`；Profile 不隔离同一目录中的会话、账号和凭据。 - 可在“设置 → 桌面设置”或恢复助手中切换数据目录，也可通过绝对路径 `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 隔离 DSH 数据（应用内选择优先于环境变量），但不会因此隔离 AA 的默认共享登录与设备绑定状态。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.23-next...v2.0.24-next) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT 已取代原稳定版通道。  ---  # English  This Next release upgrades upstream to DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.5, switches macOS builds to separate Apple Silicon (`arm64`) and Intel (`x64`) installers, organizes Desktop Settings into four tabs with a switch back to the Classic app icon, optimizes style recalculations when opening or streaming in long sessions, improves Windows installer and portable update handling, and fixes reconnect loops on 39-bit ARM64 Linux devices and desktop `LiveShare` sharing.  DSH NEXT is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.5](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.5).  ## New and improved  - **Split macOS installers by CPU architecture:** macOS releases now ship as separate Apple Silicon (`arm64`) and Intel (`x64`) DMGs instead of a Universal DMG to reduce download size. - **Tabbed Desktop Settings and Classic app icon:** Reorganized Desktop Settings into **General**, **Profiles & data**, **Browser access**, and **Diagnostics** tabs while preserving drafts across switches; added an option in Settings and onboarding to switch back to the Classic whale icon, plus automatic dark-mode icons on Windows and Linux. - **Upstream `0.2.2-rc.4` / `rc.5` and UI performance improvements:** DeepSeek models now default to DeepSeek server-side search with no per-search charge (while other models default to DeepSeek Search API); fixed `LiveShare` failing to create shares in the desktop app and improved handling of unexpected model refusal responses; optimized onboarding style scoping to reduce style recalculation overhead in long sessions; localized Linux role menus (`Edit`, `View`, `Window`); updated bundled `dshmarket` to `1.73.21`. - **Plugin failure attribution and leaner diagnostics:** Failure reports and logs now name failing or unloaded plugins from upstream diagnostics, and Quick Recovery can revert port conflicts to automatic; batched diagnostic log writes and folded repeated plugin output to reduce main-thread overhead.  ## Fixes  - **Windows installer and portable updates:** Fixed in-app Windows updates failing when Setup inherited the install working directory or a Low-integrity label on the data directory by staging downloads in the system temp folder; Windows Portable builds now detect updates and open the release page instead of running the regular installer. - **ARM64 Linux connectivity and model streaming:** Fixed continuous reconnect loops on ARM64 Linux kernels with a 39-bit virtual address space (such as Raspberry Pi 4); aligned upstream `pi-ai 1.3.0` patches to fix high CPU during large streamed tool arguments and missing Gemini thought signatures on replay. - **macOS native modules and Host exit codes:** Fixed missing architecture-specific native modules in macOS builds, and preserved the non-zero exit code when a failed Host shuts down.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS · Apple Silicon (ARM64) | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.dmg` | | macOS · Intel (x64) | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64.dmg` | | Windows x64 · Installer | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Windows x64 · Portable | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x64-Portable.zip` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.24-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - **Split macOS packages:** Starting with this release, only separate `arm64` and `x64` DMGs are provided—choose the package matching your Mac's chip when downloading manually (in-app updates from 2.0.22 / 2.0.23 select your architecture automatically; earlier releases must update manually). On Windows, if 2.0.23 failed to auto-update due to a Low-integrity data directory, install this version manually once. - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` (or use **Back up** in **Settings → Desktop settings**) before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - When upgrading from 2.0.17-next or earlier, download and install manually; in-app updates in those versions may not complete the download. - The Office skills install the bundled runtime under `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime` on first use, requiring additional disk space. - Defaults to the shared `~/.dsh` home. Profiles do not isolate sessions, accounts, or credentials within that home. - Change the data directory in **Settings → Desktop settings** or the recovery assistant, or set `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` to an absolute path to isolate DSH data (an in-app choice takes precedence over the environment variable); this does not isolate AA’s default shared sign-in and device-binding state.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.23-next...v2.0.24-next) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT replaces the former Stable edition channel.

自 v0.20.1 以来，dsh-TUI 跟上 DSH `0.2.2-rc.5` 验证线（继续兼容 `0.2.2-rc.4`、`0.2.2-rc.3` 及更早验证线）， 为 `present` 工具加上专用交付卡，让 `/restart` 与 `/update` 在 POSIX 上原地替换进程，并修好 Windows `/update` 引擎解析、全屏跨页选区复制、`@` 模糊补全缓存、Linux 终端桌面处理器以及 Host 服务行诊断与 `dsh-working-activity` 依赖兼容问题。  ## 亮点  - **适配 DSH `0.2.2-rc.5`（含 `0.2.2-rc.4`）** — 主验证线从 `0.2.2-rc.3` 推进到 `0.2.2-rc.5`，peer 范围保留 `0.2.2-rc.4`、`0.2.2-rc.3` 及更早验证线，停在 rc.3/rc.4 的用户也能直接装这版，程序内 `/update` 会把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.5`。rc.4 为 DeepSeek `refusal` 停止原因新增的 `CONTENT_REFUSED` 结束码由 TUI 既有 `reason.kind` 分类自然覆盖；rc.5 起 `dsh-base` 不再固定 `web.searchProvider`，`/settings` → 网页搜索的「搜索」未配置时呈现为「自动」（DeepSeek 模型路由用 Messages 搜索，其他路由与对话外搜索用 Search API），并以 `settings.describe()` 的 `base` 层决定是否提供「自动」，同时按凭据引用去重展开 Messages / Search API 密钥控件。宿主复刻指纹与 rc.3 一致，本版新增 `0.2.2-rc.5` 的 patch surface 键。([#2065](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2065) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **`present` 交付文件卡** — `present` 工具不再渲染成原始参数 JSON 与纯文本 `Presented` 行，而是渲染为专用交付卡：折叠态按行显示四格类型徽章（或通过 `/settings` → 外观 → `nerdFontIcons` 开启 Nerd Font 图标）、文件名、文件大小与修改时间；展开（`Ctrl+O` 或点击卡片）后单独分行展示解析后的完整目标路径与模型描述；点击文件行打开既有文件操作菜单，相对路径优先从结果中的 `Presented <绝对路径>` 行解析（以 `meta.cwd` 兜底），工作目录切换后仍能命中同一文件。([#2047](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2047) by @Lucas2011wastaken)  ## 需要留意的行为变化  - **网页搜索默认改为「自动」** — 在 DSH `0.2.2-rc.5` 上，`/settings` → 网页搜索中的「搜索」未设置时显示「自动」，选中会清除用户层覆盖并交回按路由选择。若下层组合仍固定 `searchProvider`（旧验证线 `dsh-base` 或部署自带的 base 层补丁），界面不提供「自动」且手输 `automatic` 会被拒绝，与官方 Web 菜单一致；用户 profile 补丁只写用户层，仍可选「自动」清掉覆盖。在「自动」模式下，若 Search API（`searchApiKeyEnv`）与 Messages（`apiKeyEnv` / `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`）引用不同凭据，会分别显示「Messages API 密钥」与「搜索 API 密钥」；共用同一引用时只显示一个输入框。 - **`present` 交付卡底色与 `nerdFontIcons`** — 除极简界面（`minimalUi`）外，即使 `toolBackground` 设为 `none`，`present` 交付卡也保留自己的底色（无背景时暖橙、`subtle`/`strong` 时深紫罗兰）。`/settings` → 外观新增 `nerdFontIcons`（默认关闭），开启需终端使用 Nerd Font Mono，否则图标会显示为方框。 - **`/restart` 与 `/update` 原地替换进程** — 在支持 `process.execve` 的非 Windows 运行时上，普通 `/restart` 与 `/update` 会原地替换当前进程（保留 PID 与前台作业，不再保留等待中的父进程，也不再由父进程在启动失败时打印 `TUI did not come up / session preserved`）；Windows、不支持 `execve` 的运行时、目标预检未通过与内核切换仍走子进程监督交接。已委托给 `dsh --profile` 的启动链在普通重启时保持该路径，直到下次冷启动或切换内核。 - **全屏跨页选区跟随滚动** — 全屏拖选时按住鼠标配合 `PgUp` / `PgDn` 或滚轮跨页扩展选区，起点随文本移动、另一端留在指针处；松手后两端继续随滚动移动，完全离屏后保留已捕获文本且不再高亮边缘无关字符，滚回后恢复位置与列范围。不支持鼠标拖到视口边缘自动滚动；inline 模式仍用终端原生选区。 - **`@` 模糊补全按需刷新** — 工作区文件扫描完成后缓存 2 秒，过期后的下一次 `@` 查询才重扫（菜单静止时不轮询，空结果可立即重试）；`@src/` 等路径查询及原有 100 文件 / 100 目录扫描预算不变。 - **Linux `Terminal=true` 桌面处理器范围** — 只有本地文件的默认 `.desktop` 处理器声明 `Terminal=true` 时才包装进终端运行；URL、目录与非终端处理器仍走 `xdg-open`，带非空 `Path=` 的条目也保持直接交给 `xdg-open`（其 generic 路径仍可能无 TTY 或忽略工作目录）。  ## 修复  - **`/restart` 与 `/update` 原地替换与委托清理** — 普通 `/restart` 与 `/update` 在 POSIX 上通过 `process.execve` 原地替换进程（替换前预检 `process.execPath` 并显式刷盘 `usageStats`）；同时让委托给 `dsh` 启动的 `/update` 预先证明当前 DSH 入口归属以免 pnpm 组轮换后失效，并在会话销毁时同步关闭与清理 Claude 侧问及其私有凭据临时文件。([#2024](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2024) by @Lucas2011wastaken 与 @FlyingDuck，[#2031](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2031) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **Windows `/update` 引擎解析与回退修复** — 按「显式目录 → 正在运行的引擎（`argv[1]`）→ PATH → 模块回退」的顺序解析当前宿主，不再被旧 npx 缓存遗留的共享回退链接拦住升级；识别带常规 `dsh.cmd` 且不含项目 manifest/lock 的自定义 Windows `npm config set prefix` 目录；Windows 共享回退 junction 改为先建暂存链接再换位（失败可恢复旧链接）；`npm` 就地升级改为以目标 manifest 与 PATH 探测验证结果，profile 安装改为直接执行计划选定宿主的 `bin.dsh` 入口，不再被另一套 PATH 引擎否决。([#2029](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2029) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **全屏跨页拖选与滚回复制** — 按虚拟滚动实际绘制位移和 `boxDeltaY` 平移选区，修复快速滚轮 / `PgUp` / `PgDn` 及底部页眉卸载时的漏行与重复复制，同时修复反向滚回恢复与源码尾部硬换行保留。([#2043](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2043) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **`@` 模糊文件候选按需刷新** — 工作区扫描完成后 2 秒过期并在下次查询按需重扫，新建或删除文件无需重启即可出现在 `@` 补全中，同时复用在途与快速连续输入时的同一次扫描。([#2017](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2017) by @ccch1mneyyy 与 @Lucas2011wastaken) - **Linux 下用终端运行 `Terminal=true` 桌面处理器** — 本地文件通过 `xdg-mime` 解析默认 `.desktop` 条目，对 `Terminal=true` 的条目展开 `Exec` 字段码并在终端（依次尝试 `$TERMINAL`、KDE `TerminalApplication`、`xdg-terminal-exec` 与常见终端）中启动，不再继承无 TTY 标准输入输出而静默退出；找不到终端、参数不支持或启动失败时回退到 `xdg-open`。([#2053](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2053) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **点名被禁用的 Host 服务行** — profile 中提供 Host 服务的行被禁用导致 `agents.create` / `agents.resume` 失败时，诊断指出被禁用的行 ID、已装包版本与恢复顺序，并在启动 notice 中本地化提示 `/kernel`、`/quit` 与需要对齐的包名。（DSH `0.2.2+` 已将模型选择移至会话投影，该特定 `model-selection-settings` 路径仅在 `0.2.1` 及更早兼容线上触发；其他被禁用的 Host 服务仍适用。）([#1862](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1862) by @MomentDerek) - **更新 `dsh-working-activity` 至 `0.6.0`** — 将 `dsh-working-activity` 范围从 `^0.5.0` 升至 `^0.6.0`，对齐 DSH 0.2.x 与 React 19 peer 声明，消除自定义 profile 下 `pnpm peers check` 的未满足 peer 警告。([#1974](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1974) by @ccch1mneyyy) - **文档** — 精简 README 的通知概览，将投递预算、原生 toast 注册与回退、让位规则、tmux 配置及 `jumpBack` 机制说明归入用户指南。([#1941](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1941) by @baobaolaodie)  ## 兼容与升级  - **DSH 主适配线**：`0.2.2-rc.5`；`0.2.2-rc.4`、`0.2.2-rc.3`、`0.2.2-rc.2`、`0.2.2-rc.1`、`0.2.1-rc.5`、`0.2.1-rc.4`、`0.2.1-rc.3`、`0.2.1-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.1`、`0.1.7-rc.2`、`0.1.7-rc.1` 以及更早的验证线仍在契约内，契约外版本打漂移警告。 - **DSH 在 npm 上的 `latest` 仍是 `0.2.2-rc.1`**：`0.2.2-rc.5` 走 `next` 标签。程序内 `/update` 会在目标 TUI 的 peer 范围内选已发布的最高版本，因此会把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.5`。 - **从 v0.20.1 升级**：程序内 `/update`（本版会一并把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.5`、并对齐全局启动器），或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.20.2`。 - **`dst` 自动化 CLI 的验证线未同步前移**：仍验证到 `0.2.1-rc.5`；在 0.2.2 宿主上可用 `dst dsh` 原样转交官方参数。 - **嫡系插件清单**：随包的 `tui-profile/feed/first-party.json` 钉到 `0.20.2`。 - **DSH `0.2.2` 要求 Node `>=24.9`**：本包自身仍声明 `^22.19 || >=24`；用更旧的 Node 运行 `0.2.2` 宿主属于上游未支持组合，升级前请先确认 `node -v`。 - **Windows 离线安装器**：引擎验证范围随契约前移到 `0.2.2-rc.5`；已装更高的 RC 会降回验证线。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.20.2   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`（解压后 macOS / Linux 运行 `sh install.sh`，Windows 双击 `install.bat`） 或 Windows 离线安装程序 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.20.2 -->  ## What's Changed * docs(readme): summarize the notifications entry by @baobaolaodie in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1941 * fix(restart): replace the current process instead of retaining parents by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2024 * fix(restart): repair delegated updates and side-query cleanup by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2031 * fix(update): resolve the running engine before stale fallbacks (#2028) by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2029 * fix(selection): keep cross-page copy complete while scrolling by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2043 * https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1974 * feat(tool): render present deliveries as a delivery card by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2047 * fix(open): run ## Release 3.2.0+56  - `3b1f9a7` :wrench: Skip Flutter Gradle validation for Android CI build  Gradle stays on 8.14 since CargoKit (via super_context_menu) still uses Project.exec(), removed in Gradle 9 (super_native_extensions#565 open, no 0.9.2 release yet). CI's newer Flutter enforces a Gradle >= 9.3.1 minimum, so bypass the check until upstream ships a Gradle 9 fix. (LittleSheep) - `b7d02e5` :pushpin: Pin CI Flutter to 3.47.9  Flutter 3.50.0 stable ships the Flutter Gradle Plugin compiled against AGP 9.1.1: its bytecode calls the covariant ApplicationExtension. getDefaultConfig() that only exists in AGP 9, so it crashes against our AGP 8.12.1 (bypassing the Gradle gate with --android-skip-build- dependency-validation exposed this). 3.50.0 also requires Gradle 9.3.1, which CargoKit (super_context_menu 0.9.1) still can't build under.  3.47.9 supports Gradle 8.14 + AGP 8.12.1 natively and matches the local dev toolchain. Remove the skip flag again. Unpin once CargoKit ships a Gradle 9-compatible release. (LittleSheep) - `9c62f1c` :arrow_down: Downgrade flutter_local_notifications to 22.3.3  23.x requires compileSdk 37 and AGP 9.1.1 (androidx.core 1.19.0 AAR metadata), which conflicts with the AGP 8.12.1 pin forced by CargoKit. The ^23.0.2 bump slipped past the pin comment above it in :arrow_up: Upgrade deps. (LittleSheep) - `d03ff7d` :sparkles: Add a Ctrl+Shift mode for terminal copy, paste and select all  Closes #126.  - Added a Terminal settings switch between each renderer's platform   defaults (Plain) and Ctrl+Shift+C/V/A (Ctrl+Shift). Shifted mode   hands the plain Ctrl chords back to the remote shell, so screen's   prefix, Emacs bindings and Ctrl+C interrupts work on Windows and   Android instead of being claimed by select-all, copy and paste. - Persisted the choice as `terminal_shortcut_mode` and exposed it   through `terminalShortcutModeProvider`. - MaidTerm: answered the released chords from a HardwareKeyboard   handler, which runs ahead of the renderer's shortcut scope. - xterm3: answered them in the view's key wrapper.  Fixes #126 (LittleSheep) - `0c72ca5` :arrow_up: Bump Socommon packages to 8316e85 (LittleSheep) - `0d1491b` :rocket: Launch 3.2.0+56 (LittleSheep)  ---  | Artifact | Platform | Architecture | Size | Download | | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | | app-arm64-v8a-release.apk | android | arm64 | 67.0 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/7b9d5751-f109-450d-8b81-1f2a7d25b7fb/download) | | app-armeabi-v7a-release.apk | android | armeabi-v7a | 65.2 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/75ec5c76-2761-4267-94bc-08104c8ece18/download) | | app-x86_64-release.apk | android | x86_64 | 70.6 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/57ee4e03-9460-4efe-80a2-a0e91267d3d6/download) | | build-output-linux-appimage.zip | linux | amd64 | 55.3 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/769ea283-d5f2-4e4f-9cbc-ee6a4dbc74f7/download) | | build-output-windows-installer.zip | windows | amd64 | 33.6 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/1538cd85-885f-4f1b-91d4-6fa23b7e794b/download) |  ---  Powered by Solsynth Express®

 ### 新增 - **WebDAV 配置备份**：在「设置 → 同步」连接自己的 HTTPS WebDAV 服务，上传加密备份，查看和删除历史备份。 - **按需恢复**：可选择恢复主机、网络、主机指纹和偏好设置，支持合并或替换；恢复前预览变化，移除数据前再次确认。 - **备份加密**：使用独立的同步密码加密备份；恢复旧备份时可输入其原密码。  ### 优化 - **侧边导航**：平板与横屏下导航项靠上排列，宽屏自动展开文字；主界面改为点击切换标签，取消左右滑动。 - **宽屏排版**：列表与表单居中限宽，两侧空白处仍可上下滚动；外观页在宽屏横屏下改为左右双栏。 - **终端与弹窗**：底部快捷键、扩展面板与提示栏在宽屏下居中收窄，横屏挖孔两侧背景无缝延伸；下拉菜单贴右侧弹出，底部弹窗默认完整展开。  ### 使用说明 - 每次上传生成一份新备份，不自动同步。 - Tailscale 网络只恢复配置，不迁移登录状态；新设备需要自行登录。 - 请妥善保存同步密码；备份文件名中的设备名、时间和版本信息不加密。  ### 安装要求 - Android 11+、arm64-v8a。 - 使用 mosh 或 tmux 时，远端需安装相应程序。

本次更新继续完善 Pi 运行时、漏洞管理和前端交互体验。Pi 引擎新增兼容网关的按渠道 `store` 字段省略能力，兼容推理模型的 system role 处理，并明确报告未完成运行和模型流错误。漏洞列表新增时间戳、最早优先排序和独立分页；前端统一响应式卡片、详情抽屉与技能预览，并恢复历史时间线中的 Token 用量展示。同时修复已读长任务通知仍显示的问题，改进任务及 Linux 进程组清理逻辑，区分被阻断的清理过程与真实执行失败，并补充 UI 设计规范、测试和双语文档。  ---  This update further improves the Pi runtime, vulnerability management, and frontend experience. Pi now supports channel-specific omission of the `store` field for compatible gateways, preserves the system role for compatible reasoning models, and reports incomplete runs and model stream failures clearly. Vulnerability browsing adds timestamps, oldest-first sorting, and independent pagination. The frontend now uses unified responsive cards, detail drawers, and shared skill previews, while restored timelines display token usage correctly. The release also fixes long-running notifications that remained visible after being marked as read, strengthens task and Linux process-group cleanup, distinguishes blocked cleanup from genuine execution failures, and adds UI design guidance, tests, and bilingual documentation. 
```

### 2026-11-21T08:22:24Z [**SubBoost/subboost**](https://github.com/SubBoost/subboost)

# SubBoost v2.12.0  ## 中文  - 新增 Tailscale 节点配置与表单编辑，可在订阅源中单独管理 Tailscale 节点。认证密钥会随订阅配置传递，请妥善保管订阅链接。 - 改善规则和代理组编辑，修复移动、停用及重命名后的规则目标、顺序和引用保持问题。 - 首页模板选择支持“我的模板”，修复应用模板后覆盖正在编辑的订阅信息的问题。 - 完善未保存内容提醒，并改善短屏弹窗和长节点列表的操作体验。 - 改善自部署更新与访问地址处理，更新时保留自定义端口绑定。 - 加强登录、订阅解析和规则获取的安全性，并更新受影响的依赖。  升级说明：建议先备份现有实例，再通过 `subboost update` 更新。  ## English  - Added Tailscale node configuration and form editing, with separate management of Tailscale nodes within subscription sources. Authentication keys are included in subscription configurations, so keep subscription links private. - Improved rule and proxy group editing, fixing target, order, and reference preservation after moving, disabling, or renaming entries. - Added “My Templates” to the home template picker and fixed applied templates overwriting information for the subscription being edited. - Improved unsaved input warnings and made dialogs on short screens and long node lists easier to use. - Improved self-hosted updates and access URL handling, preserving custom port bindings during updates. - Strengthened login, subscription parsing, and rule fetching security, and updated affected dependencies.  Upgrade: Back up the existing instance, then update with `subboost update`. 
```

### 2026-11-22T09:04:13Z [**Gloridust/WechatOnCloud**](https://github.com/Gloridust/WechatOnCloud)

```
## ⬆️ 升级方式（重要）  1. 面板「检查更新」把面板升到 1.7.1（在 `.env` 里固定了 `WOC_VERSION` 的，改成 `1.7.1` 后执行 `docker compose pull && docker compose up -d`）； 2. **再点「一键升级全部实例」**：「停止时应用先正常退出」在实例镜像里，**只更新面板不会完整生效**。其余修复在面板里，升级面板即可。  升级实例会保留全部数据（登录状态、聊天记录、设备标识）。  ---  ## 🐛 修复  ### 重启、升级实例时微信 / Chromium 被直接杀掉 以前面板重启、升级、自愈重建实例时，是直接把容器强制删掉的，里面的应用来不及正常退出：下次打开 Chromium 会提示「未正确关闭」，微信同样是被直接杀掉。重启实例、升级、卡死自愈、重置设备标识都会经过这一步。现在先正常停止容器（最多等 15 秒），再重建。  同时修了实例镜像里的停止顺序：原来停止时远程桌面服务（Xvnc）可能先于应用退出，应用因为显示突然消失而崩溃。现在应用先正常退出，Xvnc 后停。  ### `WOC_DATA_ROOT` 填错时被悄悄忽略（#133） 实例数据目录填了面板用不了的路径（相对路径、含空格或中文、目录不可用，以及 1.7.0 及以前在 Windows 上填 `E:/woc` 这类盘符路径）时，以前新实例照旧建在 Docker 卷里，面板和卡片上都没有任何提示，看起来就是「设置了却没效果」。现在新建实例会在「准备数据目录」一步失败，卡片上写明原因和正确写法，改好后点「重试创建」即可。已有实例不受影响。  Windows Docker Desktop 用户可以直接填 `E:/woc` 这样的盘符路径，面板会自动换算成 Docker Desktop 里该盘的实际路径；如果提示找不到该盘，到 Docker Desktop 的 Settings → Resources → File sharing 里共享这个盘。这一项还没在真实 Windows 上验证过，遇到问题请在 #133 反馈。  ---  ## 🧪 测试  - 新增发布门禁脚本 `scripts/gate/`：实例镜像探针（输入法、剪贴板、X 显示锁、窗口规则等）和面板端到端回归（创建、上传、剪贴板图片、重启时应用正常退出、数据保留、删除清理、`WOC_DATA_ROOT` 填错）。 - PR 和 main 推送时自动在 amd64、arm64 两个架构上跑这些检查。此前 PR 没有任何自动检查，1.6.0 的 #175 就是发出去后才被发现的。  ---  ## ↩️ 回滚  出现问题时可回到 1.7.0：在 `.env` 里设 `WOC_VERSION=1.7.0`，执行 `docker compose up -d`，再在面板里「一键升级全部实例」（实例切回 1.7.0 镜像，数据保留）。  镜像：`ghcr.io/gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.7.1`、`gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.7.1`（amd64 + arm64）。 
```

### 2026-11-23T13:31:20Z [**AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm**](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm)

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.4.1 重做了新建连接表单，改进了 AI 的多轮工具调用和提示缓存，并让活动会话和会话管理侧栏支持多选批量删除。  #### 🔌 连接与会话管理  - **新建连接表单重做**：顶部协议栏加单开分类抽屉，基础字段按名称/分组与主机/端口/用户对齐，测试操作与反馈移到底部。隧道、SFTP 和 FTP 表单沿用同一布局，SSH 隧道表单调换了连接弹窗的轴向并重新设计。SSH 与 BLE 连接进度改为分阶段信号轨，SSH 算法在独立页面编辑，SFTP 独立端点和路由在表单中归组。 - **批量添加与跳板机编辑**：批量添加弹窗重新设计，批量预览仅在本地生成，跳板机编辑更顺手；多语言下表单不再截断。 - **多选批量删除**：活动会话和会话管理侧栏支持单击、Ctrl 切换、Shift 范围选择，批量删除会先断开在线节点，并提示被跳过的数量；折叠分组内的隐藏成员不会被 Shift 范围选中。终端标签右键菜单提供 SSH 节点操作。（PR [#821](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/821)、[#848](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/848)） - **已关闭会话清理与空闲连接回收**：已关闭的会话按保留策略清理，空闲的 SSH 连接会被回收。（PR [#843](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/843)） - **SSH 重连**：多服务器分屏重连不再被全局多跳锁阻塞，已关闭或过期的连接句柄不再复用，物理传输断开时会如实报告；跳板链串行建立，冲突的重连会延后；MCP 租用目标前会等待跳板链就绪。 - **RDP 与 VNC**：支持保存并同步显式的空密码；修复 Windows 上剪贴板文件列表发布与重复复制的识别。 - **标签页**：分离或折叠合并标签后恢复终端标题与图标。  #### 🤖 AI  - **工具调用的上下文不再丢失**：多轮工具调用会按发送给提供方的原样重放，OpenAI 兼容、Anthropic 和 Gemini 请求都保留工具轮次；子智能体与摘要轮次共用相同的工具定义，权限在执行时检查，摘要期间不会执行工具。 - **更稳定的提示缓存**：各提供方的提示前缀保持稳定，系统提示与工具说明去除了重复内容，请求体更短，缓存更容易命中。 - **缓存用量可见**：上下文弹窗新增“提示缓存”区域，显示上一次请求和本会话累计的命中率（缓存读取 / 输入 token），以及缓存写入量。预计的下次输入量以上次实际用量为基准，保留输出改为显示 token 数。 - **崩溃修复**：刷新模型列表时设置页不再崩溃。  #### ⚡ 终端  - **渲染**：增强终端渲染和语义着色，行高按像素对齐；macOS Metal 增加深色背景的字形边缘补偿；数字读数启用 OpenType 等宽数字；可选的 OLED 泛光和 CRT 效果覆盖各 GPU 后端；Windows 与 wgpu 复合着色器改用矢量边界；修复 Windows 丢失 DirectX 渲染器后的恢复。 - **提示与标签**：可按连接配置视觉响铃，并高亮后台标签。 - **交互修复**：拖选时鼠标移出窗口会正确结束选择；右键菜单从输出区域点击时，“上一条命令”会跳回该输出所属的命令；终端补充 Windows 与 Linux 的 emoji 字体回退；未启用 shell 集成时，`df`/`free` 输出按列着色；本地 shell 的命令历史绑定到各自使用的文件。（emoji、拖选与着色：PR [#840](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/840)） - **Zen 模式**：可选择保留标签栏，退出按钮不再被标签遮挡。（PR [#839](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/839)）  #### 🛠️ 设置与其他  - **设置**：打开搜索结果后保持查询和结果可见。 - **布局**：菜单、分段控件和选择器按各语言文本自适应宽度；主机工具的紧凑表格不再截断主标签；快捷命令编辑器字段不再溢出，翻译补齐，命令首尾空格得以保留。 - **Markdown**：文档排版、代码高亮和中日韩图表有所改进。 - **桌面**：遵循窗口按钮布局，说明 macOS 登录项；记录发布版崩溃并支持重载桌面日志过滤。 - **依赖与构建**：清理未使用依赖，更新 RustSec 公告涉及的锁定版本；调整 macOS Metal 着色器源码的解析方式；Windows 便携压缩包列入发布说明。（PR [#819](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/819)、[#820](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/820)）  #### 👥 贡献者  - 感谢 @allegrox 实现活动会话侧栏的本地多选与批量删除（PR [#848](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/848)）。 - 感谢 @BilityLeu 实现已关闭会话保留与空闲 SSH 连接回收，以及 Zen 模式保留标签栏（PR [#843](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/843)、[#839](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/839)）。 - 感谢 @backkoom 完善终端拖选收尾、`df`/`free` 着色和 emoji 字体回退（PR [#840](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/840)）。 - 感谢 @moro-s 实现会话节点批量移除与侧栏多选删除（PR [#821](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/821)）。 - 感谢 @HarpeLm 清理未使用依赖并更新 RustSec 公告涉及的依赖（PR [#819](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/819)、[#820](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/820)）。  ### English  OxideTerm 2.4.1 rebuilds the new-connection form, improves AI multi-turn tool calls and prompt caching, and adds multi-select batch removal in Active Sessions and the session manager sidebar.  #### 🔌 Connections and Session Manager  - **Rebuilt new-connection form**: A protocol rail at the top with single-open category drawers, basic fields aligned as name/group and host/port/user rows, and test actions and feedback in a footer. Tunnel, SFTP, and FTP forms share the layout, and the SSH tunnel form is redesigned with the connection modal axes swapped. SSH and BLE progress shows on a staged signal track, SSH algorithms are edited on a dedicated page, and standalone SFTP endpoints and routes are grouped in the form. - **Batch add and jump hosts**: The batch add modal is redesigned, batch previews stay local, and jump-host editing is easier. Forms no longer truncate in any language. - **Multi-select batch removal**: Active Sessions and the session manager sidebar support click, Ctrl-toggle, and Shift-range selection. Batch removal disconnects live nodes first and reports how many were skipped; hidden members of collapsed groups are not included in a Shift range. The terminal tab context menu offers SSH node actions. (PR [#821](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/821), [#848](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/848)) - **Closed-session cleanup and idle connection retirement**: Closed sessions are cleaned up under a retention policy and idle SSH connections are retired. (PR [#843](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/843)) - **SSH reconnects**: Reconnecting split panes on several servers no longer waits on the global multi-hop lock, closed or stale connection handles are not reused, and transport loss is reported when a shell exits on a closed physical transport. Jump chains are built one at a time, conflicting reconnects are deferred, and MCP waits for the jump chain before leasing its target. - **RDP and VNC**: Explicit empty passwords can be saved and synced, and Windows clipboard file lists are published and repeated copies recognized. - **Tabs**: Terminal title and icon are restored when detaching or collapsing merged tabs.  #### 🤖 AI  - **Tool-call context is preserved**: Multi-turn tool calls replay exactly as sent to the provider, and OpenAI-compatible, Anthropic, and Gemini requests keep their tool turns. Child agents and summary turns share the same tool definitions, permissions are checked at execution, and no tools run during a summary. - **More stable prompt caching**: Provider prompt prefixes stay stable, and duplicated system prompt and tool descriptions were removed, so requests are shorter and caches hit more often. - **Cache usage is visible**: The context popover has a new Prompt Cache section with the hit rate (cache reads / input tokens) for the last request and the session total, plus cache writes. The next-input estimate is anchored to the last actual usage, and reserved output now shows tokens. - **Crash fix**: The settings page no longer crashes when refreshing the model list.  #### ⚡ Terminal  - **Rendering**: Improved terminal rendering and semantic coloring with pixel-snapped line height; Metal dark-background glyph edge compensation on macOS; OpenType tabular numerals for numeric readouts; optional OLED bloom and CRT effects across GPU backends; vector bounds in the Windows and wgpu composite shaders; and recovery after Windows loses a DirectX renderer. - **Bell and tabs**: Visual bells can be configured per connection, and background tabs can be highlighted. - **Interaction fixes**: A drag selection ends when the pointer leaves the window. Right-clicking inside command output makes “Previous command” jump to the command that owns it. Windows and Linux emoji fonts join the fallback chain, `df`/`free` output is colored by column without shell integration, and each local shell's command history is bound to the file it uses. (Emoji, drag selection, and coloring: PR [#840](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/840)) - **Zen Mode**: The tab bar can stay visible, and the exit button no longer overlaps tabs. (PR [#839](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/839))  #### 🛠️ Settings and more  - **Settings**: Search results and the query stay visible after opening a match. - **Layout**: Menus, segmented controls, and selects size to each language's text. Compact host-tool tables no longer truncate primary labels, and quick-command editor fields no longer overflow, with translations completed and leading and trailing spaces in commands preserved. - **Markdown**: Improved document layout, code highlighting, and CJK diagrams. - **Desktop**: Respects the window-button layout, clarifies macOS login items, records release-build panics, and reloads desktop log filters. - **Dependencies and build**: Removed unused dependencies and updated locked versions for RustSec advisories, changed how macOS Metal shader sources are resolved, and listed Windows portable archives in the release notes. (PR [#819](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/819), [#820](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/820))  #### 👥 Contributors  Thank you to the external contributors to this release:  - @allegrox: local multi-select and batch removal in the Active Sessions sidebar (PR [#848](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/848)). - @BilityLeu: closed-session retention with idle SSH connection cleanup, and an optional tab bar in Zen Mode (PR [#843](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/843), [#839](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/839)). - @backkoom: drag-selection cleanup, `df`/`free` coloring, and emoji font fallbacks (PR [#840](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/840)). - @moro-s: batch removal of session nodes and multi-select in the sidebar (PR [#821](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/821)). - @HarpeLm: removed unused dependencies and updated dependencies for RustSec advisories (PR [#819](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/819), [#820](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/820)).  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_windows_x64-setup.exe) · [Portable (.zip)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_windows_x64_portable.zip) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_windows_arm64-setup.exe) · [Portable (.zip)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_windows_arm64_portable.zip) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.4.1/OxideTerm_2.4.1_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Additional portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md

([5297383](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/commit/52973837ece9ad2b6d3142cac9b5a3ed8c80c602)) * **cargo:** narrow prefix routing and pin integration behavior ([17f5fdd](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/commit/17f5fdda4474f3521e6c66c58560177a4ae78083)) * **copilot:** let the repository's Copilot hook run without rtk installed ### 2026-11-24T09:48:11Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 新功能  - 代码片段支持导入和导出 JSON 文件 (contributed by @AndreasK79) (PR [#15554](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15554)) - SQL 编辑器支持双击方括号或花括号选中括号内的内容 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#15589](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15589)) - SQL 编辑器在光标位于嵌套语句内时按层级提供「执行当前层语句」候选 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#15513](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15513)) - Shift+Delete / Ctrl+X 可剪切文档最后一行，行为与主流编辑器一致 (contributed by @AndreasK79) (PR [#15553](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15553)) - 支持跳转到上次编辑位置 (contributed by @chukui2005) (PR [#15493](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15493)) - 网格右键菜单可直接打开 BLOB 文本的预览 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#15538](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15538)) - Redis Stream 支持从最新条目开始倒序浏览 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#15537](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15537)) - Web 版 MySQL 连接可配置是否显示系统数据库 (contributed by @zhwq1216) (PR [#12582](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12582)) - 多个本地 SQL 文件可批量设置数据源 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#15543](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15543))  ### 改进  - GaussDB 非 DWS 模式下从 pg_settings 读取并显示真实产品版本号 (contributed by @AndrewDi) (PR [#15570](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15570)) - 数据库导出的 SQL 语句按表分组，便于按表执行和回滚 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#15521](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15521))  ### 修复  - 表结构编辑器的下拉框不再因网格刷新意外关闭，滚动条悬停加粗更易辨认 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#15595](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15595)) - PostgreSQL 数据传输重建表时不再因目标库存在同名索引或约束误报表不存在，避免级联误报 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#15337](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15337)) - Doris 元数据查询避开不兼容的 information_schema 列并缓存 MySQL 兼容视图定义 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#15541](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15541)) - Db2 目录注释存在无法解码的字符时不再导致读取失败，写入路径不受影响 (contributed by @bigdogcome) (PR [#15567](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15567)) - MCP 插件工具调用遵循连接保存的传输层配置，不再回退直连 (contributed by @forthedreamhy) (PR [#15549](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15549)) - MySQL 修改预览的 SET 子句读取同一语句中更早的赋值时拒绝生成预览，避免展示错误的前后对比 (contributed by @thejesh23) (PR [#15545](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15545)) 
```

### 2026-11-22T07:10:51Z [**JuliusBrussee/caveman**](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman)

```
Caveman 4.2.0 adds a QuickJS Code-Mode MCP gateway (`caveman codemode` / `caveman wrap --codemode`) with multi-agent overlays, multi-harness tool-envelope unwrapping in the compression engine, cache-safe proxy optimizations (local turn-effort gating, duplicate-read collapsing, canonical tool ordering, and `defer_loading`), `@caveman-ai/middleware/codemode`, opt-in Code-Mode support in `@caveman-ai/pi` 0.4.0, and a native Codex CLI provider for `caveman-compress`.  ### New  **QuickJS Code-Mode MCP gateway** (#1559) - **`caveman-mcp codemode` and `caveman codemode`** collapse large upstream stdio and HTTP MCP catalogs into a static, prefix-cache-safe set of Code-Mode tools (`codemode_search_tools`, `codemode_execute`, `codemode_mutate`) backed by an embedded pure-Go QuickJS sandbox (`modernc.org/quickjs`) plus built-in repo and PR helpers (`batch_read`, `symbol_outline`, `pr_read`, `ci_logs`). - **`caveman wrap --codemode`** installs temporary per-session Code-Mode overlays for Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Gemini CLI, Qwen Code, Kilo, OpenClaw, Pi, and Hermes (including `config.yaml` sync-back of in-session Hermes edits; Cursor MCP configs are discovered by `caveman codemode`). - **Workspace MCP discovery is explicit:** workspace-local MCP definitions are loaded only when `--trust-workspace-mcp` or `CAVEMAN_TRUST_WORKSPACE_MCP=1` is set. - **Exact recovery and non-text fidelity:** Code-Mode execution outputs and built-in tools store originals in CCR before elision; upstream HTTP and stdio MCP decoding preserves integers above `2^53` and non-text attachment blocks (images, audio, resources), streams HTTP SSE replies incrementally, and strips auth/custom headers across cross-origin redirects.  **Cache-safe proxy optimizations** (#1559) - **`--turn-effort`** applies cache-safe per-turn effort gating across Anthropic (including Opus 4.7 / adaptive reasoning models), OpenAI/Codex, and Gemini without breaking prefix caches, replaying Codex/ChatGPT effort marks across substantive turns. - **Duplicate-read collapsing**, **canonical `tools[]` ordering (`--canonical-tools`)**, **MCP tool deferral (`--defer-mcp-tools`)**, and **tool catalog shrinking (`--shrink-tools`)** keep provider prompt prefixes byte-stable while trimming redundant input context. MCP-disabled streaming OpenAI Responses requests remain byte-identical.  **Multi-harness engine envelopes** (#1559) - The compression engine unwraps tool-result envelopes emitted by Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, and Hermes so code, search results, and test reports compress cleanly across harnesses while preserving Go build and setup failure diagnostics in verbose `go test` output.  **Framework middleware and Pi** (#1559) - **`@caveman-ai/middleware` 1.2.0** adds `@caveman-ai/middleware/codemode` (`createCodeModeToolset`), a zero-dependency in-process Code-Mode toolset with BM25 discovery, structured pipelines, and read/write separation. - **`@caveman-ai/pi` 0.4.0** adds opt-in Code-Mode tools (`CAVEMAN_PI_CODEMODE=1`) backed by a lazy `CodemodeClient`, honors `CAVEMAN_MCP_PREFIX_ARGS`, and extends RPC timeouts past caller script deadlines.  **caveman-compress** - Native Codex CLI provider for `caveman-compress`, isolated from local tools (#1427 by @danez).  ### Fixed  - **Engine and proxy:** guard `unwrapFileWrapper` against whitespace-only input; recover per segment so an engine panic fails open while still re-raising `http.ErrAbortHandler` (#1518). - **OpenCode:** preserve whitespace in configured `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` (#1518). - **CLI:** remove stale `dev` and `deploy aws` commands that referenced removed targets. - **Python SDK:** restore the TLS byte budget after HTTP `CONNECT` reads (#1502).  ### Maintenance  - Go toolchain bumped to Go 1.26.11 (#1532). - Documentation consolidated around `AGENTS.md` and `docs/WHY.md`, with public benchmark sample sizes stated alongside published figures. - Binaries: `bin-v4.2.0`. CLI: `@caveman-ai/cli` 4.2.0. Middleware: `@caveman-ai/middleware` 1.2.0. Pi: `@caveman-ai/pi` 0.4.0. 
```

([0c0a233](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/0c0a2339ce88d47f3bfece089104d5fe76126c5f)) - **MD077**: keep the fix from turning paragraph text into a block ([d97c8e0](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/d97c8e0ae6df00dfede4e2dd73bcfab3d15ea1c6)) - **tables**: detect a table whose header row opens with a code span ([6c441a5](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/6c441a525d2546e4fd4ac301b8556800915845b1)) - **tables**: keep the list context across a lazy continuation line ([61c894e](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/61c894ed358b3790cf4d36261569a4177702e938)) - **MD077**: align a continuation line that opens with a code span ([94766cc](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/94766cc81eeedd350335314a8eec459ca962d880)) - **deps**: refresh Rust dependencies for v0.3.6 ([c75c611](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/c75c611b41e2661971114b123c90b8959539e47b))  ### Performance  - **lsp**: convert the ranges of a quick fix in one pass ([5fbdd19](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/5fbdd190b77aa57767b74dbbf1ef87ec1000a8ce)) - **tables**: scan a lazy line without walking every open list level ([cbeb9c0](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/cbeb9c06d31e58b579cbf84eaefa10c2ec620177))  ### Security  - Update `url` to 2.5.9 to address # GhidraMCP Release 7.1.0  Production-ready Model Context Protocol server for Ghidra reverse engineering platform.  ## By the Numbers  | Metric | Value | |--------|-------| | **MCP Tools** | 220 | | **GUI Endpoints** | 216 | | **Headless Endpoints** | 201 | | **Java** | 25 LTS | | **Ghidra** | 12.2 |  ## Installation  1. Download **GhidraMCP-7.1.0.zip** below 2. In Ghidra: **File > Install Extensions > Add** and select the ZIP 3. Restart Ghidra, enable plugin: **File > Configure > Configure All Plugins > GhidraMCP** 4. Start server: **Tools > GhidraMCP > Start MCP Server** 5. Install Python bridge: `uv tool install ./ghidra_mcp_bridge-7.1.0-py3-none-any.whl` (or `pip install` the same wheel) 6. Run: `bridge-mcp-ghidra` (or `python -m bridge_mcp_ghidra`)  ## What's Included  - **GhidraMCP-7.1.0.zip** — Ghidra extension (GUI plugin) - **ghidra_mcp_bridge-7.1.0-py3-none-any.whl** — MCP protocol bridge wheel (220 tools)  See [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/bethington/ghidra-mcp/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) for detailed release notes. See [README.md](https://github.com/bethington/ghidra-mcp#readme) for full documentation. 
```

### 2026-11-20T16:25:01Z [**lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager**](https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager)

> Evolving from v5.0.3 to v5.0.4 through 20 preview releases, this version delivers a comprehensive refactor of streaming zero-tampering contracts, stealth sideloading and anti-degradation architectures, seamless official Hub & IDE parity, and a resilient scheduling/self-healing core:  -   **Streaming & Thinking Resilience**: Canonical `finishReason: "STOP"` and `[DONE]` frames for thought-only streams with zero content tampering, preserving real token usage; precise streaming truncation semantics preserving `MAX_TOKENS` and adding model-family boundaries for tier promotion; thinking cache corruption protection; Claude/Gemini thought folding and discriminated object union merging (Fixes #4008, #4006, #3997, PR #4007, #4005, #3878, #3866, #3867). -   **WAF Evasion & Prompt Engineering**: Injected `[SYSTEM MANDATE]` authority anchor to completely eliminate model instruction decay, rule forgetting, and blind `ls -R` under stealth sideloading; neutralized upstream WAF pseudo-429 triggers across Pi agent codemode, WorkBuddy, Codex, and DSH; introduced fine-grained rule scope selectors (`all` / `prompt` / `tools`) (Ref #3997, Fixes #3981, #3982, #3983, PR #3986). -   **Ecosystem Parity & Protocol Robustness**: Official Hub baseline upgraded to 2.33.0 (CL 1023160026) with local binary dynamic extraction; Antigravity IDE dual-write seamless hot-switch and onboarding terms suppression; Gemini 4+ dynamic codename extraction with Neon quota UI migration; enforced `type: array` on nested tool schemas (Fixes #3998, #4001, #3745, #3842). -   **Scheduler Core & Quota Self-Healing**: CacheFirst sticky session deadlock prevention via auto-unbinding and pool failover; mutual exclusion between capacity exhaustion and budget degradation; cache-settling delay grace window maximizing long-context prefix reuse; per-model-family account priority scheduling and 403 authority convergence (Fixes #4002, #4000, #3991, #3863, #3875, PR #3879, #3881, #3882). -   **Operations & Multi-Platform**: 403 recovery visibility gate and transparent probe error surfacing; warmup confirmation transparency; dual-track updates with Homebrew switching and instant channel linkage; Windows deadlock resolution and Termux Android ARM64 support (Ref #3995, #4009, Fixes #3993, #3818, PR #4010, #3996).  ---  > 从 v5.0.3 演进至 v5.0.4，历经 20 个预览版持续迭代，系统完成了流式响应零篡改规范重构、WAF 隐身侧载与防降智架构升级、官方 Hub 与 IDE 生态无感对齐及调度自愈内核加固：  -   **流式响应与思考协议**: 纯思考空回复遵循零正文篡改规范输出 `finishReason: "STOP"` 与 `[DONE]` 并保全 Token 用量；流式思考截断判定精准收敛至异常中断，透传 `MAX_TOKENS` 等正常终止，增加高容量提档模型系列边界守卫；思考缓存防污染；支持 Claude/Gemini 思考块折叠与 Schema 联合类型智能合并 (Fixes #4008, #4006, #3997, PR #4007, #4005, #3878, #3866, #3867)。 -   **WAF 防护与提示词工程**: 注入 `[SYSTEM MANDATE]` 权威执行锚点，彻底消除长提示词隐身侧载下的模型降智与规则遗忘；全面中和 Pi agent codemode、WorkBuddy、Codex 与 DSH 触发的 WAF 伪 429 风控；支持规则细粒度生效范围（`all` / `prompt` / `tools`）(Ref #3997, Fixes #3981, #3982, #3983, PR #3986)。 -   **生态兼容与协议对齐**: 官方 Hub 指纹基准升级至 2.33.0 (CL 1023160026) 并支持本地 binary 动态同源提取；Antigravity IDE 支持凭据双写无感热切号与条款声明免弹窗；动态提取 Gemini 4+ 代号并迁移 Neon 配额；补齐工具嵌套数组 `type: array` 声明 (Fixes #3998, #4001, #3745, #3842)。 -   **调度内核与配额自愈**: 粘性会话原地重试耗尽防死锁解绑与多号池故障转移；容量枯竭与思考降级互斥隔离；长文本 KV 缓存就绪防竞争延迟；支持 Gemini / Claude 分模型族独立优先级调度与 403 恢复权威收敛 (Fixes #4002, #4000, #3991, #3863, #3875, PR #3879, #3881, #3882)。 -   **运维与多端适配**: 修复 403 解限门禁并透出真实测活错误；预热机制弹窗说明与 Token 提示；在线更新与 Homebrew 双轨自由切换与通道即时联动；修复 Windows 死锁并支持 Termux Android ARM64 (Ref #3995, #4009, Fixes #3993, #3818, PR #4010, #3996)。  ## What's Changed * fix(docker): 修复 ### 2026-11-23T17:18:44Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)

```
**新增功能与改进**  1. Windows、macOS 和 Linux 桌面端支持双向复制粘贴文件。在本机或远端的文件管理器中复制文件，到另一端粘贴时才开始传输。接收端可查看进度或取消传输。 2. 每台远程设备都有独立的控制窗口，可同时查看多台电脑。重新连接时会恢复该设备上次的窗口大小。macOS 上的每个控制窗口也可进入独立的全屏空间，并可从全屏工具栏最小化。 3. Android、iOS 和鸿蒙支持 IP 直连。在手机或平板上输入电脑的 IP 地址和端口，即可通过局域网或 VPN 连接。直连时可在网络状态中查看两端地址。 4. 移动端支持可信设备配对。在「设置 → 本机配对信息」中复制配对信息，添加到被控电脑并核对指纹后，即可使用连接密码访问开启「仅允许可信设备控制」的电脑。 5. 移动端新增适配 Windows、macOS 和 Linux 的快捷键面板，支持自定义、排序和固定常用组合键。最近连接中的设备也可设置别名并分组。 6. 改善移动端双指缩放的响应速度，虚拟鼠标可独立拖动。放大画面后，视野会随光标移动。打开键盘时，将光标移到键盘上沿即可查看被遮住的内容。 7. 桌面端可调整鼠标指针和滚轮灵敏度，滚轮设置按远程设备保存。Windows 和 macOS 新增纯黑隐私屏，远控悬浮按钮在空闲三秒后淡出并收起。 8. 被控电脑可从主窗口工具栏查看当前连接的控制端，向指定设备发送文件或断开连接。桌面端在安装或升级后首次启动时会显示本版更新内容和赞助信息。 9. 缩短视频帧从解码到显示的等待时间，改善中继连接的画质稳定性，以及丢包后的画面恢复。  **问题修复**  1. 修复 macOS 屏幕采集中断后画面可能无法恢复、当前 Retina 分辨率未出现在选项中，以及关闭会话状态栏时可能崩溃的问题。 2. 修复 Windows 在采集尺寸变化后读取画面异常的问题。Linux X11 在显示器恢复后可继续采集，并减少采集失败时的重复重试。 3. 修正部分 SDR 画面的色偏和明暗异常，改善 Windows 硬件编码兼容性及视频解码中断后的恢复。 4. 修正远控时的 Shift 键状态、macOS 大写锁定键和 ISO 键位映射，改善 macOS 触控板和 Windows 高精度滚轮的小幅滚动。 5. 修复 Android 和 iOS 使用输入法时已确认文字可能丢失，以及 Android 重新打开键盘后无法继续输入的问题。 6. 修复移动端重复发起连接、iOS 自定义服务器设置未保存，以及 Android 和鸿蒙在多会话下未将剪贴板文本发送到当前设备的问题。 7. 修复信令重试可能停滞、Windows 便携版对较长 Unicode 路径处理异常，以及部分 Linux 安装包因界面运行库版本不匹配而无法启动的问题。  **升级说明**  1. 使用文件复制粘贴时，请将两端桌面客户端都升级到 **1.7.7**。每次最多可复制 **128 个普通文件**，文件夹可通过双栏文件传输窗口发送。Linux 接收端需要 FUSE 2 或 FUSE 3 及挂载权限。 2. Intel 和 Apple Silicon 安装包均要求 **macOS 14.0 或更高版本**。本版安装程序会在安装前检查系统版本。  ---  **New Features and Improvements**  1. Copy and paste files in either direction between Windows, macOS and Linux desktops. Copy files in one computer’s file manager and paste them on the other. The transfer starts when you paste, and the receiving side can track progress or cancel. 2. Each remote device opens in its own control window, so you can view several computers at once. Reconnecting restores the last window size for that device. On macOS, each window can use its own full-screen Space and can be minimized from the full-screen toolbar. 3. Android, iOS and HarmonyOS can now connect directly to a computer by IP address and port over a LAN or VPN. Network Status can show both endpoints of a direct connection. 4. Mobile devices can now pair with computers that require trusted controllers. Copy the pairing information from Settings, add it on the host and verify the fingerprint. Paired devices still use the connection password. 5. The mobile shortcut panel includes presets for Windows, macOS and Linux. You can create, reorder and pin key combinations, and organize recent devices with aliases and groups. 6. Pinch-to-zoom responds faster on mobile, and the virtual mouse can be repositioned independently. The view follows the pointer when zoomed in. With the keyboard open, move the pointer to its top edge to reveal content behind it. 7. Desktop controllers now offer pointer and scroll sensitivity controls and remember scroll sensitivity per remote device. Windows and macOS also offer a solid black privacy screen. The floating control button fades and retracts after three seconds of inactivity. 8. A toolbar menu on the host lists connected controllers. Select one to send files or disconnect it. The desktop client also shows release notes and sponsors on the first launch after installation or upgrade. 9. Reduced the delay between video decoding and display, improved video quality stability over relay connections, and improved picture recovery after packet loss.  **Bug Fixes**  1. Fixed macOS screen capture failing to resume after an interruption, the active Retina resolution missing from display options, and a potential crash when closing the session status bar. 2. Fixed Windows capture errors when the captured surface changes size. Linux X11 capture now resumes when a display becomes available again and backs off between failed attempts. 3. Corrected color casts and brightness errors in some SDR video. Improved Windows hardware encoding compatibility and recovery after decoding interruptions. 4. Fixed remote Shift key handling, macOS Caps Lock behavior and ISO key mapping. Small scroll movements now register more reliably on macOS trackpads and high-resolution Windows mouse wheels. 5. Fixed cases where Android and iOS input methods lost committed text, and where reopening the Android keyboard prevented further input. 6. Fixed duplicate mobile connection attempts, custom server settings failing to persist on iOS, and clipboard text failing to reach the active device on Android and HarmonyOS. 7. Fixed stalled signaling retries, long Unicode path handling in Windows portable builds, and Linux startup failures caused by a mismatched UI runtime in some packages.  **Upgrade Notes**  1. Upgrade both desktop clients to **1.7.7** to copy and paste files. You can copy up to **128 regular files** at a time. Use the dual-pane file transfer window for folders. Receiving pasted files on Linux requires FUSE 2 or FUSE 3 and permission to mount a FUSE filesystem. 2. The Intel and Apple Silicon installers require **macOS 14.0 or later** and now check the system version before installation.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog** · [[v1.7.6-20261112…v1.7.7](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.6-20261112...v1.7.7)](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.6-20261112...v1.7.7) 
```

### 2026-11-24T19:55:16Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v2.2.0  **OpenSpec now has a skill router.** Type `/openspec`, say what you want, and the router picks the right OpenSpec skill and runs it.  ## How to upgrade  Run `openspec update`. The update adds the `openspec` router skill and changes no other skill.  ### New: the `/openspec` skill router  - **One command for everything** - `/openspec` accepts any OpenSpec request in plain words. In Codex the command is `$openspec`. - **The router picks the skill** - You no longer need to know which of the 12 workflow skills to call. - **The router answers questions** - Ask "where does add-auth stand?" and the router checks your project, then tells you the status and the next step. - **The router runs several steps in order** - Ask "verify add-auth, then archive it" and the router runs both skills, pausing wherever a step needs your approval. - **Every existing skill still works** - `/openspec-propose` and the other 11 skills keep their names, their tab completion and their behavior. - **Every profile gets the router** - Core and custom profiles both install the router, and `openspec config profile` does not list it because the router is not a workflow you choose.  ### New: reference files for skills  - **A skill can now ship a `references/` folder** - The folder sits next to `SKILL.md` and holds detail the agent opens only when a task needs it. - **The router is the first skill with references** - `openspec init` and `openspec update` install, refresh and remove reference files together with their skill.  ### Improved  - **`openspec update` repairs reference files** - A reference file that was deleted or edited is restored on the next `openspec update`.  ### Fixed  - **Windows install no longer fails on a locked file** - The install script now retries for up to five seconds when Windows still has the new `openspec.exe` locked.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v2.1.1...v2.2.0 
```

# GPUI Kit v0.11.0  GPUI Kit 0.11.0 is a minor release. It moves to gpui-pre 0.3.14 and GPUI Fast 0.1.19, makes the Linux X11 backend an optional default feature, replaces `chrono` with `jiff` in the date and time components, and brings a long run of editor, Markdown, diff and accessibility work. The X11 feature, the jiff types and the upstream GPUI API changes are breaking, so this is a minor release.  ## Breaking Changes  - **Linux X11 is a feature**: `gpui-kit/x11` is now a default feature. Apps that turn off the defaults and still run on X11 must ask for it; Wayland-only apps no longer link `libxkbcommon-x11`. ([#4060](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4060))   diff   - gpui-kit = { version = "0.10", default-features = false }   + gpui-kit = { version = "0.11", default-features = false, features = ["x11"] }    - **Calendar, DatePicker, TimeField use jiff**: `Date`, `Matcher`, `CalendarState` and `TimeFieldState` take `jiff::civil::Date` / `Time` instead of chrono types, so `cargo deny` no longer flags the unmaintained `chrono` (RUSTSEC-2026-0543). Format strings are unchanged. ([#4015](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4015)) - **Upstream GPUI**: gpui-pre now also gives every `Element` a blanket `IntoElement`, so drop your own `impl IntoElement` on element types on both backends. `Path` is no longer generic, `WindowBounds::centered` takes `Option<DisplayId>`, and `FillOptions::tolerance` is gone. ([#4092](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4092))  ## New APIs  - **Input**: a suggestion list under the field like `<input list>`; public `fold_all`, `unfold_all`, `fold_at_level` and `set_folded`. ([#3785](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3785), [#4028](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4028)) - **Editor**: `GutterStyle` for the line-number gap, color and font; show the active line only while focused. ([#3737](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3737), [#4067](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4067)) - **Diff**: `span_highlights` for character spans, the character under the pointer for hosting links, and partial files at their own line numbers. ([#4011](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4011), [#4042](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4042), [#4043](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4043)) - **Components**: resizable handle drag state, `SelectableText` range reveal, a left-side vertical scrollbar, and a `DockSkin` option to hide the resting split divider. ([#4039](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4039), [#4052](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4052), [#4078](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4078), [#4084](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4084)) - **Shell**: `PluginManager::with_host_module`. ([#3948](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3948))  ## Editor and text input  - Faster soft wrap for large and non-ASCII documents; f64 vertical coordinates past 2^24 px and 9-digit line numbers; clip text to its side of the gutter. ([#3974](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3974), [#4022](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4022), [#4077](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4077)) - End IME composition on blur, redact IME surrounding text in masked inputs, report the document length so the iOS keyboard shows. ([#4032](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4032), [#4059](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4059), [#4082](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4082)) - Ctrl+Delete stops at white space and line breaks; a single-line input drops tabs like newlines; each paragraph of a text area starts at the edge it reads from; the input text takes the theme foreground. ([#4034](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4034), [#4071](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4071), [#4016](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4016), [#4051](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4051)) - Less accessibility text-run memory on large documents. ([#4090](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4090))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - Inline flows lay out once in a shrinking bubble and wrap without rescanning the paragraph; inline-code paragraphs keep their text between frames; closing marks stay on the word's line. ([#4029](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4029), [#4058](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4058), [#4063](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4063), [#4087](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4087)) - Show Markdown as plain text if the parser panics; stop sweeping retained layouts that are all alive; select a wrapped row's first character; share linked-image clicks; expose the rendered body to the accessibility tree. ([#4013](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4013), [#4000](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4000), [#4012](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4012), [#4053](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4053), [#4056](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4056), [#4054](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4054)) - Highlighter injection layers follow the caller's parse timeout. ([#4074](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4074))  ## Components  - **Diff**: file headers and annotation cards stay in the viewport while scrolling horizontally. ([#3996](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3996), [#4009](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4009)) - **Dialog, Notification, Popover, Root**: the backdrop stays inside the client frame; notifications sit above modal layers; a controlled popover moves focus after the frame; Tab works before anything is focused. ([#4049](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4049), [#3577](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3577), [#4023](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4023), [#4017](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4017)) - **Menu, Scrollbar, Table, Tabs, Resizable**: no scrollbar on a menu that fits; only the primary button drags the track; `set_selected_*` no longer scrolls; Tabs focus on pointer down; resizing skips hidden panels. ([#4014](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4014), [#4038](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4038), [#4046](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4046), [#4002](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4002), [#4064](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4064)) - **Title bar, Kbd, Motion, Icon, Button**: follow Linux window button layouts; shifted-symbol shortcuts on Windows; a closed `Presence` reports Absent; warn once for a missing icon path; expose the expanded state. ([#4018](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4018), [#3993](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3993), [#4062](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4062), [#4075](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4075), [#4035](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4035)) - **WebView**: no gaps at fractional sizes on macOS, clip partly visible WebViews on Windows. ([#4065](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4065), [#4066](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4066))  ## Docs and tooling  - A Claude Code plugin for the GPUI Kit skills, fixed guide links in the standalone skills, a fuller zh-CN List guide, and the right canvas size for web examples in Firefox. ([#4089](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4089), [#4088](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4088), [#4030](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4030), [#4045](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4045), [#4048](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4048), [#4047](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4047))  ## Dependencies  - gpui-pre 0.3.14 and GPUI Fast 0.1.19 (via 0.1.17); `chrono` replaced by `jiff` 1.0; Cargo.lock updated for 8 RustSec advisories; unused `anyhow` removed. ([#4027](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4027), [#4092](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4092), [#4015](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4015), [#4080](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4080), [#4081](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/4081))  ## Contributors  <details> <summary>Thank you to everyone who contributed to this release</summary>  @huacnlee, @dux-web, @lurenjia534, @sassman, @bcl-dev, @hlcfan, @iam4x, @ylinwind, @ketansingrole, @ManasDasri, @Abdulrahman-AlGhamdi, @HappyFox001, @madcodelife, @jeeeeetshahhhhh, @byht1, @TheVSAKeeper, @yuluod, @costajohnt, @leonmarq, @sator-imaging, @bhavishyasharma, @iamichi, @subhdotsol, @wangdiandao, @superoskin, @peterdpong, @wyattjoh, @mwatts, @PeatBoy, @erenatas, ### 2026-11-20T05:17:35Z [**komari-monitor/komari**](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari)

```
## 主要更新内容  - **终端控制协议**：终端 WebSocket 改用「二进制帧传终端内容 + 文本帧传控制消息」的双通道设计，新增 `heartbeat`、`resize`、`terminate`、`abort`、`assigned`、`ready` 等消息类型。控制消息不会污染终端缓冲区，且两端都会忽略未知消息，新增类型无需客户端改代码。服务端与 Agent 均已实现。 - **终端会话可恢复**：浏览器或 Agent 意外断开后，会话在服务端保留 5 分钟，可用同一 `request_id` 重连续接，期间自动向在线一方发送 `abort` 等待重连；超时才发送 `terminate` 并清理。新增 `admin:listRecoverableTerminalSessions` 与 `admin:terminateTerminalSessions` 接口，用于列出和终止等待恢复的会话。 - **终端自由分屏**：终端工作区支持任意方向、任意层级的窗格分割，可自由拖动调整布局，标签与分屏结构相互独立。 - **终端、编辑器与文件管理体验优化**：完善终端设置对话框、背景媒体、上传进度展示，优化编辑器内终端面板与远程文件树的交互；终端默认关闭 EOL 转换，避免输出出现多余回车。 - **全面增强管理员日志**：登录/登出（含 User-Agent）、2FA 启用/禁用、OAuth 绑定/解绑、用户编辑（含字段变更明细）、客户端增删改、插件与主题安装更新、备份下载/上传、数据库维护、文件传输、通知发送等操作均记录操作者 IP 与 UUID；同一终端会话只写一条审计记录，重连、挂起、结束时原地更新时长、重连次数与结束原因。连接串凭据等敏感值自动脱敏。 - **日志筛选查询**：管理端日志接口新增 `msg_type` 与 `q` 参数，支持按消息类型及关键词（内容、IP、UUID）筛选。 - **Agent 可被服务器读取启动参数**：服务器可通过协议获取 Agent 的完整启动配置，便于在线排查部署问题。 - **Agent 支持远程切换版本**：可在管理端请求 Agent 切换到指定版本线（稳定版或快照版），切换完成自动重启生效。 - **前端性能优化**：重构构建配置，移除 `vite-plugin-pages` 改为静态路由，优化代码分包、首屏加载与 PWA 缓存策略，显著减小打包体积并改善渲染性能。 - **后台视觉与动效更新**：新增全局动画设计，统一后台页面的过渡与操作反馈，优化整体视觉表现。  ## Bug修复  - 修复主题解压时目录权限沿用压缩包内的 `0664`（缺少执行位）导致后续写入文件失败、市场安装主题报 `permission denied` 的问题，目录统一以 `0755` 创建。 - 修复自动续费月末到期日溢出问题：按 `AddDate` 计算会把 10 月 31 日续费成 12 月 1 日，现改为保持在目标月份内（如 1 月 31 日 → 2 月 28/29 日）。 - 修复 GPU 温度字段被截断为整数的问题，服务端与 Agent 均改为保留小数精度（如 `55.25°C`）。 - 修复备份白名单中 `plguin-data/` 拼写错误，更正为 `plugin-data/`。 - 修复插件设置页左右栏错位及空状态提示重复的问题，并补充「请在左侧选择要配置的插件」引导。 - 修复 Linux 下 LXC 容器上报的 uptime 为宿主机数值的问题，改为读取 `/proc/uptime`。 - 修复 Windows 安装脚本在用户名或路径含空格时安装失败的问题（UAC 提权后自动重新转义参数，nssm 服务路径不再写入多余引号）。相关 [#118](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari-agent/issues/118)、[komari#655](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/issues/655) - 静态资源写入失败时补充明确告警，便于定位权限问题。  ## 其他  - WebSocket 连接改为自行管理在线状态，避免 HTTP 上报与连接生命周期互相干扰。 - 非 API 请求（静态资源、前端页面）的 2xx/304 响应不再写入请求日志，减少日志噪音；异常响应与 API 请求仍完整记录。 - 删除正在使用的主题前增加二次确认提示。 - 允许 Ping 检测任务留空，不再强制要求填写目标。 - 新增 CI 测试工作流，包含 `go vet`、单元测试、重复运行隔离测试、race detector 与覆盖率报告；清理部分冗余或易冲突的测试用例。 - 同步多语言翻译，补充终端、日志与分屏相关文案。 
```
