# Chatless 0.8.2 发布  ## 下载 ### Windows - [Chatless Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_x64-setup.exe) - [Chatless MSI (.msi)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_x64_en-US.msi)            ### macOS - [Apple Silicon](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_aarch64.dmg) - [Intel 处理器（通过 Rosetta 2 运行）](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_aarch64.dmg)  > **打不开、提示「已损坏」？** 拖进 Applications 之后，终端运行下面这一行就好（本构建是 ad-hoc 签名，没有做 Apple 公证）： > `sudo xattr -r -d com.apple.quarantine /Applications/Chatless.app`  ### Linux - [Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora (.rpm)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless-0.8.2-1.x86_64.rpm) - [Ubuntu/Debian (.deb)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_amd64.deb) - [通用格式 (.AppImage)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.8.2/Chatless_0.8.2_amd64.AppImage)            ---  ## 安装说明  ### Windows 1. 下载 .exe 文件（推荐）或 .msi 文件 2. 双击运行安装程序 3. 按提示完成安装  ### macOS 1. 根据处理器选择版本：    - Apple Silicon (M1/M2/M3): 下载 aarch64.dmg    - Intel 处理器: 下载 aarch64.dmg 通过 Rosetta 2 运行 2. 双击打开，拖拽到 Applications 文件夹 3. 从 Applications 启动应用  **注意**: 首次启动可能提示"已损坏"，请： 1. 点击"取消" 2. 打开终端，运行:   3. 重新启动应用  ### Linux 1. 根据发行版选择格式：    - Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora: 下载 .rpm 文件    - Ubuntu/Debian: 下载 .deb 文件    - 其他发行版: 下载 .AppImage 文件 2. 使用包管理器安装或直接运行  ---  ## 常见问题  **Windows 闪退**: 安装 [Visual C++ 运行库](https://aka.ms/vs/17/release/vc_redist.x64.exe)  **macOS 无法打开**: 使用终端命令移除隔离属性（见上方说明）  **Linux 权限问题**: 确保给予执行权限 `chmod +x`  ---  ## 文档  - [安装指南](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/blob/main/INSTALLATION_INSTRUCTIONS.md) - [项目主页](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless) - [问题反馈](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/issues)  ---  ## 更新内容  ### 功能与体验 - 会话侧栏支持按工作区或时间分组，可归档和恢复工作区，主导航可单独折叠。 - 新会话页以输入框为中心。输入 `/` 选技能、`#` 选提示词、`@` 选 MCP 服务或工作区文件；这些入口也可通过 `+` 打开。 - 工具审批弹窗并列说明两种模式，并提示哪些操作仍需确认。  ### 修复与性能 - 会话工具现在使用实际绑定的项目目录：`@WorkDir`、相对路径和命令的默认目录不再指向独立的会话产物目录。绑定工作区不会自动授予文件权限。 - 界面外壳出错时，可在恢复页重新加载或关闭桌面窗口。 - 减少启动时的重复数据库查询，延后非必要任务，让会话历史和输入框更早可用。 - 应用窗口预设或恢复窗口尺寸时，将窗口限制在当前屏幕的可用区域内，避免在 Windows 缩放场景下超出屏幕。 
```

### 2026-11-23T11:45:30Z [**AChep/keyguard-app**](https://github.com/AChep/keyguard-app)

```
> [!NOTE]   > Browser extensions themselves are not yet released and are waiting for their appropriate store reviews. Therefore you can not yet use the browser integration right now, but you will after the extensions gradually appear in the stores.  ### New - Browser extension: Added Chrome and Firefox browser extensions.  ### Improvements - Desktop: Dialogs, settings screens, and vault screens now have better focus navigation and keyboard handling. - Desktop: The approval request window is now guarded against accidental clicks for the first second. - Generator: Usage history older than a configurable number of days is now automatically deleted. - Generator: Wordlists can now be imported in the Diceware format. - Generator: Words in a wordlist can now be added, edited, and deleted. - Autofill: URIs can now be blocked from the save requests list. - UI: Concealed password text fields now show Caps Lock and non-English keyboard layout badges. - UI: The app now checks whether a newer version is available on GitHub. - Android: The predictive back animation now includes a Y-axis parallax effect.  ### Security - Desktop: HotSpot Attach is now disabled in release builds. - Windows: Windows Error Reporting data is now limited.  ### Fixes - Bitwarden: Fixed server URLs with single-label hostnames not being supported (#1222). - UI: Fixed Settings and Backup items so they are grouped together correctly. 
```

### 2026-11-20T23:21:29Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - New button "Edit the infobar message" in the toolbar of the annotation editor. It edits the message displayed in the infobar of the saved page, i.e. the text produced by the option "Infobar > template of the infobar content" or entered at save time with "Infobar > open a prompt dialog to edit the infobar content". A line break is written `\n` in the prompt, and an empty text removes the message. The button also works when the infobar is not displayed in the editor, and it is hidden when the page does not carry the comment SingleFile writes at the top of a saved page (#2065)  ### Fixes  - Saving a selection no longer saves the unselected text written directly in the elements that hold the selection. The selection was marked on elements only, so text that is in no element of its own was kept: on a page writing its content as bare text of `<body>`, every saved selection came with all of that text. The content script now records which text of a partly selected element is selected (attribute `data-single-file-selected-text`) and the rest is removed (#2066) - "Save selected links" no longer misses the first link when the selection starts inside the text of a link, and a selection made inside the text of a single link finds that link instead of nothing (#2068) - The toolbar of the annotation editor can be dragged again when it does not fit in the window, e.g. on a small screen in portrait orientation. On a single page the limit of the drag was computed from a hidden button and the toolbar did not move (regression in 1.23.0) - Translated the labels added in 1.39.0 and in this version in 14 languages, updated the Russian (#2064) and Polish (#2067) translations - Updated single-file-core to 1.15.0 
```

## Release Notes  Released on 2026-11-19.  ### Preview features  - \[`flake8-type-checking`\] Add a fix for `runtime-string-union` (`TC010`) ([#15635](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/15635))  ### Bug fixes  - Fix handling of parenthesized duplicate items (`B033`, `RUF068`) ([#30493](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30493)) - \[`pyflakes`\] Fix false negative when a name is assigned more than once (`F823`) ([#30524](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30524)) - \[`pylint`\] Avoid false positive when collection is modified in loop (`PLR1733`, `PLR1736`) ([#30466](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30466)) - \[`ruff`\] Avoid syntax error in `useless-finally` fix for inline `try` bodies (`RUF072`) ([#30349](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30349)) - Treat relative imports from `.__future__` as ordinary imports ([#30260](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30260))  ### Rule changes  - \[`flake8-simplify`\] Avoid redundant `bool` wrappers in `SIM208` fixes ([#30457](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30457)) - \[`pyflakes`\] Ignore `__traceback_hide__` in `unused-variable` (`F841`) ([#29600](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29600))  ### Performance  - Decode in-memory notebooks directly from their source string ([#30385](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30385)) - Short-circuit exact qualified-name checks ([#30058](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30058)) - Stream JSON diagnostics to the output writer ([#30384](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30384))  ### Documentation  - Fix the multiline string example in Known Deviations from Black ([#30447](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30447)) - Include the category in individual rule documentation pages ([#30503](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30503))  ### Other changes  - Update Rust toolchain to 1.100 and MSRV to 1.98 ([#30416](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30416))  ### Contributors  - [@pseudo-usama](https://github.com/pseudo-usama) - ### 2026-11-19T16:14:07Z [**PBH-BTN/PeerBanHelper**](https://github.com/PBH-BTN/PeerBanHelper)

```
This version includes fix patches for important security issues.  ## 安全性改进  * **【重要-Upstream Exploit】** 此版本已更新 JVM 启动参数以缓解 OpenJDK 的 [CVE-2026-114938(Java4Shell)](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-114938) 远程代码执行漏洞。攻击者可通过 (1) 向 WebUI 发送恶意请求、(2) 欺骗用户输入恶意构造的 PBH 许可证代码 (3) 连接恶意架设的第三方 BTN 实例 (4) 连接恶意架设的 Transmission 下载器或 BiglyBT 下载器，路径触发漏洞获取设备访问权限。   * 我们通过启动参数应用了临时缓解错误，并继续等待上游 Java OpenJDK 运行环境的安全补丁。通过 JRE、批处理等方式手动部署和启动的 PeerBanHelper 用户，由于使用自定义启动脚本，请自行更新您的启动参数。 * **【重要-Upstream Exploit】** 此版本已更新 OpenJDK 运行环境，以缓解 OpenJDK 的 [CVE-2026-91403(eMudhra)](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-91403) HTTPS 安全信任漏洞。eMudhra 是一家印度 SSL 证书印度公钥基础设施公司。由于其中间 CA 证书已泄露且其签发的 SSL 证书广受设备和厂商信任，恶意攻击者可通过 DNS 劫持和伪造签发的 HTTPS 证书，劫持您访问恶意架设的更新服务器、BTN 服务器、规则订阅更新服务器，并通过软件更新、BTN 脚本下发、规则下发方式进行远程代码执行，或将 PeerBanHelper 更新至精心构造的恶意构建。   * 此问题还影响所有 Windows 和 Linux 发行版的系统证书库、嵌入式设备和其它语言的应用程序。建议您及时排查并撤销 eMudhra 的证书信任。  此外，本月 Linux 内核、PHP、Nginx 都另有多个高危权限提升漏洞，建议您及时排查更新操作系统内核与软件包，关注安全资讯。   请您帮助转告他人有关本次安全更新的重要通知，并及时升级 PeerBanHelper 程序和 Docker 镜像。本版发布后，BTN 服务器将会启动对使用受影响版本且未更新用户的安全通知推送，安全告警将通过 BTN 账户绑定的 GitHub 邮箱发送。  ## 新功能  * IP 查询现在支持网段评论，您可以将您对某个网段的发现发送为评论，以供其它用户或研究人员参考 @Ghost-chu @Gaojianli  ## 错误修复  * 修复特定情况下的 SWT 初始化可能阻塞 Swing 初始化导致 UI 窗口黑屏/白屏无响应的问题 @Ghost-chu  * 修复 AutoSTUN 使用 io_uring 初始化套接字时，系统 I/O Wait 指标激增的问题 @Ghost-chu @paulzzh    * 目前，AutoSTUN 默认继续使用 epoll (Linux), kqueue (macOS) 和 select (Windows) 模型 * 修复某些情况下，BTN 网络返回错误响应时，PeerBanHelper 重复读取响应体出现 IllegalStateException 的问题  @Ghost-chu * 修复更新 IPDB 时，由于网络错误、网络中断或服务器异常响应时，可能导致更新的 IPDB 文件损坏的问题 @Ghost-chu  * [WebUI] 统一 URL 前缀 @Gaojianli   ## 有关爱发电、面包多渠道捐赠事宜更新通知  即日起，我们已停止受理通过人工邮件方式找回 PBH Plus 许可证的请求。许可证的签发和管理将通过 BTN v3.1 进行。您可以登录 BTN v3.1，前往 "个人资料->设置->捐赠支持与许可证" 页面绑定爱发电/面包多订单号来自助管理许可证。  如您在爱发电捐赠时填写的邮箱与登录 GitHub 的邮箱一致，您的爱发电账号信息将自动与您的 BTN 账号绑定。如果没有成功自动绑定，请您手动输入爱发电订单号进行账号绑定。   由于此前部分签发的许可证备注信息带有购买订单号，因此如您将许可证泄露或发送给他人，可能会被第三方绑定爱发电账号，请您及时绑定。  如出现被他人绑定或无法绑定的情况，请与我们联系。  ## Docker  DockerHub: `ghostchu/peerbanhelper:v9.6.0`   阿里云国内镜像加速: `registry.cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/ghostchu/peerbanhelper:v9.6.0`  ---  [部署教程](https://docs.pbh-btn.com/docs/category/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E9%83%A8%E7%BD%B2) | [常见问题](https://docs.pbh-btn.com/docs/faq) | [如何设置下载器](https://docs.pbh-btn.com/docs/category/%E4%B8%8B%E8%BD%BD%E5%99%A8%E9%85%8D%E7%BD%AE) | [PBH-BTN 论坛](https://bbs.pbh-btn.com)   ---   以下是近期 BTN 服务的更新日志。  ## BTN v3.1 更新日志  <img width="2160" height="451" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/018be300-df97-46ac-87a4-7b1bc7097eed" />  欢迎来到 BTN 网络 v3.1 版本。v3.1 是 PBH-BTN 官方 Sparkle BTN 实例的重大升级，在视觉、稳定性和数据分析能力上得到了大幅提升和改进。以下是主要更新内容。  ### 新的图表  新增多个 BTN 网络趋势图表，以便帮助您快速了解网络和威胁整体趋势与状态。  <img width="1093" height="644" alt="1" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/f709d72b-c8f3-4bc0-8bcf-3ced871a6206" /> <img width="1095" height="458" alt="2" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/3da089c9-d7bf-4702-be48-a4d129cc04b8" /> <img width="1104" height="443" alt="3" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/fd3a2845-293e-4ce5-a141-455ad65884dd" /> <img width="1086" height="646" alt="4" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/f6f907e8-88e6-4818-aa87-0fd00ec8d278" /> <img width="1264" height="697" alt="5" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/0b7c8244-52ca-41d5-ba63-0fd38912ed53" /> <img width="1269" height="700" alt="6" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/bcb711c7-da36-48a1-8c62-5fa72247a625" />  ### 全面增强的 IP 查询能力  BTN 新增 IP 查询功能，可一键汇总查询指定 IP / CIDR 的历史数据。   新的 IP 查询还增设了评论区能力，您可以将您对某段 IP 的发现与其它人分享。切换网段大小可以查看更大网段下的所有评论。  <img width="1970" height="1160" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b4c69446-a2d4-473d-ba9b-d574709b38a1" />  <img width="1954" height="1256" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/5c005d09-3001-4aa1-865a-ddba1ef7b1b2" />   ### 新设计的个人资料页面与统计数据  现在个人资料页面将能够统计和显示您在 BTN 网络上的历史统计数据。  <img width="1606" height="1226" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b2d4c9e7-9c36-48a1-8bd1-357ba654aeec" />  ### 个人流量统计  我的流量统计页面现在能够汇总显示关联 BTN 客户端的流量活动。  <img width="2157" height="1261" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/212e0705-e55c-47e0-9e85-140d91f8a2d8" />  <img width="2129" height="606" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/080b2bab-2d90-4deb-be26-e5840a3bdf10" />  ### 提交记录历史数据  如果您好奇您的 BTN 客户端提交了哪些数据，现在您可以在我的提交记录页面中查看您账号下各 BTN 客户端近 7 天提交的历史数据记录。  <img width="2197" height="1220" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/359680aa-cf93-465a-83f9-f3f475745f78" />  ### 积分与排行榜  之前由于算法性能问题遭到移除的积分与排行榜现已回归，您可以查看您的积分变动详情和排行榜数据。  <img width="1795" height="1191" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/33eaa918-963d-42f4-a024-30d6f4343c75" />  <img width="2216" height="1234" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/95f8c805-7d55-4143-ab90-311731524788" />  ### 新的隐私设置  新的隐私设置页面允许您对自己账号的哪些数据显示和公开进行设置。  <img width="1756" height="1169" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/e90886e3-0c29-496f-9b56-be0cb01db9f2" />  ### 通知和提醒偏好  新的通知和提醒系统允许您自定义设置哪些信息通过站内信发送，哪些信息通过邮件发送，或者完全拒绝接受某一类的通知信息。  <img width="1746" height="1237" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/ab012acb-40db-40eb-a213-ecff7cd1dcdc" />  ### 社区群组  现在我们在 GitHub 上也建立了社区群组，您可以通过社区群组页面快速加入 GitHub 群组、Telegram 频道和 QQ 群。  <img width="1722" height="1208" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/8a38bc1b-5b50-472e-928c-dd72ca4916fc" />  ### 账户注销  BTN 现在支持账户注销功能，注销的账户将解除与您的 Github 账户和邮箱的关联，并匿名化您的个人信息。  ### 视觉更新  全新的基于 Arco Design 设计的前端样式，支持亮色和暗色模式及跟随系统，本次还额外增加了英语 （English） 语言支持。  ### 捐赠支持与许可证  现在爱发电和面包多的数据由 BTN 管理，您可以在系统上申请新的许可证，或者查询之前发送给您的爱发电许可证信息。 
```

### 2026-11-18T09:55:28Z [**langgenius/dify**](https://github.com/langgenius/dify)

```
**Dify 1.20.0 keeps your traces flowing and your knowledge bases safer.** Export traces to any OpenTelemetry collector, keep Langfuse working after its legacy ingestion endpoint retirement, and scan uploaded knowledge files for malware before they are indexed. Chatflows can show Agent tool activity as it happens, and personal access tokens let scripts and agents call the Dify OpenAPI on your behalf. The bundled plugin daemon is upgraded to **0.6.15-local**.  [New Features](#user-content-new-features) · [Configuration and Integration Changes](#user-content-configuration-and-integration-changes) · [Database Migrations](#user-content-database-migrations) · [Upgrade Guide](#user-content-upgrade-guide)  ## New Features  ### New HITL: bring approvals into your team's messaging tools  **Integrate human review into your workflows via email and the messaging tools your team already uses.** New HITL introduces shared channels, centralized contacts, notification templates, and verified approval forms—allowing your team to configure recipients once and reuse them across Human Input nodes, and is now enabled by default (`NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_HITL_IM=true`). (#44864, #45945)  * **Reach approvers where they work.** Configure email and an IM platform—**Slack, Feishu/Lark, Microsoft Teams, DingTalk, or WeCom**—to deliver workflow review requests through your team's existing channels. * **Manage recipients in Contacts.** Sync IM directories and select approvers from a shared contact list. Workflows can also address the initiator or use one-time and dynamic email recipients. * **Approve in cards or forms.** Recipients can review requests directly in supported IM cards or open standalone forms, with Dify login or email verification to verify their identity. * **Customize notifications with workflow context.** Use templates and workflow variables to include essential context in notification subjects and bodies. * **Test and track delivery.** Send test notifications to verify your setup and inspect delivery records to troubleshoot requests that did not arrive as expected. * **Adopt New HITL in existing workflows.** Use opt-in migration to upgrade legacy Human Input nodes when your workflow is ready.  Deployment requirements are listed under [Configuration and Integration Changes](#user-content-configuration-and-integration-changes).  ### Export traces to any OpenTelemetry collector  Bring Dify execution traces into your existing observability stack. The new **OpenTelemetry provider** exports app and Agent traces to any compatible collector, allowing you to track workflow steps, model calls, and tool activity together in your preferred platform. Use these traces to inspect request execution and diagnose failures without requiring a vendor-specific Dify integration. (#45659)  **Langfuse tracing now supports Langfuse v4 and the current Langfuse Cloud ingestion service.** Existing Langfuse configurations continue to work. (#45606)  > [!IMPORTANT] > Langfuse Cloud retired its legacy ingestion endpoint on **November 16, 2026**, and self-hosted Langfuse v4 rejects events sent to it. Upgrade to 1.20.0 to keep delivering traces to those deployments.  ### Scan knowledge uploads for malware  Protect your knowledge bases by scanning uploaded files for malware before they are extracted or indexed. When enabled (`FILE_SCAN_ENABLED=true`), workspace owners and admins can configure scanners once under **Integrations → File Security Scan**, then define how each knowledge base uses them. Supported scanners include **Cloudmersive, Scanii, OPSWAT MetaDefender Cloud**, and a **Custom Endpoint**. (#46090, #46492)  * **Choose protection per knowledge base.** Select a scanner and configure fallback behavior when scanning is unavailable or a file cannot be scanned. * **Protect knowledge uploads across Dify.** Scanning covers classic knowledge bases, RAG Pipeline local-file sources, and Agent Knowledge Base uploads. * **See why files were blocked.** Scan failures appear in the document list for review.  ### Watch Agent tools work in Chatflow  When a Chatflow delegates tasks to an Agent v2 node (requires `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_AGENT_V2_IN_CHATFLOW=true`), users can now see which tools it invokes in real time. Tool activity appears alongside the response in both previews and published chats, making it easier to follow the Agent's progress. Expand any tool card to inspect what it requested and returned. (#46220)  ### Automate OpenAPI calls with personal access tokens  Grant scripts, CI jobs, and personal agents access to the Dify OpenAPI on your behalf without sharing your Console session. Create a personal access token from your **Account** page and scope it to specific workspaces and resources. You can set an optional expiration date, update its scope as your automation evolves, or revoke it when it is no longer needed. (#45540, #46606)  ### Choose GitHub knowledge sources from a tree  Browse and select the GitHub content you want to add to a knowledge base from an expandable tree of repositories, directories, issues, and pull requests. Explore the source structure and pick the exact pages to import, whether you are creating a new knowledge base or adding a source to an existing one. (#45990)  ### Search and navigate on smaller screens  Search across content and narrow results using selectable scopes in **Goto Anything**. On smaller screens, search and navigation open in drawers to preserve space for the current page. (#45675, #45650)  Direct links can now open Studio's blank-app or template creation flows. (#45717)  ### More flexible knowledge descriptions and metadata  * **Longer knowledge base descriptions.** Increased description length limits allow you to provide more context for each knowledge base. (#45075) * **External knowledge metadata.** External knowledge retrieval now returns document metadata alongside content. (#22496)  ## Improvements  **Lower database and startup overhead.** Node execution updates avoid reloading stored rows, credential policies are evaluated once per provider configuration, model defaults use short-lived sessions, and embedding calls no longer hold open a database session. Web feature dependencies also load more lazily. (#46410, #46441, #45806, #45788, #46145)  **Clearer document failures and steadier layouts.** Knowledge documents now report specific failure reasons and processing stages. Dialogs adapt to narrow viewports and maintain consistent lifecycles across accounts, knowledge, apps, Skills, and workflows. Authentication pages load faster, fit smaller screens, and keep keyboard focus accessible. Segmented controls animate smoothly while preserving compact layouts and respecting reduced-motion preferences. (#45128, #46487, #45686, #45668, #45634, #45600, #45640, #45974)  ## Bug Fixes  * **Safer file previews and signed links.** Sandbox inline SVG previews and verify the original signature before renewing expired file links. (#46505) * **Stronger access protection.** Redact node-retry details from public streams, prevent OAuth callback reuse, enforce member-role permissions, and scope vector deletion to the target dataset. (#46517, #46001, #46053, #45586) * **Security dependency updates.** Upgrade `github.com/klauspost/compress` in `dify-agent-runtime` to address CVE-2026-116591, and update Node.js in runtime images to security release 24.22.3. (#46544, #46206, #46064) * **Reliable blocking workflows.** Release database connections before execution and return preparation failures immediately, preventing connection-pool exhaustion and hanging requests that wait until timeout. (#46254) * **More stable workers.** Resolve the Redis `BRPOP` busy loop and prevent overlapping plugin upgrade checks, capping concurrency at 20 checks across workers. (#45940, #46500) * **Complete Chatflow streams.** Properly close streams after direct replies and reset the reconnect budget when a stream resumes. (#45552, #46110) * **Correct Agent execution.** Preserve parallel calls to the same plugin tool, pass declared app inputs to Agent runs, and deliver uploaded images to vision models. (#45207, #45425, #45745) * **Accurate knowledge retrieval.** Restore Weaviate keyword/hybrid results by returning BM25 scores, and keep embedding vectors aligned when a batch item is dropped. (#45642, #45569) * **Reliable custom API tools.** Resolve OpenAPI server variables, percent-encode path parameters, and support OpenAPI 3.1 nullable unions. (#46504, #45965, #45895)  ## Configuration and Integration Changes  ### New and changed settings  | Setting | Default / change | Purpose | | --- | --- | --- | | `OPENAPI_ENABLED` | `true` (was `false`) | Enable `/openapi/v1/*` endpoints by default so personal access tokens and `difyctl` work out of the box. Set to `false` to disable. (#46606) | | `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_HITL_IM` | `true` (was `false`) | Enable New HITL IM, Contacts, V2 editing/migration, and the approval UI. Restart Web and reload the page after changing this value. | | `HUMAN_INPUT_GLOBAL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | `604800` seconds (7 days) | Maximum duration a workflow can remain paused while awaiting human input. | | `HUMAN_INPUT_IM_EVENT_TRANSPORT_MODE` | `WEBHOOK` | Deployment-wide IM event transport mode. Webhook callbacks require an externally reachable API. Only `WEBHOOK` mode is available now. | | `TRIGGER_URL` | Set to the public HTTPS API origin for IM callbacks | Used to generate channel Callback URLs. Update the IM provider configuration when this origin changes. | | `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_AGENT_V2_IN_CHATFLOW` | `false` | Enable Agent v2 nodes in Chatflow, including live tool activity. | | `FILE_SCAN_ENABLED` | `false` | Enable knowledge-file malware scanning globally; individual knowledge bases must also opt in. | | `FILE_SCAN_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | `300` | Timeout for a scanner request. | | `FILE_SCAN_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `3` | Maximum attempts when a scanner is temporarily unreachable. | | `FILE_SCAN_CLEAN_VERDICT_TTL_HOURS` | `24` | Reuse period for a clean verdict on the same file content and scanner. | | `DATASET_DESCRIPTION_MAX_LENGTH` | `400` | Maximum character length for knowledge base descriptions. Restart the API after changing this value. | | `DEEPEVAL_DISABLE_DOTENV` / `DEEPEVAL_TELEMETRY_OPT_OUT` | `1` | Shared backend environment defaults prevent DeepEval from loading dotenv files or exporting telemetry. | | `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_SHARED_MOUNT_PATH` | Removed | Shared-volume Home Snapshots are no longer supported by the OpenShell backend. | | `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_DRIVER_CONFIG` | Now optional | When left empty, an empty driver configuration is sent. | | `EXPERIMENTAL_ENABLE_VINEXT` | Removed | Removed from `web.defaults` and Web Docker image builds. (#46616) |  For Docker Compose, configure shared backend overrides in `envs/core-services/api-worker.env` and Web overrides in `envs/core-services/web.env`. Root `docker/.env` takes precedence. Apply changes with `docker compose up -d`; a plain restart does not reload environment files.  The OpenTelemetry provider supports OTLP/HTTP with protobuf; gRPC is not supported. Live Agent tool activity requires streaming output, is unavailable for buffered-output paths, and is not replayed from chat history.  File scanning is disabled by default at the deployment level and must also be enabled per knowledge base. Policies for scanner unavailability and unscannable files both default to blocking the file.  Disabling `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_HITL_IM` does not roll back saved data or halt server-side work already in progress.  ### Reverse proxy and worker configuration  * **New API route:** In `WEBHOOK` mode, forward `/callbacks/human-input/v2/im/` to the API service (`api:5001` in Docker Compose), preserving the request path, raw body, and provider signature headers. The bundled nginx template already includes this rule; add its equivalent to custom nginx, Ingress, or other reverse proxy configurations. Configure the IM provider with the channel's generated Callback URL. (#44864) * **New Celery queues:** Include `human_input_delivery` for asynchronous IM callback processing and post-submission card updates, and `human_input_contact_sync` for IM contact directory synchronization. The default Docker worker list includes both. Custom `CELERY_QUEUES`, `CELERY_WORKER_QUEUES`, or Celery `--queues` lists must provide an active consumer for each queue alongside existing subscriptions. (#44864)  ### Docker environment files  `docker/envs/core-services/shared.env` is **no longer loaded**. Move common API, worker, and Beat settings to `api-worker.env` before recreating containers. Preserve any service-specific settings in the override file used by that service. (#46497)  Public URLs are shared by Web, API, and workers through the new `envs/core-services/urls.defaults` and optional `urls.env`:  * `CONSOLE_API_URL` * `CONSOLE_WEB_URL` * `SERVICE_API_URL` * `APP_API_URL` * `APP_WEB_URL`  Set browser-accessible base URLs without `/console/api`, `/api`, or `/v1` suffixes. Explicit values in `urls.env` override service-specific URL settings; root `docker/.env` overrides both. Web's internal `SERVER_CONSOLE_API_URL` remains in `web.env`. This also resolves an issue where generated icon links pointed to `127.0.0.1:5001` on fresh installations. (#46497)  ## Database Migrations  Upgrading from **1.19.0** adds **six migration revisions**, moving the migration head from `53c10126d551` to `f8b6e2c4a901`. Apply the complete Alembic chain through the normal migration runner.  | Revision | Change | | --- | --- | | `6b7e2a9c4d10` | Add nullable `started_by_sys_user_id` to `human_input_im_sync_runs` and require exactly one initiating identity per row. | | `b2e4c6d8a013` | Add personal access tokens and their resource/workspace scope tables. | | `8c2f5d7a1e30` | Add file scanner, scan policy, and scan-result tables. | | `91f4b2d7c8e0` | Add Evaluation configuration, run, and run-item tables. | | `c7e4a1b2d9f0` | Add the Evaluation framework column with a default value. | | `f8b6e2c4a901` | Merge the Evaluation and main migration branches. |  The Evaluation migrations apply to the **Community Edition** as well, even though CE does not expose the Evaluation feature. Include these revisions when applying the migration chain; none of the six revisions require a separate manual backfill command. (#45945, #45540, #46090, #46141)  New HITL introduces the necessary database schema and automatically backfills Contact identities for existing accounts with no extra commands required. These migrations are already included in 1.19.0—existing 1.19.0 deployments need no action, while older versions should run the full migration chain. Legacy Human Input nodes are not automatically migrated and remain opt-in.  ## Upgrade Guide  > [!IMPORTANT] > Move settings out of the retired `shared.env` before recreating containers. Review public URLs, custom HITL callback routing, and worker subscriptions. OpenShell deployments require a compatible gateway upgrade and no longer restore Home Snapshots. The bundled plugin daemon is updated to **0.6.15-local**.  ### Prepare the deployment  1. **Back up data and configuration.** Back up your database, vector-store data, uploaded files, plugin daemon storage, Agent workspace storage, deployment secrets, and environment files. Schedule a maintenance window for a single-stack upgrade. 2. **Upgrade the Dify components together.** Use matching `1.20.0` API, worker, Web, Agent Backend, and Local sandbox images, alongside plugin daemon `0.6.15-local`. Keep `SECRET_KEY`, any configured `PASSWORD_PEPPER`, and the Local sandbox authentication token unchanged. Personal access tokens depend on `SECRET_KEY`; ensure the same value is used across all API replicas. If upgrading from a version earlier than 1.19.0, also follow the [1.19.0 upgrade guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/releases/tag/1.19.0). 3. **Migrate `shared.env`.** If `envs/core-services/api-worker.env` does not exist, rename `envs/core-services/shared.env` to that path. If both exist, merge only missing settings and preserve existing `api-worker.env` values, including intentional empty assignments. Retain service-specific settings in the appropriate service override file. Deployments without `shared.env` require no migration. 4. **Consolidate public URLs.** Create `envs/core-services/urls.env` from `urls.defaults` only if it does not already exist. Move the intended public URL values there, then remove duplicate assignments from individual service files. Existing root `.env` values retain precedence. Keep internal Web API routing in `web.env`. 5. **Apply environment, route, and queue changes.** Follow [Configuration and Integration Changes](#user-content-configuration-and-integration-changes), including the New HITL settings, IM callback route, and both Celery queues. 6. **Let plugin storage initialization run.** `init_permissions` now also prepares `./volumes/plugin_daemon` for the daemon's UID 1001, and the daemon waits for it to complete. Recreate the deployment with `docker compose up -d` so the init service runs. (#45615)  ### OpenShell deployments  Upgrade the OpenShell gateway and SDK together to **0.1.3 or later within the supported 0.1.x line**, and upgrade the Dify API and Agent Backend together. The gateway must support native service exposure and bearer passthrough, preserving the per-sandbox authorization token derived from `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_SHELLCTL_AUTH_TOKEN`. Keep that master secret identical across Agent Backend replicas. (#45862)  Build the OpenShell runtime image from the release checkout and set `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_SANDBOX_IMAGE` to the exact image reference available to the gateway's compute nodes. The image must include both `shellctl` and `iproute2`; an older cached runtime image may lack the required networking tools.  When upgrading from the previous shared deployment-wide `shellctl` token, generate a fresh high-entropy master secret and update all Agent Backend replicas together. Drain active work, then stop and restart existing sandboxes so `shellctl` restarts with the derived credential. Reacquiring an already healthy sandbox does not refresh its credential.  Shared-volume Home Snapshots and `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_SHARED_MOUNT_PATH` are removed. Existing running sandboxes retain their state across stop/restart cycles, whereas newly created Bindings start from the image's default Home. `DIFY_AGENT_OPENSHELL_DRIVER_CONFIG` is now optional. Review the [OpenShell deployment guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/blob/1.20.0/dify-agent/docs/dify-agent/guide/openshell.md), including its runtime-image requirements, before upgrading. Other sandbox backends are unaffected.  ### Docker Compose  Once the `1.20.0` tag and images are published, stop the deployment gracefully, switch to the release, migrate local configuration files, and then pull and start the updated services:  bash cd dify/docker docker compose stop -t -1 git fetch --tags git checkout 1.20.0 # Migrate shared.env, public URLs, and any custom proxy/worker configuration. docker compose pull docker compose up -d   Keep API startup migrations enabled (`MIGRATION_ENABLED=true`), or apply the full migration chain through your managed migration process before starting new workers. Use `docker compose up -d` after changing environment files; a plain restart does not reload them.  ### Source deployments  Use **Python 3.13**, **Node.js 24.22.3 or later in the 24.x line**, and **pnpm 12.18.0**. After stopping the services and switching to the release, run the following commands from the repository root:  bash uv sync --project api --frozen uv run --directory api flask db upgrade pnpm install --frozen-lockfile   Rebuild Web and restart the API, workers, scheduler, and Agent services from the same release. Update the standalone Agent Backend's locked dependencies if deploying it separately. Preserve existing worker subscriptions and add the two HITL queues where needed.  **Full Changelog:** [1.19.0 → 1.20.0](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/compare/1.19.0...1.20.0) 
```

### 2026-11-24T09:16:05Z [**0x2E/fusion**](https://github.com/0x2E/fusion)

```
## Security  - Feed discovery (`POST /api/feeds/validate`) now sends every request, redirect and probed link through the SSRF-checked client, and the dial check validates the IP actually being connected, closing a DNS rebinding gap. (#275)  ## Behavior changes  - OIDC-only setups (`FUSION_OIDC_ISSUER` set, no `FUSION_PASSWORD`) now start without `FUSION_ALLOW_EMPTY_PASSWORD`, and the login page only shows the enabled sign-in methods. (#283, #292) - `GET /api/oidc/enabled` is removed; use `GET /api/auth/methods`. The image `HEALTHCHECK` is updated, but custom Docker Compose or Kubernetes probes on the old route must be changed. - `FUSION_OIDC_REDIRECT_URI` is now optional. When unset it is derived from the request as `<scheme>://<host>/api/oidc/callback`. Keep setting it if your IdP has the legacy `/oidc/callback` path registered, or if your reverse proxy does not forward `Host` and `X-Forwarded-Proto`. (#284) - When `FUSION_OIDC_ALLOWED_USER` is unset, any account on the OIDC provider can sign in and the sidebar shows a notice; a future version will require it. A whitespace-only value now counts as unset. - OPML import now imports the valid feeds and lists each failed link with its reason, instead of rejecting the whole file. - Feed validation returns `400` for an unparsable proxy. With private feeds disallowed, a private proxy such as `127.0.0.1:7890` is now rejected for validation too, matching feed pulling.  ---  Details: * feat(auth): expose auth methods and support OIDC-only login page by @0x2E in https://github.com/0x2E/fusion/pull/288 * feat(auth): notify about unset FUSION_OIDC_ALLOWED_USER; derive OIDC redirect URI by @0x2E in https://github.com/0x2E/fusion/pull/289 * fix(feed): import valid OPML feeds when some links are invalid by @0x2E in https://github.com/0x2E/fusion/pull/291 * fix(feeds): route feed discovery through the SSRF-checked client by @0x2E in https://github.com/0x2E/fusion/pull/290   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/0x2E/fusion/compare/v1.3.1...v1.3.2 
```

superfile v1.6.1 adds native trash support across Linux, macOS, and Windows, multi-file selection for the file picker, and directory item counts. It also fixes symlink copying, ZIP names for hidden files, and several shortcuts that acted on the wrong panel.  ## Install  [**How to install superfile**](https://github.com/yorukot/superfile?tab=readme-ov-file#installation)  ## Highlights  - Press `ctrl+d` to send files to the system trash. On Linux, files on other mounted drives can now use the trash on that drive. Thanks @yorukot and @paczek654321. - Pick several files for a script with `--chooser-file`. Press `v`, mark the items with `enter`, then press `e` to write their paths in panel order. Thanks @Abhirup0. - The size column now shows how many items a folder contains. To see its total size, press `m` to focus the metadata panel. Thanks @booth-w and @VXNCXNX. - Copying a symlink preserves the link, and compressing a dotfile such as `.env` produces `.env.zip`. Thanks @xelavopelk and @toller892. - If you use **cd on quit**, replace the function in your shell profile and reload it. The updated Bash, zsh, fish, and PowerShell functions ask `spf` where it writes the last directory. Thanks @VXNCXNX. [Shell setup](https://superfile.dev/configure/superfile-config/#cd_on_quit) - Try the two new Rosé Pine themes by setting `theme` to `rose-pine-dawn` or `rose-pine-moon`. Thanks @MJLiiii.  <details><summary>Theme screenshots</summary>  ![Rosé Pine ### 2026-11-18T10:03:02Z [**dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email**](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email)

```
## What's Changed  ### Features  - feat: |邮件转发| 规则新增收件人地址正则过滤，复用现有站内转发流程，支持任一或全部匹配 - feat: |邮箱登录| 保留会话 Token 查看入口并显示到期时间，密码模式隐藏旧凭据内容并禁止旧缓存切换，保留缓存删除功能 - feat: |邮箱会话| 将可续期的 30 天 JWT 签发扩展至创建邮箱及用户、管理员、Telegram 授权入口 - feat: |邮箱会话| 网页仅更新当前使用的续期 JWT，沿用本地字符串缓存，历史 token 过期后需重新登录；列表同时存在两种 Token 类型时，以独立列或标签展示登录方式 - feat: |管理后台| 支持切换单个或批量创建，批量创建最多 100 个随机名称邮箱，并导出自动登录链接 CSV（电子表格安全或无损格式） - feat: |管理统计| 新增 Dashboard，展示邮件、邮箱、用户和发件总数及 7 天、30 天活跃邮箱和用户数，支持按日期查看全站趋势 - feat: |邮件转发| 转发规则按配置域名复用站内收件流程，独立提取和推送且不再转发，自动回复仅执行一次；规则可选流程结束后删除原邮件，全局转发仅支持外部地址并保持原有方式 - feat: |用户系统| 新增 `DISABLE_USER_SYSTEM` 总开关，统一拦截用户接口、停用用户管理员令牌并隐藏前端用户界面 - feat: |邮箱会话| 密码登录开关生效时签发 30 天 JWT，支持邮箱鉴权及剩余不足 7 天时通过 settings 续期 - feat: |邮箱登录| 新增登录页仅显示密码登录的开关，仅在启用邮箱密码时生效；旧凭据、登录链接、API 访问和旧凭据 JWT 时效保持原有行为 - feat: |邮箱凭证| 自动登录链接折叠项新增二维码，方便手机扫码打开邮箱，并提示持有二维码者可访问该邮箱（issue #1189）  ### Bug Fixes  - fix: |邮箱登录| 登录策略加载后刷新邮箱切换列表，避免继续显示旧的登录方式 - fix: |验证码识别| 本地提取忽略无关邮件内容，减少误识别  - fix: |CI| 单测 Job 超时上限从 5 分钟调整为 15 分钟，给依赖安装预留时间 - fix: |站点认证| `PASSWORDS` 配置解析失败或不是字符串数组时拒绝访问，保留正常配置和空配置行为，避免误关闭站点密码保护 - fix: |CI| Webhook UI 测试等待保存和测试请求响应，避免读取空记录或旧请求导致偶发失败 - fix: |自动更新| 支持可选的 `UPSTREAM_SYNC_TOKEN` 同步 workflow 变更，未配置时保留默认 Token，并声明同步所需的内容写权限 - fix: |收件箱| 分页请求失败时保留当前邮件，并恢复页码和每页条数 - fix: |收件箱| 极简模式仅在成功取得邮件后提示刷新成功 - fix: |邮件阅读| 允许加载远程图片时，Shadow DOM 邮件预览仍会净化 HTML - fix: |健康检查| Worker 绑定 `ASSETS` 时 `/health_check` 仍返回 `OK`，不再返回前端页面，便于可用性监控 - fix: |创建邮箱| 随机生成名称时先转为小写再过滤字符，避免默认 `[^a-z0-9]` 规则删除每段名称的首字母导致名称残缺或过短 - fix: |邮件阅读| 为普通和全屏 iframe 邮件预览启用沙箱，禁止执行邮件正文中的脚本 - fix: |定时清理| 分别捕获各项内置清理和自定义 SQL 清理异常，记录错误并继续执行后续任务 - fix: |顶部导航| 为手机端页眉添加左右留白 - fix: |收件箱| 保留批量删除失败的勾选，仅在全部删除后提示成功 - fix: |邮件处理| metadata 更新和 Telegram 邮件链接改用本次入库 ID，避免重复或缺失 Message-ID 导致更新错记录或链接定位错误 - fix: |邮件阅读| 重置 Shadow 邮件继承的应用默认样式，使用浏览器普通文档的默认系统颜色并保留发件人配色 - fix: |Telegram| 管理员删除邮箱前清理当前推送用户的绑定及推送映射，释放名额并避免同名重建后继续推送；KV 清理失败时保留邮箱供重试，数据库删除失败时可能已解绑 - fix: |CI| 修复 WASM npm 包发布路径，避免本地 tarball 被误识别为 GitHub 仓库 - fix: |CI| E2E 使用测试镜像自带的工具探测服务，构建时无需访问 Ubuntu 软件源 - fix: |CI| 修复近期邮件清理测试在跨秒时偶发失败的问题 - fix: |CI| 等待转发设置保存后的刷新完成，避免浏览器测试偶发提交旧设置 - fix: |邮件解析| 升级 WASM 解析依赖，仅对结构化邮件头补充原始值，保留原有文本头解码行为，修复 `header.From` Webhook 过滤失效及移除附件后发件人丢失的问题 - fix: |邮件解析| 兼容 postal-mime v4 的首个头字段语义，备用解析支持空或缺失 From，移除附件时重新生成 MIME 类型与编码头，避免入库后正文丢失 - fix: |Telegram| 使用独立的永久内部绑定 token，兼容历史过期绑定；打开邮箱时签发邮箱凭据，阻止内部 token 用于邮箱 API - fix: |Worker| 未知邮件、未绑定地址及空地址清理改为分批处理，避免大量积压数据导致 D1 操作超时 - fix: |用户系统| 邮箱转移确认按钮绑定已有请求加载状态，修复 loading 变量未定义  ### Improvements  - perf: |CI| 复用 E2E 镜像，并行调度浏览器测试，减少页面重载、固定等待和重复的浏览器操作，加快 WASM 构建工具安装 - docs: |文档| 新增邮箱、用户中心与管理后台的登录入口说明，逐项列出 Worker 变量是否必填及默认行为并说明自定义角色，补充发信通道对比，并按兼容日期更新 Node.js 兼容配置步骤 - perf: |数据库| Schema 升级至 `v0.0.10`，为未知地址邮件清理增加覆盖索引、为域名清理增加表达式联合索引；按主、次、补丁版本逐段比较迁移版本，避免两位数版本误判及回退版本标记   - 在 Admin 清理设置中使用未知邮件清理或自定义来源域名清理时，可利用新增索引。   - 可通过 Admin 数据库页面升级至 v0.0.10，或执行[索引 SQL](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/blob/v1.14.0/db/2026-11-06-cleanup-indexes.sql)；仅新增索引，不修改邮件数据，不使用相关清理可暂缓添加。   - 减少相应清理查询的扫描量；本次新功能不需要增加数据字段。 - docs: |用户系统| 补充邮箱绑定、获取已绑定邮箱凭据及解绑 API 的双重鉴权说明和请求示例  - perf: |CI| 整理测试目录，独立 Webhook UI 功能组，与 API、其他浏览器测试、SMTP/IMAP 并行执行；仅启动各组所需服务，保留单元测试、WASM 测试与统一检查结果  <details> <summary>English</summary>  ### Features  - feat: |Mail Forwarding| Add recipient address regex filters to forwarding rules, reusing internal delivery with any/all matching - feat: |Mailbox Login| Keep session-token access with expiry details, hiding legacy credential contents and blocking legacy cache switches in password-only mode while retaining cache removal - feat: |Mailbox Sessions| Extend renewable 30-day JWT issuance to mailbox creation and authorized user, admin, and Telegram access - feat: |Mailbox Sessions| Update only the active mailbox JWT on renewal, retaining the existing string cache; expired historical tokens require login; show login method columns or tags only when both token types appear in the list - feat: |Admin| Switch between single and batch creation, create batches of up to 100 random-name mailboxes, and export their auto-login links as spreadsheet-safe or lossless CSV - feat: |Admin Statistics| Add a dashboard with mail, mailbox, user and sent mail totals, 7- and 30-day mailbox and user activity, and site-wide trends by date - feat: |Mail Forwarding| Route forwarding rules internally by configured domain with independent receive processing, extraction and notifications, no second forwarding and only one auto-reply; optionally delete the original at the end and keep global forwarding external-only with its existing behavior - feat: |User System| Add `DISABLE_USER_SYSTEM` to block user APIs, disable user admin tokens, and hide user interfaces - feat: |Mailbox Sessions| Issue 30-day JWTs at password login when password-only mode is effective, validate mailbox access, and renew through settings below 7 days - feat: |Mailbox Login| Add a password-only login-page switch that requires mailbox passwords; preserve existing credentials, login links, API access, and legacy JWT lifetimes - feat: |Mailbox Credential| Show a QR code of the auto-login link in the credential dialog for opening the mailbox on mobile, with a reminder that anyone with the QR code can access the mailbox (issue #1189)  ### Bug Fixes  - fix: |Mailbox Login| Refresh the mailbox picker after login policy loads instead of retaining stale login methods - fix: |Code Extraction| Skip unrelated mail content during local extraction to reduce false positives  - fix: |CI| Increase the unit-test job timeout from 5 to 15 minutes to allow time for dependency installation - fix: |Site Authentication| Fail closed for malformed or non-string-array `PASSWORDS` configurations while preserving valid and empty configuration behavior - fix: |CI| Wait for save and test responses in Webhook UI tests to avoid flaky assertions against missing or stale requests - fix: |Auto Update| Support an optional `UPSTREAM_SYNC_TOKEN` to sync workflow changes, retain the default token when unset, and declare the contents write permission required for syncing - fix: |Inbox| Keep the current message and restore the page and page size when pagination fails - fix: |Inbox| Show simple inbox refresh success only after a successful fetch - fix: |Mail Reader| Sanitize Shadow DOM email previews when remote images are allowed - fix: |Health Check| Return `OK` from `/health_check` when the Worker has an `ASSETS` binding instead of the frontend page, so uptime monitors can use it - fix: |Create Address| Lowercase generated names before filtering characters, so the default `[^a-z0-9]` rule no longer drops the first letter of each name part and leaves names mangled or too short - fix: |Mail Reader| Sandbox regular and fullscreen iframe previews to block scripts in email bodies - fix: |Scheduled Cleanup| Catch errors for each built-in and custom SQL cleanup task, log failures, and continue with subsequent tasks - fix: |Header| Add horizontal spacing to the phone header - fix: |Inbox| Keep failed batch-delete selections and show success only when the batch completes - fix: |Mail Processing| Use the newly stored row ID for metadata updates and Telegram links, avoiding wrong-row updates or links when Message-ID is repeated or missing - fix: |Mail Reader| Reset inherited application defaults in Shadow mail and use browser-default system colors for a normal document while preserving sender colors - fix: |Telegram| Clean up the current push recipient's binding and push mapping before admin mailbox deletion, freeing a slot and preventing stale pushes on recreation; KV cleanup failures preserve the mailbox for retry, while database deletion failures may leave it unbound - fix: |CI| Fix WASM npm publishing paths so local tarballs are not interpreted as GitHub repositories - fix: |CI| Use the E2E image's built-in tools for service probes so its build does not need Ubuntu package mirrors - fix: |CI| Prevent intermittent failures in the recent-mail cleanup test when it crosses a second boundary - fix: |CI| Wait for the forwarding settings refresh after saving so browser tests do not intermittently submit stale settings - fix: |Mail Parser| Upgrade WASM parser dependencies and use raw values only for structured headers, preserving decoded text headers while fixing `header.From` webhook filters and sender loss after attachment removal - fix: |Mail Parser| Support postal-mime v4's first-header semantics: handle empty/missing From in fallback parsing and regenerate MIME type/encoding headers when removing attachments to preserve stored message bodies - fix: |Telegram| Store separate permanent internal binding tokens, preserve historical expired bindings, and issue mailbox credentials on access while rejecting internal tokens at mailbox APIs - fix: |Worker| Process unknown-mail, unbound-address, and empty-address cleanup in bounded batches to avoid D1 timeouts on large backlogs - fix: |User| Bind the mailbox transfer confirmation button to the existing request loading state, fixing the undefined loading variable  ### Improvements  - perf: |CI| Reuse E2E images, schedule browser tests in parallel, reduce page reloads, fixed waits and repeated browser actions, and speed up WASM tool installation - docs: |Documentation| Add guidance for mailbox, User Center, and Admin Console login entry points; list each Worker variable's required status and default behavior; explain custom roles; compare sending channels; and update Node.js compatibility steps by compatibility date - perf: |Database| Upgrade the schema to `v0.0.10` with a covering index for unknown-mail cleanup and an expression-based composite index for domain cleanup; compare major, minor, and patch versions numerically to avoid two-digit version errors and version-marker downgrades   - Use unknown-mail cleanup or custom sender-domain cleanup in Admin cleanup settings to benefit from the new indexes.   - Upgrade to v0.0.10 through the Admin database page or apply the [index SQL](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/blob/v1.14.0/db/2026-11-06-cleanup-indexes.sql); this only adds indexes, does not alter mail data, and can be deferred if these cleanup paths are unused.   - Reduce scans for the corresponding cleanup queries; the new features in this release require no additional data columns. - docs: |User| Document mailbox binding, bound mailbox credential retrieval, and unbinding APIs with dual authentication requirements and request examples  - perf: |CI| Organize test suites and run a dedicated Webhook UI group alongside API, other browser tests and SMTP/IMAP, with only each group's required services, separate unit/WASM tests and one aggregate check  </details>  ### [更新或者部署网页不生效请如图勾选清理缓存](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/discussions/487)  <details> <summary>PRs</summary>  * fix: bind mailbox transfer button loading state by ### 2026-11-23T08:56:18Z [**labring/FastGPT**](https://github.com/labring/FastGPT)

```
## 升级指南  ### 镜像更新  - FastGPT 主服务使用 `ghcr.io/labring/fastgpt:v4.17.5`。 - FastGPT 商业版使用 `ghcr.io/labring/fastgpt-pro:v4.17.5`。  ## 安全修复  1. 修复对话 HTML 在新标签页预览时的安全隔离问题。 2. 修复从根目录移动应用、知识库、技能及知识库集合时的权限校验遗漏。  ## 🐛 修复  1. 修复非 root 用户在主页对话或仅有读取权限的应用中，已读回复仍显示为未读的问题。 2. 修复在工具文件夹中创建 HTTP 工具时的权限校验错误。 3. 修复循环或并行执行中，结束节点引用失效导致工作流详情页白屏、聚合输出类型未重置的问题。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/labring/FastGPT/compare/v4.17.4...v4.17.5) 
```

### 2026-11-19T12:48:43Z [**jason5ng32/MyIP**](https://github.com/jason5ng32/MyIP)

## What's Changed * Added Snapshot History: the homepage test results are saved per visit in this browser, so you can compare any two snapshots and see what differs * SpeedTest becomes more informative and shows more details * Connectivity: in single-refresh mode, you can set to discard the first result so the latency leaves out DNS lookup and connection setup * IP History is retired; Snapshot History now covers its role * Fixed some issues  ## New Contributors * @PRASTUTI0725 made their first contribution in https://github.com/jason5ng32/MyIP/pull/520 * @shaktimaan00 made their first contribution in https://github.com/jason5ng32/MyIP/pull/562  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/jason5ng32/MyIP/compare/v8.1.0...v8.2.0 
```

### 2026-11-20T13:44:18Z [**gitbutlerapp/gitbutler**](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler)

```
## Changes  - Linux: GitButler is now on [Flathub](https://flathub.org/apps/com.gitbutler.gitbutler) as an official, verified package, with the native app included  ## Fixes  - Windows: A second amend, reword or move into the same commit no longer fails with "Failed to find commit … in rebase editor" (a regression from 0.23.3) - Windows: Committed symlinks now work on Linux and macOS - GitHub: Pushing the rest of a stack after several of its branches merge together no longer fails with "There are no new commits between base branch …" - Deleting your last applied branch no longer detaches `HEAD` when `main` has moved on, which blocked Update and `but pull` - Loading a large project far behind its upstream no longer takes gigabytes of memory  ## CLI  - Adds `but worktree recycle`, which reuses a clean archived worktree for a new branch and keeps its build output and dependencies  ## Native app  Highlights from the native app. New installs open it by default; everyone else can turn it on (or off) under "Experimental" in settings:  - You can now pick separate themes for light and dark mode - The app reopens where you left off after an update, quit or crash - "Open on GitHub" (or GitLab, Bitbucket, Azure) opens the repository in your browser - Dragging a branch between two stacks makes it a stack of its own - The navigator now shows each branch's commits by default - Branches with an open pull request no longer offer a local "Merge into …" - Common errors now explain the fix instead of showing raw Git output - Light mode is brighter, and controls match the rest of GitButler's design - Windows: Chinese, Japanese and Korean text no longer shows as boxes - Repositories with thousands of open pull requests no longer keep the CPU busy  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/compare/release/0.23.4...release/0.23.5  ## Downloads  Download bins from https://gitbutler.com/ 
```

### 2026-11-19T07:44:59Z [**hiddify/hiddify-app**](https://github.com/hiddify/hiddify-app)

```
# Highlights - **A smarter Keep top.** Only the fastest proxies run and the others are parked. A kept proxy   that stops answering is replaced, and the parked ones are checked again every hour. Client   endpoints, WARP included, take part too. - **21 new languages.** Nine more translations are now complete. - **Lighter on CPU and battery.** Animations stop when nobody can see them. The connect button's   light costs much less to draw. The core uses only as many processors as its load needs and   paces the reads of busy connections. - **OpenVPN profiles.** The app reads `.ovpn` files and `ovpn://` links. - **Psiphon gets through more often.** As the first hop, Psiphon scans CDN edges with Chrome's   TLS handshake and connects through the ones that answer. You can change its country from the   home page. - **WARP keeps searching, and can search deeper.** WARP searches for up to 3 minutes and learns   which noise and tunnel work on each network. When a WARP or Psiphon search finds nothing, the   app offers a deep search. - **Proxies that are not secure are marked.** A proxy that anyone on the way can read gets a red   *Not secure* mark that explains why. The list shows each proxy's transport and security, such   as `VLESS · XHTTP · REALITY`. - **Subscriptions download on hard networks.** The core downloads them with Chrome's TLS   handshake. When a download fails, the app says why and what to try. - **The desktop core runs in a process of its own.** The app's core is a library in the app, and   only the proxies run in the engine. If the engine crashes, the app stays open, shows why it   crashed and recovers. - **VPN mode by default on desktop.** When VPN mode needs administrator permission, the app asks   with a clear choice. On Linux it works without pkexec, and it also works in the AppImage. - **Router mode on Linux.** A transparent proxy for a whole network. - **Linux arm64, RPM and one install script.** There are deb, rpm and AppImage builds for x64 and   arm64. A single `install.sh` for macOS and Linux verifies what it downloads. - **Report a problem from the app.** A report carries every log and the crash. It can first turn   on 10 minutes of detailed logging. The core encrypts the report, and you send it by Telegram   or email, or keep it on your device. - **A beta channel.** GitHub pre-releases go only to users who join the beta channel. The update   dialog shows the change log, and every download is checked against its SHA-256 before it is   installed. - **A newer core.** xray-core v26.11.9, a Chrome 155 fingerprint, and ECH that works with every   fingerprint. Fake IPs survive a restart of the core. Many crash and hang fixes for XHTTP, WARP,   Psiphon, AmneziaWG and WireGuard. - **Steadier on every platform.** When the core stops by itself, Android, iOS and macOS take the   VPN down cleanly and say why, and a desktop VPN service that hangs has its engine stopped.   Android gives the core a quarter of   the device's RAM, so connections no longer lag. The Quick toggle and always-on VPN start the   core properly.  ## Downloads  **Download based on your OS:**  **بر اساس سیستم عامل خود دانلود کنید:**  <!-- ASSETS:BEGIN --> <table> ## API  ### 🚀 Features  - **api-service:** log subscriber hashes signed with API keys that have to be rotated fixes NV-9508 ([#13392](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13392)) - **api-service,shared:** add a flag that stops old API keys from signing subscriber hashes fixes NV-9508 ([#13391](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13391)) - **api-service:** log calls made with API keys that have to be rotated fixes NV-9508 ([#13384](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13384)) - **api-service,dashboard:** add an API key revocation policy fixes NV-9508 ([#13377](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13377)) - **api-service:** mint subscriber login tokens with POST /v2/subscribers/:subscriberId/tokens fixes NV-9422 ([#13345](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13345)) - **api-service,dashboard:** block enabling workflows over the active workflow limit (NV-9495) ([#13364](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13364)) - **api-service,dashboard,dal,framework,shared:** agent access rules on every inbound event fixes NV-9430 ([#13341](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13341)) - **api-service,shared,root:** add AWS Bedrock support for the Copilot behind IS_COPILOT_BEDROCK_ENABLED fixes NV-9377 ([#13291](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13291)) - **api-service:** split long Telegram agent replies fixes NV-9395 ([#13342](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13342)) - **api-service:** set the WhatsApp business profile from the relay agent fixes NV-9477 ([#13336](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13336)) - **api-service:** sign subscriber tokens with a rotatable ES256 key set fixes NV-9421 ([#13308](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13308)) - **api-service,dashboard,dal,shared:** read v2 workflow runs from notifications and step_runs_v2 fixes NV-9343 ([#13210](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13210)) - **api-service,worker,dal,shared:** write job status changes to step_runs_v2 fixes NV-9311 ([#13205](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13205)) - **api-service,worker,dal,shared:** store trigger overrides once per workflow run fixes NV-9310 ([#13179](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13179)) - **api-service,dashboard,shared:** WhatsApp as an Agent Inbox channel fixes NV-9399 ([#13324](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13324)) - **api-service:** record subscriber session auth method and SDK per environment fixes NV-9419 ([#13309](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13309))  ### 🩹 Fixes  - **api-service:** refuse Inbox deletes of channel connections the caller does not own ([#13420](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13420)) - **api-service:** trust cf-connecting-ip by default on Novu Cloud fixes NV-9517 ([#13416](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13416)) - **root:** move first-party code to zod 4.6.6 and support zod 4 in framework fixes NV-9162 ([#13389](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13389)) - **providers,api-service:** always validate outbound webhook destinations fixes NV-8583 ([#12320](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/12320)) - **api-service:** resolve the client IP from one trusted header fixes NV-9517 ([#13380](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13380)) - **api-service,dashboard:** extend the API key revocation policy to subscriber hashes fixes NV-9508 ([#13382](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13382)) - **shared:** sign bridge requests with every active secret key fixes NV-9458 ([#13312](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13312)) - **api-service:** skip the error response when the response was already sent fixes NV-9497 ([#13367](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13367)) - **api-service:** let every role read GET /users/me under RBAC fixes NV-9483 ([#13348](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13348)) - **api-service:** key inbound edits by Chat SDK editedAt fixes NV-9467 ([#13338](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13338))  ### ❤️ Thank You  - Nikita Grossman @nikitagrossman - Pawan Jain @jainpawan21 - Paweł Tymczuk @LetItRock  ## Dashboard  ### 🚀 Features  - **api-service,dashboard:** add an API key revocation policy fixes NV-9508 ([#13377](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13377)) - **api-service,dashboard:** block enabling workflows over the active workflow limit (NV-9495) ([#13364](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13364)) - **api-service,dashboard,dal,framework,shared:** agent access rules on every inbound event fixes NV-9430 ([#13341](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13341)) - **api-service,dashboard,dal,shared:** read v2 workflow runs from notifications and step_runs_v2 fixes NV-9343 ([#13210](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13210))  ### 🩹 Fixes  - **dashboard:** animate the Copilot launcher between pill and circle fixes NV-9506 ([#13374](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13374)) - **dashboard:** show the Copilot canvas loading bar in popover mode fixes NV-9500 ([#13371](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13371)) - **dashboard:** animate the Copilot popover open and close fixes NV-9503 ([#13372](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13372)) - **dashboard,shared:** remove IS_EU agents gate and pass --region eu to novu connect fixes NV-9493 ([#13357](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13357)) - **dashboard:** open the publish modal's changes tree collapsed fixes NV-9470 ([#13334](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13334)) - **dashboard:** keep job overrides fallback when resending older activity runs ([#13335](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13335)) - **dashboard:** stop agent channel setup crashing when it shows a QR code fixes NV-9463 ([#13326](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13326))  ### ❤️ Thank You  - Pawan Jain @jainpawan21 - Paweł Tymczuk @LetItRock  ## Worker  ### 🚀 Features  - **worker:** name standard worker transactions by job type ([68623f5f84](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/commit/68623f5f84)) - **api-service,worker,dal,shared:** write job status changes to step_runs_v2 fixes NV-9311 ([#13205](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13205)) - **api-service,worker,dal,shared:** store trigger overrides once per workflow run fixes NV-9310 ([#13179](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13179))  ### 🩹 Fixes  - **worker:** send WhatsApp when an override recipient is set without a subscriber phone fixes NV-9530 ([#13413](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13413)) - **shared:** sign bridge requests with every active secret key fixes NV-9458 ([#13312](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13312)) - **worker,providers:** remove FCM tokens rejected as "Device unregistered" fixes NV-8760 ([#13361](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13361)) - **worker:** await outbound Svix webhooks again in send-message steps fixes NV-9484 ([#13353](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13353)) - **worker:** stub stepRunV2Writer in RunJob spec to stop worker unit tests OOM ([#13328](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13328))  ### 🔥 Performance  - **worker:** cut DB and network waits from channel step execution fixes NV-9484 ([#13347](https://github.com/novuhq/novu/pull/13347))  ### ❤️ Thank You  - Claude Fable 5.2 - Claude Opus 5.5 - Cursor Agent @cursoragent - Dima Grossman @scopsy - Himanshu Garg @merrcury - Nikita Grossman @nikitagrossman - Pawan Jain @jainpawan21  ## WS  This was a version bump only for @novu/ws to align it with other projects, there were no code changes.  ## 🐳 Community Docker Images Community Docker images are now available for v3.21.0, making it easy to deploy in containerized environments.   ## 📦 SDKs Server side SDKs(Typescript, Golang, Python, PHP, C-Sharp and Java) and Inbox SDKs (JS, React, Next.js, and React Native) for v3.21.0 are available to help you integrate with the platform using your preferred programming language. These SDKs are maintained alongside the core release and community images to ensure compatibility and a smooth upgrade experience.

- 支持自选译文样式快捷轮换，原文与译文均支持高亮显示收藏生词。 - 为本地版面分析开放 WebGPU 加速，服务能力测试增加耗时显示。 - 统一插件预定义模型与自定义型号规则，并在服务配置中隐藏 Origin 重写域名。 - 修复流式片段渲染中的富文本占位符，移除触屏段落翻译底部状态条，并防止 YouTube 原生字幕窗口重新出现。 - 油猴脚本允许连接 InfiniCLOUD WebDAV。

### 2026-11-20T04:58:59Z [**gyroflow/gyroflow**](https://github.com/gyroflow/gyroflow)

```
### ✨ Improvements - **Canon Cinema RAW Light (.crm) playback** - CRM clips now open and play directly in Gyroflow, a playable copy is no longer needed. The Canon RAW SDK is downloaded the first time you open one (Windows x64, macOS, Linux x64). As with BRAW, rendering them in the app isn't available: export a project file and use one of the video editor plugins - Added "Default CLI behavior" in `Advanced`: what Gyroflow does with the files it's started with, e.g. from the "Open with" menu or when they're dropped on its icon. It can render them (as before), open them in the app, or add them to the render queue. A command line with any option (e.g. `-f`) isn't affected - GoPro HERO4 Black/Silver: the lens profile is now selected automatically from the clip's field of view (Wide or Medium), for use with external motion data. HERO3 files are now recognized as GoPro (by @anton-belkin) - GMFlow (AI): stability fixes on some GPUs, and less time spent tuning for the GPU  ### 🐛 Bug fixes - Fixed the horizon drifting with horizon lock in clips with a lot of motion, e.g. aerobatic flights, FPV and skiing (regression in 1.7.0) (#1287) - Fixed a tilted horizon with horizon lock on cameras with a large gyro bias, e.g. some Sony a7 IV (#1298) - Fixed the picture jumping a few frames before the end of a trim range (or after its start), also in the rendered video - Fixed rendering to a network drive (SMB) on macOS (#1291) - Fixed the preview being very pixelated (stuck at 32x32 px) on some Macs - Fixed a crash after switching away from a BRAW file - Fixed a crash on Linux when showing text that needs a fallback font, e.g. the language list in `Advanced` - Fixed the IMU orientation of the Insta360 X6, and of the Insta360 ONE RS with the 360 lens modules (1-Inch 360 Edition, 360 Lens) - Fixed lens profiles with large tangential distortion terms (OpenCV standard and Insta360 models): the corners of wide lenses were off by up to hundreds of pixels, which affected "Lens correction" below 100% - Fixed per-frame lens data being ignored in files that don't state the sensor size, e.g. Gyroflow Protobuf files without the pixel pitch  ### 📦 SDK Updates: - FFmpeg is now 9.1 - Added Canon RAW SDK 2.10.0 (downloaded on first use)  [Microsoft Store](https://apps.microsoft.com/store/detail/gyroflow/9NZG7T0JCG9H) | [macOS/iOS App Store](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/gyroflow/id6447994244) | [Google Play](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=xyz.gyroflow)

### 2026-11-23T10:05:23Z [**lollipopkit/flutter_server_box**](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)

```
### Added  - **MCP server in the app and on Monitor:** expose ServerBox hosts and settings through a local MCP server in the app, and expose an authenticated MCP endpoint on Monitor with scoped API tokens and per-IP / per-token rate limiting. - **Expanded BMC (Redfish) management:** read and clear event logs, inspect drives and predictive failure status, set boot device overrides, mount or eject virtual media, and toggle the locator LED. - **SSH agent and jump-host multiplexing:** authenticate using keys from the system `ssh-agent`, forward the agent to terminal sessions, and connect through a jump host's existing OpenSSH ControlMaster. - **Agent secret redaction:** automatically redact sensitive tokens, keys and passwords before the Agent sends context to a model. - **Monitor fallback address and per-device metric history:** configure a fallback address for Monitor connections, and query per-device metrics history over custom time ranges. - **Server status sharing and swap breakdown:** share a server's live status as a poster image or formatted text, and inspect each swap area's size, usage and priority individually.  ### Changed  - **macOS builds are now unsandboxed only:** Mac App Store builds and sandbox compatibility branches have been removed. - **iOS local Linux environment** migrated to the new userland engine (`iul`). - **Unified UI interactions:** dialogs replaced with bottom sheets across the app, a single server picker with search, tags and sorting used everywhere, and the bottom bar adapts the number of visible tabs to the window width. - **Agent chat:** tool calls now fold neatly with improved scroll following. - Older app builds can now open local storage databases written by newer schema steps they understand. - Upgraded to Flutter 3.50.  ### Fixed  - Read CPU temperatures from `hwmon` in addition to thermal zones. - Keep Agent replies intact across dropped connections and request iOS background execution time while a reply is streaming. - Deduplicate virtual media slots when a Redfish service lists the same slot twice. - Align server list titles properly with non-square distribution marks. - Prevent background sync and connection teardown from blocking UI dismissal. - Keep the theme background steady beneath pages during route transitions. - Respect the wake-lock setting in terminal sessions.  中文更新日志：https://serverbox.lollipopkit.com/docs/zh/changelog/  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box/compare/v1.0.1925...v1.0.1962 
```

### 2026-11-23T14:33:19Z [**tw93/Pake**](https://github.com/tw93/Pake)

<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.21.0</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. A link in a mail to another site's sign-in page, such as `github.com/login` or a `/auth/verify` link, now opens in the default browser instead of replacing the page, as it did in an Outlook web mail app. A site's own sign-in still stays in the app. 2. A Cmd-click on macOS, a Ctrl-click on Windows and Linux, or a middle-click opens the link in the default browser, as in a browser. 3. Ctrl+Shift+H and the Home menu item leave a window the page opened alone, so a sign-in popup is never sent to the app's home page halfway through. 4. The runtime moves to Tauri 2.12.7, Wry 0.57.3 and url 2.5.9, which fixes RUSTSEC-2026-0592, where a nested `blob:` address could abort an app while downloading. 5. The CLI now needs Node.js 22.12 or later.  ### 更新日志  1. 邮件里指向别的网站登录页的链接，比如 `github.com/login` 或 `/auth/verify` 这类，现在会在默认浏览器里打开，不再像之前在 Outlook 网页邮箱应用里那样把页面整个换掉，网站自己的登录还是留在应用里。 2. macOS 上按住 Command 点、Windows 和 Linux 上按住 Ctrl 点，或者用中键点链接，会和浏览器一样在默认浏览器里打开。 3. Ctrl+Shift+H 和菜单里的回到首页不再作用于网页自己打开的窗口，登录弹窗不会在登录到一半时被带回应用首页。 4. 运行时升到 Tauri 2.12.7、Wry 0.57.3 和 url 2.5.9，修复了 RUSTSEC-2026-0592，之前嵌套的 `blob:` 地址可能在下载时让应用直接退出。 5. CLI 现在需要 Node.js 22.12 或更高版本。  If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.  ### Pake App  If you would rather click than type commands, [Pake App](https://pake.run/) makes apps from a ready-made gallery or any URL on Mac, Windows and Linux, with no Node.js or Rust to install, and adds ad blocking and updates for the apps you made. You can [download it here](https://pake.run/). The CLI stays free and open source.  > https://github.com/tw93/Pake

### 2026-11-22T22:59:48Z [**BurntSushi/ripgrep**](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep)

```
[Sponsorship is appreciated!](https://github.com/sponsors/BurntSushi/)  > In case you haven't heard of it before, ripgrep is a line-oriented search > tool that recursively searches the current directory for a regex pattern. > By default, ripgrep will respect gitignore rules and automatically skip > hidden files/directories and binary files.  ripgrep 16 is a new major version release of ripgrep that mostly has bug fixes, some minor performance improvements and a small number of new features. Here are some highlights:  * There is now an `--in` flag for telling which files ripgrep should search by pointing to another containing line delimited file paths. (There is also a corresponding `--in0` flag for NUL delimited file paths.) * ripgrep now has a `-G/--glob-not` flag, which is recommended in lieu of using `!` to negate a glob. That is, `-g '!*.toml'` can now be spelled as `-G '*.toml'`. * ripgrep now respects a new `RIPGREP_THREADS` environment variable for setting the default number of threads to use for parallel searches. Whenever `-j/--threads` is specified on the command line (or in a configuration file), it takes precedence over the environment variable. * `riscv64` has been re-added to ripgrep's release pipeline. Binaries can now be downloaded for that target.  Enhancements:  * [#3522](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3522): Add new `-G/--glob-not` flag for negating `-g/--glob`. * [#3425](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3425), [#3622](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3622), [#3511](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3511): Add nushell completions via `--generate complete-nushell` (#3425). * [#3255](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3255): Add Zed hyperlink alias. * [#2962](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/2962): Add POSIX character class support in bracket expressions. * [#3035](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3035): Add `--max-bytes` flag to limit bytes per file. * [#273](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/273), [#3459](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3459): Add --in/--in0 flags for reading file paths. * [#3317](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3317), [#3415](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3415): Support RIPGREP_THREADS environment variable. * [#3620](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3620), [#3617](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3617): Delay-load userenv.dll on Windows MSVC.  Bug fixes:  * [#3609](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3609), [#3603](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3603): Reject partial path components when stripping Unix prefixes. * [#3605](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3605), [#3604](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3604): Continue reading ignore files after invalid UTF-8 lines. * [#3607](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3607), [#3614](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3614): Statically link binary on Windows ARM64/MSVC (#3607). * [#3593](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3593): `-v -m N -A k` printing the rest of the input as matches. * [#3581](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3581), [#1764](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/1764): `-g !DIRNAME/` couldn't match when the CWD is `DIRNAME`. * [#2552](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/2552): Don't report ignored broken symlinks when following links. * [#3577](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3577): Hostname buffer size at HOST_NAME_MAX. * [#3382](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3382): Preserve buffered matches after parallel search errors. * [#3526](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3526): Hyperlink paths that are not valid UTF-8. * [#2745](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/2745): Exclude path separator from character classes. * [#3534](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3534): Detect symlink loops above the search root. * [#3617](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3617): Don't compare against stdout when it's a pipe on Windows. * [#2958](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/2958): Sanitize paths by removing control codes in output. * [#3527](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3527): Treat lone `!` gitignore line as a no-op. * [#3484](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3484): Separate Scheme from Lisp file types. * [#3342](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3342): Detect compressed files by magic number, not just extension. * [#3477](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3477): Parse trailing spaces correctly in gitignore patterns. * [#1983](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/1983), [#3364](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3364): `--json` should emit multiple matches in multiline mode. * [#3222](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3222): Separate flags from paths in decompression commands. * [#3503](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3503): Fall back past MAX_LOOK_AHEAD in multi-line match discovery.  Documentation:  * [#3598](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3598): Clarify PATH environment variable in flag documentation. * [#3551](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3551): Note that nesting `{...}` alternates is allowed. * [#3538](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3538): Document git divergence in matched_path_or_any_parents. * [#3243](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3243): Emit .TP for each flag entry to generate linkable anchors. * [#3500](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3500): Clarify that the `-r/--replace` flag is not recursive search. * [#3323](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3323): Remove `$` prompt symbols from README code blocks. * [#3448](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3448): Update guide examples for a more recent release.  Performance:  * [#3597](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3597), [#3594](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3594): Skip path resolution when hyperlinks are disabled. * [#2832](https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/2832): Honor custom line terminators in the regex engine.
