### 2026-11-18T08:46:04Z [**charmbracelet/bubbletea**](https://github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea)

```
# Calmer, cooler, sturdier 🧋  A patch release, but it's a good one! Idle Bubble Tea programs now really sit idle, the terminal gets restored after just about anything goes wrong, and a long list of renderer bugs is gone.  ## Rest light!  The renderer no longer redraws when there's nothing to draw. After about half a second without changes, the frame ticker stops, and it picks right back up the moment something does change. Views that are empty or taller than the screen also skip redraws when they haven't changed.  ## Restoring in many more cases!  Bubble Tea now cleans up properly when:  - A command in `Batch` or `Sequence` panics - `Run` returns early with an error - `RestoreTerminal` swaps the input reader - `Run` finishes and the TTY files it opened for input need closing  And when you come back from suspending a program, Unicode mode comes back with it, so wide characters and emoji line up just like before!  ## Smoother rendering  - Resizing the terminal no longer flashes a stale frame. - The view's height is right from the very first frame. - Lines printed above your view keep their exact width, and they're measured the same way as the rest of your program. - Running a subprocess with `Exec` clears your view first, so it doesn't leave a ghost behind.  Big thanks to everyone who contributed to this release, and especially to the many first-time contributors! 💘  Read more below! 👇🏻   Have a nice day, Charm XX 🌟   ## Changelog ### Fixed * 9806b0c763fb391cc2c4ffbb3aa243b901a812ef: fix(exec): clear View before releasing terminal for subprocess (#1762) (@zhaoxinyi02) * 418cada8885b84de11160f103534a5bba0fd2d5d: fix(logging): preserve Unicode prefix spacing (#1759) (@Ricardo-M-L) * 94d45c807dde21d7e46d14f3916ff4e3fac437f8: fix(renderer): prevent emitting stale frame on resize (#1856) (@Guurdeep) * 4853fdaaa066e5d2fe0f93de543020eddd660e18: fix(renderer): skip redraws when empty or tall views haven't changed (#1897) (@janearc) * 6b30ecf3280d88d949dcc916bb91fc5e9d56f667: fix(renderer): use view height before first flush (#1766) (@fzlzjerry) * 55ba0f6de3ba6348597360690c008f7528d5813f: fix: allow Program.Run to be called again after the program exits (#1890) (@simpleqt) * 7a446b73f6f1cae03a91160e97e040bce334beba: fix: avoid blocking prints after program exit (#1758) (@Ricardo-M-L) * 1b8005fc10b235970f0414788339b18bcb6d6885: fix: avoid terminal wait without input (#1829) (@lowgame) * 13fb42d8ea1ac91e7eec2bd6717922802c1a0f48: fix: close the TTY files opened for input when Run returns (#1855) (@drawmeanelephant) * 56a2fe7fed8ae475f315da0491fe4ce0c03b7041: fix: close the replaced cancel reader on RestoreTerminal (#1821) (@drmzperx) * 18d6fa3b34c84f3b773cd307a5d7b0a56b12c72a: fix: close trace file after program run (#1844) (@lowgame) * 5982a61ef5e5d078255b2a67c45fd182ff54ec3c: fix: data race with Kill() and Run() (#1774) (@andrinoff) * f3d74b724c7d8f2dc5a9d272f12e4942daaa1817: fix: ensure terminal state is restored after panics in Batch/Sequence commands (#1818) (@dajiaohuang) * b9e7454fd49640ed1eac9c29dc59d2ec86463ae2: fix: make Quit a no-op before Run starts (#1840) (@tianrking) * df137dee9a2e6fc009cd2d837d0b0dae68b23c0e: fix: measure insertAbove lines with the active width method (#1861) (@Sanjays2402) * bd09ae6834335499ac8422be16ff4215050ca94e: fix: preserve exact-width lines printed above the view (#1888) (@imharjot) * 12b7b4d0eb0874e0f63d949ac33fd2d9022baa77: fix: restore terminal state on Run error paths (#1854) (@drawmeanelephant) * 8675fd8a858d4d82766509eb1aaffcd80085881b: fix: restore unicode mode on resume (#1896) (@janearc) ### Other stuff * 73b79b3737b9a68c76f3f2e6c49bf1bae327ee96: Provide the platform input/signal stubs on wasip1/wasip2 (#1767) (@calvinrp) * e4d617e7e0c0dd595cc553a856236221c678470f: perf(renderer): stop the frame ticker while nothing changes (#1832) (@viktordanov) * 38ddf5f58cfb4d14bfe607bcac5af502ab9ed6d6: v2.1.2 (@andrinoff)  ---  <a href="https://charm.land/"><img alt="The Charm logo" src="https://stuff.charm.sh/charm-banner-next.jpg" width="400"></a>  Thoughts? Questions? We love hearing from you. Feel free to reach out on [X](https://x.com/charmcli), [Discord](https://charm.land/discord), [Slack](https://charm.land/slack), [The Fediverse](https://mastodon.social/@charmcli), [Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/charm.land).
```

### 2026-11-23T08:27:04Z [**FreshRSS/FreshRSS**](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS)

```
* [Milestone](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/milestones/1.31.0)  This is a security-oriented major release with several critical security patches.  Our rolling-release channel (*edge*) is still recommended for faster security patches.  Breaking change 💥: * Require PHP 8.2+ (drop PHP 8.1) * Replace bcrypt challenge auth with direct password comparison. We are dropping a custom client-side crypto that avoided transmitting the login password in plain-text, in favour of HTTPS. The custom crypto was a design choice made when HTTPS was rare for self-hosted solutions. But this had other problems and did not protect the session anyway. We will now simplify and rely on HTTPS only.  Feature highlights✨: * Native HTML5 parsing (only if PHP 8.4+) as well as native img lazyload, for better compliance, security, and performance * User queries can define their own sorting criterion (field and direction)  Security highlights 🛡: * Fix several critical vulnerabilities, both local and remote * Fix security exploits in search queries * Use HTTP header `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin` * Use HTTP header `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` more generally * WebSub implement support for `hub.secret` * Docker images updated regarding the security of Apache, PHP, cURL  Improved performance 🏎️: * SQL speed improvement for batch mark as read under SQLite and PostgreSQL  Bug fixes highlights 🐛: * Fix HTTP Retry-After cache when proxy is used * Fix 32-bit systems * Fix API lookup of the localised default category * Etc.  This release has been made by @Alkarex, @Frenzie, @iatman, @IEEE-754, @Inverle, @Ricardo-Simoes, @sad270, @shcheglovnd, @spa77k,  and newcomers @A-AL-ANAZI, @benlk, @Busy-SweetPotato, @CaoMeiYouRen, @CharoenwitKunna, @ChrisB85, @CyberNativeAI, @darodi, @EduardoNovato, ### 2026-11-25T06:55:20Z [**byoungd/up**](https://github.com/byoungd/up)
```

```
# v1.1.0 — Still Here: A Field Guide to Beginning Again  《人生进阶指南：一个普通人的失败、恢复与重新学习》第一版。  This release re-centres the book on failure, depression, recovery, and relearning. The prologue now opens the book; the memoir and recovery arc becomes **Part I: Return to Life**; English becomes **Part II: Learning Again, Opening Input**, the first training ground rather than the whole book. Three chapters that were not book material — the author's product disclosure, the book's own case study, and the B2B resource-layer chapter — move to the **Case Studies and Author Practice** appendix.  ## What is inside  - **88 published chapters** in each language (was 81): new copyright, who-it-is-for, roadmap, sources, bibliography, reading-map and practice-note pages, plus a reordered six-part manuscript. - **中文版**：455 页 PDF · 88 章 EPUB 3.3 · 中文为主版本。 - **English edition**：573 页 PDF · 88 章 EPUB 3.3 · an adapted rewrite, not a literal translation; the historical pen name 离谱 / Li Pu now appears only on the copyright page and in the prologue. - **Covers** carry the new titles; EPUB metadata now includes a subtitle, UUID identifier, date, subjects, edition 1.1.0 and series information. No ISBN or publisher was invented.  ## Fixed on the way  - EPUBCheck error: the NCX `dtb:uid` now matches the OPF unique identifier. - English PDF rendered Chinese glossary terms as black squares; both editions now embed the OFL Noto subsets and check glyph coverage. - Four AI pages were rechecked against official sources on 2026-11-23 and given current `sources_checked` dates. - The bibliography cites the Hu & Nation (2000) Crossref record instead of a retired ScholarSpace DOI, so the published editions no longer embed a dead link; the retired-URL guard now scans sources only. - The external-link extractor no longer mangles inline-code URLs; the retired-DOI guard judges sources, not build output. - CI audits production dependencies plus a reviewed allowlist with an expiry date; the orphaned unit tests run in `npm run test:unit`.  ## Verification  `npm run check`, `npm run book:validate`, `npm run docs:build` and the Playwright suite pass in CI on `main`, and the scheduled external-link check passes on the tagged commit. The download pages publish SHA-256 values for every file; the manifests and a `SHA256SUMS` file are attached below.  ## Editions and checksums (this release)  | File | SHA-256 | | --- | --- | | `life-level-up-guide-zh.epub` | `cd8f6c69e0f6e7a82d5957e160f91b0f5d62c2bb213d7f839c2988583fc4ee16` | | `life-level-up-guide-en.epub` | `ecbe61cc38cecbb77dbe4647197b5b324f3f35bdb6f28031d32edb0fded14926` | | `life-level-up-guide-zh.pdf` | `62dfc6ed3b834fdbc53343c36b1347d488abc065e09af6f78cdda6a12fd5dc87` | | `life-level-up-guide-en.pdf` | `4fc4916475495e5481d9568ab3bbf30cc65798920ce63b3bbbfa7071dada62db` | | `epub-manifest.json` | `c0bd7118b960e2de05920512feba5e7a6d8f7c36c928b1e8f806d34408a97123` | | `pdf-manifest.json` | `a4f6e6a8d792891525e2b55252771297c4b6615c88c99cf13ea881d387e74e29` |  ## Known limits  - The PDF is print-aware (6 × 9.6 in, embedded fonts) but untagged and RGB; the EPUB is the accessible edition. - The recovery chapters keep their non-clinical boundaries and have not received a qualified clinical review. - No sales, ranking or bestseller claim is made or implied.  ## Assets  - `life-level-up-guide-zh.epub`, `life-level-up-guide-en.epub` - `life-level-up-guide-zh.pdf`, `life-level-up-guide-en.pdf` - `epub-manifest.json`, `pdf-manifest.json`
```

### 2026-11-19T04:00:29Z [**acmesh-official/acme.sh**](https://github.com/acmesh-official/acme.sh)

```
1. Security: values sent by the CA can no longer run shell commands. A single quote in a URL from the CA (finalize, order, cert or account URL) broke out of the quoting in the saved conf file, and a challenge token or URL holding `#` could make acme.sh source a local file as a DNS hook. Challenge tokens and URLs from the CA are now validated. Exploiting either needs a malicious CA or a broken TLS connection to the CA. Reported by Martin Arlitt and Srini Seethapathy (Corelight). 2. `--insecure` now applies to the current run only and is no longer saved to account.conf. A value saved by an older version is ignored and removed; see the wiki [Note on --insecure](https://github.com/acmesh-official/acme.sh/wiki/Options-and-Params). 3. Signed requests are retried on transport errors, and wget2 is supported. 4. `--install-cert` can now update the pre/post/renew hooks ([#7346](https://github.com/acmesh-official/acme.sh/pull/7346)). 5. Conf files: a write that changes nothing is skipped, and the file is kept intact when a write fails ([#7278](https://github.com/acmesh-official/acme.sh/pull/7278)). The email given on the command line no longer overwrites the one in account.conf. 6. New DNS providers: bergdns.at, MVMNET, Public Internet (dns_pblin), Abion, PrimeDNS, ConnByte, hosttech, Domain Chief, NameOcean, MizbanCloud, Timeweb (dns_timeweb_host). Cloudflare supports IDN domains, and Simply.com uses Bearer auth. 7. New deploy hooks: Nginx Proxy Manager / NPMplus, Redfish BMCs, HP printers (hp_ews), pfSense (pfREST), Proxmox Mail Gateway. deploy/ssh supports pfx and stops at the first failed step; synology_dsm can generate 2FA codes from SYNO_TOTP_SECRET. 8. New notify hook: Healthchecks.io. 9. Docker: set-default, update-account-key, list-profiles and make-dns-persist-value are exposed as commands. 10. A lot of bug fixes. 
```
